### rqls10-guard-qualification-v7-codex-v1.prompt.stdout.txt
Path: /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/institute/runs/six-lane-2026-10-18-96h/review/rqls10-guard-qualification-v7-codex-v1.prompt.stdout.txt

**HOLD, conditional on independently accepted supervisor v12 source.** The package still has oracle, confinement, lifetime, and interface blockers.

This review used only the supplied text. No tools, imports, execution, edits, delegation, or launches occurred. Supplied SHA256 values were treated as claimed pins, not independently recomputed hashes. Nothing here authorizes runtime.

The seven prior findings resolve as follows:

| Prior finding | Status | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Artifact integrity and run binding | **PARTIAL** | External approval SHA, actual artifact hashes, exact config reconstruction, cross-record digests, and staged-link checks are substantial repairs. Early negative returns and incomplete cross-record validation remain. |
| 2. Exact outcomes, reasons, types | **PARTIAL** | Receipt failure/trip sets, child rc, loop state, admission reasons, and actual exit are substantially exact. Missing clock evidence, substring ACK causes, and contradictory summary fields remain admissible. |
| 3. Complete samples and intended behavior | **PARTIAL** | Launched controls require nonempty, fully decoded samples. History completeness, intended descendant identities, and PID-specific resource attribution remain insufficiently proved. |
| 4. Artifact confinement | **PARTIAL** | Fixed paths, fresh scenario creation, no-follow traversal, exclusive creation, and one-link/no-create mutations repair the original path APIs. Creation-to-mutation inode continuity is missing. |
| 5. Child lifetime and wall claims | **PARTIAL** | GO waiting and flood writes are cooperative; exec uses remaining lifetime; descendant exceptions retain failure. Blocking `waitpid(..., 0)` calls remain outside the cooperative deadline. |
| 6. Parent allocation/release transaction | **FIXED — contract only** | The five-part independent transaction is explicit. Busy2 reserves two slots at threshold 1, with global cap 2. Actual parent implementation and fresh late-resource evidence remain prerequisites. |
| 7. Checker version identity | **FIXED** | `guard_qual_check_v8` is correct and the external approval pins the actual checker bytes. |

Eight **MAJOR** package/interface findings remain.

1. **MAJOR — Admission-negative controls bypass common final evidence checks.**

   **Location:** `guard_qual_check_v8.verify()`, the `if not launched: ... return result` branch.

   All five admission negatives return before final-census validation, known-owned registry validation, maxima reconciliation, TMPDIR inspection, and publication/reservation arithmetic.

   **Counterexample:** An otherwise qualifying `helper_overrun` bundle can omit `final_census`, `owned_identities`, `maxima`, and `min_free`. After updating the ordinary receipt/settlement/summary digest links, those omissions need not fail checking. The mandatory helper proof also does not require an integer helper `rc`, despite requiring a known wait status.

   **Minimal repair:** Put applicable census, registry, wait-status, manifest, and publication-budget validation before this return. Preserve intentionally absent child samples and launch artifacts. Capture sufficient publication/resource evidence for ACK-refused outcomes without inventing a clock or successful ACK.

   For launched controls, excluding documented late artifacts from reconstruction of the *historical* ACK charge is reasonable. Release additionally needs fresh accounting after those late artifacts; their hashes alone are insufficient.

2. **MAJOR — Mandatory clock evidence can be omitted while age replay passes.**

   **Location:** `integrated_v12_evidence()`, especially:

   ```python
   for commit in (c for c in commits if c is not None):
   ```

   **Counterexample:** Remove every helper’s `launch_commit` and `admission.checked` from an otherwise valid positive bundle. `helper_evidence()` does not require them, and the replay silently skips them. The remaining launch/GO/ACK commits can still pass.

   Additional gaps are material:

   - Admission/start/end date fields are not joined to the corresponding successful captured helper.
   - Canonical stability spelling and the source’s explicit nonnegative date-floor difference are not reproduced exactly.
   - The checker does not independently enforce the spawn-to-GO upper-age bound.
   - Intended ACK refusal uses substring membership in `summary.error`, rather than an exact cause.

   **Minimal repair:** Require every applicable commit, its exact phase, and its clock provenance. Permit the no-prior-clock exception only for the first clock-establishing helper. Join date fields to helper purpose/PID/capture; reproduce canonical/future/age checks; enforce GO age; match the precise refusal cause.

3. **MAJOR — History-prefix and contradictory-record checks remain incomplete.**

   **Locations:** `integrated_v12_evidence()` and `verify()`.

   The census prefix is compared exactly, but:

   - Summary `coverage_history` is never checked.
   - Summary unproved history is checked only for inclusion of unique IDs.
   - Postpublication census-history additions are not subjected to the receipt-history validation.
   - Samples, helper proofs, admission censuses, clearance, and final census are not required to join their corresponding retained observation records.
   - Several duplicated summary fields are ignored.

   **Counterexamples:** A summary can omit `coverage_history`, alter retained unproved identity fields while preserving their unique IDs, or contradict the receipt through `failure_codes` or `child_returncode`, without necessarily failing. A retained observation can also be removed and maxima lowered without an obligatory join to the unchanged sample stream.

   **Minimal repair:** Validate all history additions and embedded census joins. Require exact coverage-prefix preservation. Preserve immutable first-observation evidence and validate monotonic last-observation extensions against retained observations. Reconcile every duplicated outcome/blocker field. Reject truncation and contradictions.

4. **MAJOR — Intended ownership identities and resource causes remain under-proved.**

   **Locations:** `intended_descendant_is_only_blocker`, `reported_zombie_identity_observed`, `no_unreported_owned_identity`, and `rss_intended_crossing`.

   **Counterexample:** `ownership_slow` can qualify with its reported PID classified only as unproved, although its retained v6 definition requires observed current-parent ownership. The same generic derivation also permits that weakening for `ownership_group`.

   Moreover, `no_unreported_owned_identity` is only an upper-bound check: deleting extra owned-registry entries can make it vacuously true. Zombie and busy proofs largely join bare PID lists, rather than proving the reported incarnation and retaining it. RSS checks an aggregate lane crossing, without proving the intended child’s measured contribution.

   **Minimal repair:** Join each reported PID to its full observed identity, relevant ownership edge/root, and retained history. Require known ownership for group/slow; allow the expressly defined known-or-unproved alternatives only for fast/double. Require all observed reported identities to remain retained. Supply PID-specific RSS evidence sufficient to distinguish the intended allocation from unrelated lane growth.

5. **MAJOR — A one-link regular file is not proof of the freshly created fixture.**

   **Locations:** writer `write_new()`/`main()`, child `append_fixture()`, checker mutation checks.

   **Counterexample:** After preparation, replace `RUN/pinned-scratch.txt` by renaming an older, single-link file containing exactly `FIXTURE` into that name. The child accepts its bytes/size/link count and appends `x`. The checker compares the child’s reported inode only with the *current* mutated file, so this can qualify while modifying an older inode.

   No symlink or hard link is needed. For `mutate_evidence`, eventual supervisor detection of a replacement would still occur after the prohibited mutation.

   **Minimal repair:** Bind the original creation identities and run anchors into approved preparation evidence, convey the expected identity to the child, and verify the opened descriptor before writing. Bind the launch mutation to its original published inode too. Post-mutation inode agreement is insufficient.

6. **MAJOR — Explicit blocking joins exceed the cooperative child lifetime.**

   **Locations:** child `main()` busy2 and intermediate joins, and `intermediate()`’s zombie reap:

   ```python
   os.waitpid(..., 0)
   ```

   **Counterexample:** A reported descendant or intermediate remains stopped or fails to progress after the PID report. Its parent can remain inside blocking `waitpid` beyond `START_MONO + life`.

   **Minimal repair:** Poll with `WNOHANG` within the existing absolute cooperative deadline, preserve failure and identity evidence, and leave allocations held if closure is unproved. Do not add tool timeouts, unbounded parent waiting, or escalation.

7. **MAJOR — Busy2 lacks the required explicit negative authorization check.**

   **Location:** checker `verify()`.

   Ownership controls and `zombie_hold` require `approval.ownership_negative is True`; busy2 does not.

   **Counterexample:** Busy2 can PASS with a trusted approval omitting that field or setting it false, despite the plan requiring every forking workload’s individual negative authorization.

   **Minimal repair:** Apply the authorization predicate centrally to every `NEGATIVE_PROCESS` member and bind the negative designation into its individual GO as required by v7. Decode and validate `preparation.json` semantically as well; hashing it does not verify its control declarations.

   The existing two-slot reservation and threshold-1/global-cap-2 arithmetic must remain.

8. **MAJOR — The permitted unproved `ownership_double` outcome conflicts with v12’s exact interface.**

   **Locations:** checker `expectations()` versus supervisor `final_body()`.

   When the intended grandchild is **only unproved**, streams are closed, and other cheap-clear predicates hold, this v12 condition applies:

   ```python
   if s.clear is None and not c["owned_live"] and s.cheap_clear():
       s.fail("TIMELY_CLEARANCE_NOT_ESTABLISHED")
   ```

   It does not exclude `unproved_live`. Consequently, the permitted double-fork branch produces an additional failure which the checker correctly rejects.

   **Minimal repair:** Resolve this interface mismatch through the separate supervisor review and a newly pinned successor. The all-clear condition must account for unproved blockers. Do not broaden the package’s expected failure set to excuse this unrelated failure.

   This finding concerns compatibility with the supplied interface; it is not acceptance of the supervisor implementation.

The nine v11-fix interfaces and three disclosed integration blockers have this disposition:

The exact retained 22-control contract is below. These are required outcomes, **not executed results**. All main controls require acknowledged publication and matching actual exit; no additional failure is excused.

Here, `G` means `GRACE_EXPIRED_AFTER_CHILD_EXIT`, and `S` means `STREAMS_OR_THREADS_NOT_CLOSED`.

| # | Control | Required receipt / actual exit and isolated cause |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `date_fault` | NOT_LAUNCHED / 14; exactly `HELPER_FAULT_INJECTED`; no helper PID. |

| 22 | `identity` | Same, plus exact injected `IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED`; TERM explicitly unsent because of injection. |

All four helper negatives additionally require exactly `DATE_HELPER_UNAVAILABLE`, null clock observation, and ACK_REFUSED/actual 14 caused precisely by `STABILITY_AGE_UNVERIFIABLE`. `go_unlisted` requires its precise missing-config cause. Arbitrary ACK refusal does not qualify either family. There is no busy3.

Before any release or subsequent GO, the independent parent must complete all five parts:

1. **Allocation and approval:** Serialize the current 20,971,520-byte reservation and CPU allocation; busy2 takes both slots. Retain the approval SHA externally, binding exact sources/interpreter/config/GO/stability/preparation and current outstanding totals. Require the applicable explicit negative authorization.
2. **Execution evidence:** Retain actual exit separately and capture exactly one summary outside `out/`. Independently verify both publications, digests, byte counts, and original-inode staged-link evidence. Printed ACK with mismatching actual exit is failure.
3. **Checking:** Require the repaired checker’s exact intended outcome from independently read artifacts. Checker PASS is one control result and grants no release.
4. **Ended identities:** Independently prove every observed known, helper, reported, and unproved identity ended, including historical identities already absent at final census. Preserve unresolved coverage. Leader reap, PID-only absence, and eventual self-exit are insufficient substitutes.
5. **Late audit and release:** Audit all RUN artifacts and earlier frozen bytes; validate only documented late outputs against the original identities and intended behavior. Recompute applicable output/reservation/free/lane accounting after late changes. Write and independently verify a **new exclusive `/bin/date`-stamped disposition** joining all five parts before releasing allocations. Original UNSETTLED records remain immutable.

The clock gates must remain explicit: canonical actual `/bin/date` observations; conservative age

`date_floor − stability_epoch + 1 second + elapsed_since_before_read`;

nonnegative date difference; age ≤300 seconds at every applicable commit; raw GO expiry and epoch/monotonic fit; spawn-to-GO age ≤`min(60, wall/2)`; and complete postpublication ACK census no older than one second for these configs. Completion checks after GO/ACK I/O and the trusted **actual exit** remain mandatory. Parent date, gate, and observer operations also need their independently accepted ownership/artifact envelopes.

Missing empirical obligations are separate from these source defects. **None of the 22 controls has been established as executed or passing here.** Before SCIENCE, the parent must freeze and individually admit additional controls needed by the final source verdict, including:

- Native delayed-return/successful-late-reap and real helper failure/closure cases.
- UID, exec/version, parent-edge, historical-unproved, incomplete-retry, and new-ACK-blocker cases.
- Missing/future/stale clocks, GO-age/expiry boundaries, revocation during I/O, and commit cancellation—including printed ACK followed by actual 14.
- OUTPUT/reservation/FREE/LANE boundaries, late-resource changes, inode/anchor replacement, capture caps, and write/fsync/close/readback failures.

The finite matrix explicitly does not cover all those behaviors. Their runtime evidence is **not required in this source pass**, but package acceptance cannot establish entire-science qualification. Publish repairs as new pinned versions, preserve mandatory date and complete ownership coverage, and retain timeout0, bounded cooperative polling, independent parent disposition, and no SIGKILL or automatic advancement.

## 2. Preparation, exact individual authority, and creation identity

Preparation itself needs the parent's individually authorized owned envelope; the writer is not permission to invoke it. The writer spawns nothing, imports no package source, writes no GO/stability/approval, and invents no clock/resource observations. It accepts only fixed scenario metadata and the actual realpath `.../Resources/Python.app/Contents/MacOS/Python`, not the framework launcher.

Before creation it checks fixed paths and actual four-source/interpreter digests. A fresh run/out/tmp is anchored using no-follow descriptors. For `mutate_pin`, the original inode, size, one-link count and bytes are captured with fstat/readback on the **CREATED exclusive descriptor before close**. Preparation is written before config; it contains no config or GO digest, avoiding circularity.

Preparation has exactly:
`kind, scenario, run, output_root, fixture, source_pins, python, allocation, negative_control`.
Kind is `qualification-creation-preparation-v1`; run/out are `{path,inode:[dev,ino]}`; fixture is null except the original pin `{path,inode,sha256,bytes,nlink:1}`. Four source pins are exactly `{path,sha256}` at the listed fixed paths. Allocation is `{bytes,cpu_slots,outstanding_reservations_bytes,lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes}`. Negative control is true except `ok`/`rc_nonzero`. The checker independently hashes the actual preparation/config/GO/stability/source/interpreter bytes and semantically reconstructs the complete preparation/config.

Parent approval, trusted by an external SHA passed to the checker, has kind `v15-qualification-invocation-v1`; it pins scenario, config/GO/stability/preparation hashes, all four actual sources, interpreter and current allocation. Explicit flags are:
- Every `busy2`, `ownership_*`, `zombie_hold`: `ownership_negative=true`, plus GO `qualification_negative={scenario,kind:"ownership",creation_channel:false}`.
- The four helper injections: `helper_injection=true`, GO kind `helper_injection`, false creation channel.
- Both mutations: `mutation_negative=true`, GO kind `mutation`, literal boolean true creation channel.
- Other controls omit the negative GO object; their exact config SHA/purpose/scenario still needs its own individual authority.

Each GO binds exactly one config digest and the v15 BOUND_KEYS, including `creation_channel`; only deliberate `go_unlisted` has an empty config map under its own negative authority. Busy2 reserves **both CPU slots**, threshold1, unchanged global ceiling2. No busy3 exists.

Ordinary/science/certificate consumers keep exact bytes `GO\n` then EOF, with `creation_channel={}`. Only the two declared mutations receive the one fixed canonical compact JSON line, at most4096 bytes, then EOF. Exact schema, source binding and terminal/accounting contract are frozen in `review/rqls10-v15-qualification-interface-v1.md`, SHA `bc18425509159d362b4392e88eb54c4d8fa6fb165cd4a635bddc619e5d5ab290`. No command/target selection is added. The child verifies approved run/out anchors and original target descriptor inode/hash/size/one-link **before append**, then verifies the same descriptor/name again. An older renamed same-content inode is refused before mutation. The launch target uses its original supervisor publication inode/digest. The checker joins exact received-line digest, preparation facts, original and after facts, and before/append/fsync/after monotonic ordering.

## 3. Retained original22 controls — exact required outcomes, NOT results

`G` = `GRACE_EXPIRED_AFTER_CHILD_EXIT`; `S` = `STREAMS_OR_THREADS_NOT_CLOSED`. An ownership blocker code is derived only from the single full intended incarnation's actual final classification; no unrelated failure is excused.

|---|---|---|

| ignore | UNSETTLED / 11 | rc null; grace-stop; DEADLINE only; exactly GRACE_EXPIRED_AFTER_STOP_REQUEST+CHILD_NOT_REAPED+OWNED_LIVE+S; TERM actually sent and ignored |
| identity | UNSETTLED / 11 | rc null; grace-stop; same plus IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED; explicitly unsent injected TERM, no escalation |

All main controls require one complete captured stdout summary, complete separately captured supervisor stderr and separately captured actual exit. Helper/admission negatives require their exact ACK_REFUSED interface, not a successful ACK. All launched controls require nonempty complete decoded samples, with exact byte/count reconciliation; an immediate exit cannot qualify through an empty stream. A printed positive ACK followed by actual14, incomplete capture, or any unexpected failure/trip is FAIL/HOLD, not accepted under an expanded code set.

Lifetimes/walls/thresholds remain the retained control definitions: common child life60s, ownership40s, zombie10s, deadline120s; supervisor wall60s except deadline/ignore/identity10s. Cooperative child joins use WNOHANG with absolute earlier deadlines inside the module-entry lifetime and reserved evidence time; unresolved joins record identity/error and fail, never finally-exit0. Sleep exec uses only remaining life. Default helper call/grace5s/5s; overrun call0.01s; group/EOF call0.2s/grace0.5s. Actual measured crossings, not requested flags/delays, determine acceptance.

## 4. Common oracle — including every admission-negative return

The checker has no early admission-negative bypass of common final evidence:
- Complete final census, full main/helper root registry, integer helper wait status, actual source/config/input/approval binding, retained all-UID/mixed-UID history, resource extrema, free minimum, empty test TMP and per-root inventory are mandatory for all22.
- For NOT-LAUNCHED **main only**, it requires the explicit five-path all-null artifact map, empty main expected-digest/stream maps, zero typed sample counters and explicit null main identity/rc/exit/stop/signal fields. It does NOT erase or demand empty observed helper/date/ownership histories.
- Every applicable HELPER/ADMISSION/LAUNCH/GO/ACK commit is required with exact phase/provenance. Canonical/future/nonnegative date-floor checks, actual captured helper purpose/PID/date joins, first-helper-only no-prior-clock exception, nonrenewing monotonic expiry replay, actual post-write/close GO upper age and remaining main runtime are checked. Refusal machine causes are exact, not substrings.
- Receipt/current history prefixes, immutable first observations, monotonic last extensions and variants/counts are checked. Current reconciliation has the complete typed schema. Every sample/helper proof/admission/launch/pre-GO/clear/final/ACK census joins a unique retained observation. All post-publication additions and individual resource measurements are checked; aborted attempts remain explicitly aborted, not completed. Duplicate summary state/rc/failure/blocker/history fields cannot contradict or disappear.
- Intended full incarnations, known ownership roots/edges and lower-bound registry retention are required, not just bare PIDs/upper bounds. Group/slow cannot qualify as unproved; fast/double permit only their defined known-or-unproved alternative. Reported identities are retained for the separate parent ended-identity audit. Zombie, RSS and busy proofs are PID/incarnation-specific. Busy predicates/counters remain evidence from this reviewed implementation, not an independent kernel implementation or proof of numerical backend threading.
- Actual receipt/settlement bytes/digests, original same-inode two-link staged publication, exact distinct-inode charged payload, reservation/output/FREE/LANE arithmetic and actual b/end are mandatory on positive ACK **and ACK_REFUSED**. Precheck a or fresh values cannot substitute. Late artifacts are labelled/excluded only from historical charge reconstruction, never from the later fresh parent audit. No in-flight peak claim is made.

Numeric misses, source review and recovery controls are not nonexistence proofs or maximal-basin evidence. These sources/checker are one implementation with cross-record checks, not an independent runtime verifier or Lean proof.

## 5. Parent-only five-part release transaction

No checker PASS, receipt status, JSON ACK, child rc, supervisor exit or elapsed lifetime alone releases anything. For each exact individual invocation the parent must join all five parts:

1. **Authority/allocation:** serialize current exact item allocation and all other outstanding reservations; fresh stability/gate; exact approved source/config/preparation/GO pins and explicit negative scope. No next invocation without its own current authority.
2. **Execution/publication:** separately capture actual exit and complete stdout AND stderr; verify actual invocation identity, both original publication digests/inodes/link counts/readbacks, post-publish monotonic bound and full successful parent publication acknowledgement. A positive printed ACK with actual14 is rejected.
3. **Oracle:** run only the separately admitted fixed checker with the external trusted approval digest and actual exit; inspect all criteria and exact cause; reject missing/contradictory evidence.
4. **Ended identities:** independently establish endedness for every known main/helper/descendant, reported incarnation and historical unproved identity; retain unresolved coverage. No PID-only or 'missing means gone' shortcut. Wait any authorized surviving workload through its declared life; no escalation, deletion or automatic release.
5. **Late/resources/exclusive commit:** after endedness, independently audit the **entire RUN**, including captures, check output and every late artifact, with fresh bounded descriptor-anchored resource accounting; write a NEW exclusive actual-owned-`/bin/date`-stamped parent disposition joining authority, actual exit, capture/artifact digests, oracle, ended-identity and late-resource evidence. Only successful completion/acknowledgement of that parent transaction releases the allocation. Original UNSETTLED/NOT_LAUNCHED publications stay immutable and are not relabelled.

The parent owns this transaction implementation and every admission/gate/release; this package supplies no competing scheduler or release ledger.

## 6. Separate FULL finite matrix, not science qualification from22

Every obligation in v13 source review `7b042421e9fb410cf3fec99d9953dd69c4a6c5736628a3186b18de3f50db2ddb` lines108–132 and v14 review `1866052f63d8b8c143ece0cee646b277e2f87cbba2f5c58ae4edf5d111fca413` lines30–34 remains required and UNEXERCISED. This base package provides the original22 implementations and oracle, not the additional isolated fault/state fixtures. The full matrix requires an exact separate source/plan/oracle mapping and separately reviewed bounded fixtures where needed, measured actual outcomes/cutoffs, complete actual capture/exit/artifact identities and honest distinction between state-model coverage and native/OS integration. It includes persistent post-ACK stderr failures through shutdown and all four accounting stages with honest nulls. No host-clock mutation or synthetic value presented as actual `/bin/date` output is permitted.

Even successful source review and all22 base controls would not alone qualify the unrestricted fixed-B assay for science. The assay/checker/backend-thread proof remains held, unchanged, until the full required guard qualification and the parent's next separate decisions.

196:     raw = pidinfo(pid, 17, 56, True)
197:     return struct.unpack_from("=Q", raw, 16)[0], struct.unpack_from("=i", raw, 32)[0]

201:     raw = pidinfo(pid, 13, 64, True)
202:     p, pp, pg, st = struct.unpack_from("=IIII", raw)
203:     flags, uid = struct.unpack_from("=II", raw, 32)
204:     return {"pid": p, "ppid": pp, "pgid": pg, "status": st, "euid": uid,
205:             "ruid": struct.unpack_from("=I", raw, 44)[0], "traced": bool(flags & 2),

213: def identify(pid):
214:     a, s1, b, s2, c = unique(pid), short(pid), unique(pid), short(pid), unique(pid)
215:     if not (a == b == c):
216:         raise GuardError("IDENTITY_CHANGED %d" % pid)
217:     for s in (s1, s2):
218:         s.update(uniqueid=a[0], idversion=a[1])
219:     if fingerprint(s1) != fingerprint(s2):
220:         raise GuardError("IDENTITY_METADATA_CHANGED %d" % pid)
221:     return s2

228:     end = field.find(b"\0")

234: def argv_of(pid, maximum):
235:     mib = (ctypes.c_int * 3)(1, 49, pid)
236:     buf, size = ctypes.create_string_buffer(maximum), ctypes.c_size_t(maximum)
237:     ctypes.set_errno(0)
238:     if LC.sysctl(mib, 3, buf, ctypes.byref(size), None, 0):
239:         raise GuardError(ctypes.get_errno() or errno.EIO, "ARGV %d" % pid)
240:     raw = ctypes.string_at(buf, size.value)
241:     if len(raw) < 5:
242:         raise GuardError("ARGV_SHORT %d" % pid)
243:     argc = struct.unpack_from("=i", raw)[0]
244:     end = raw.find(b"\0", 4)
245:     if not (0 <= argc <= maximum) or end < 0:
246:         raise GuardError("ARGV_MALFORMED %d" % pid)
247:     out, pos = [raw[4:end].decode("utf-8", "surrogateescape")], end
248:     while pos < len(raw) and raw[pos] == 0:
249:         pos += 1
250:     for _ in range(argc):
251:         end = raw.find(b"\0", pos)
252:         if end < 0:
253:             raise GuardError("ARGV_UNTERMINATED %d" % pid)
254:         out.append(raw[pos:end].decode("utf-8", "surrogateescape"))
255:         pos = end + 1
256:     return out
257: 
258: 
259: def task_of(pid):
260:     raw = pidinfo(pid, 4, 96)
261:     rss = struct.unpack_from("=Q", raw, 8)[0]
262:     u, s = struct.unpack_from("=QQ", raw, 16)
263:     return rss, u + s
264: 
265: 
266: def start_of(pid):
267:     s, us = struct.unpack_from("=QQ", pidinfo(pid, 3, 136, True), 120)
268:     return s + us / 1e6
269: 
270: 
271: def all_pids(dl):
272:     dl.check("before pid list")
273:     count = LP.proc_listallpids(None, 0)
274:     if count <= 0:
275:         raise GuardError("PID_LIST_FAILED")
276:     cap = count * 2 + 1024
277:     for _ in range(4):
278:         buf = (ctypes.c_int * cap)()
279:         n = LP.proc_listallpids(buf, ctypes.sizeof(buf))
280:         dl.check("after pid list")
281:         if n <= 0:
282:             raise GuardError("PID_LIST_FAILED")
283:         if n < cap:
284:             vals = list(buf[:n])
285:             if any(p < 0 for p in vals) or len(set(vals)) != len(vals):
286:                 raise GuardError("PID_LIST_MALFORMED")
287:             return [p for p in vals if p > 0]
288:         cap *= 2
289:     raise GuardError("PID_LIST_OVERFLOW")
290: 
291: 
292: # Bounded regular-file and descriptor-anchored directory operations.
293: def read_file(path, cap, dl, dir_fd=None, keep=False):
294:     dl.check("before file open")
295:     st = os.stat(path, dir_fd=dir_fd, follow_symlinks=False)
296:     if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or not 0 <= st.st_size <= cap:
297:         raise GuardError("NOT_BOUNDED_REGULAR %s" % path)
298:     fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=dir_fd)
299:     parts, h, got = [], hashlib.sha256(), 0
300:     try:
301:         fs = os.fstat(fd)
302:         if (fs.st_dev, fs.st_ino, fs.st_size) != (st.st_dev, st.st_ino, st.st_size):
303:             raise GuardError("FILE_CHANGED_OPEN %s" % path)
304:         while got < st.st_size:
305:             dl.check("file read")
306:             b = os.read(fd, min(1 << 20, st.st_size - got))
307:             if not b:
308:                 raise GuardError("FILE_SHORT %s" % path)
309:             h.update(b)
310:             got += len(b)
311:             if keep:
312:                 parts.append(b)
313:         tail = os.read(fd, 1)
314:         end = os.fstat(fd)
315:         if tail or (end.st_size, end.st_mtime_ns) != (st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns):
316:             raise GuardError("FILE_CHANGED_READ %s" % path)
317:     finally:
318:         os.close(fd)
319:     dl.check("after file close")
320:     return {"sha256": h.hexdigest(), "bytes": got, "inode": [st.st_dev, st.st_ino], "nlink": st.st_nlink,
321:             "birth_epoch": st.st_birthtime}, b"".join(parts) if keep else None
322: 
323: 
324: def write_all(fd, data, dl):
325:     view = memoryview(data)
326:     while view:
327:         dl.check("before write")
328:         n = os.write(fd, view)
329:         if n <= 0:
330:             raise GuardError("ZERO_PROGRESS_WRITE")
331:         view = view[n:]
332:     dl.check("after write")
333: 
334: 
335: def bytes_json(obj):
336:     return (json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, indent=1, allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()
337: 
338: 
339: def pairs(kv):
340:     out = {}
341:     for k, v in kv:
342:         if k in out:
343:             raise ValueError("duplicate JSON key %r" % k)
344:         out[k] = v
345:     return out

348: def json_float(s):
349:     x = float(s)
350:     if not math.isfinite(x):
351:         raise ValueError("nonfinite JSON number")
352:     return x
353: 
354: 
355: def json_constant(s):
356:     raise ValueError("nonfinite JSON constant %s" % s)
357: 
358: 
359: def parse_json(raw):
360:     return json.loads(raw.decode(), object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_float=json_float, parse_constant=json_constant)

363: def parse_date_epoch(text):
364:     if not isinstance(text, str):
365:         raise GuardError("STABILITY_TIMESTAMP_MISSING")
366:     parsed = datetime.datetime.strptime(text, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z")
367:     if parsed.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z") != text:
368:         raise GuardError("STABILITY_TIMESTAMP_NONCANONICAL")
369:     return parsed.timestamp()

371: 
372: def norm_abs(p):
373:     return isinstance(p, str) and p.startswith("/") and os.path.abspath(p) == p
374: 
375: 
376: def below(p, root):
377:     return p == root or p.startswith(root.rstrip("/") + "/")
378: 
379: 
380: def directory_at(fd, rel, dl):
381:     """new directory open descriptions, no symlink components, relative to the retained anchor."""
382:     dl.check("before directory traversal")
383:     cur = os.open(".", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
384:     try:
385:         for component in rel.split("/"):
386:             if component in ("", "."):
387:                 continue
388:             if component == "..":
389:                 raise GuardError("PARENT_DIRECTORY_REFUSED")
390:             nxt = os.open(component, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=cur)
391:             os.close(cur)
392:             cur = nxt
393:             dl.check("directory traversal")
394:         dl.check("after directory traversal")
395:     except BaseException:
396:         os.close(cur)
397:         raise
398:     return cur
399: 
400: 
401: def absolute_directory(path, dl):
402:     root = os.open("/", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
403:     try:
404:         fd = directory_at(root, path.lstrip("/"), dl)
405:     finally:
406:         os.close(root)
407:     dl.check("after absolute directory open")
408:     return fd

410: 
411: def stat_id(fd):
412:     s = os.fstat(fd)
413:     return s.st_dev, s.st_ino
414: 
415: 
416: def list_directory(fd, dl):
417:     names = []
418:     with os.scandir(fd) as it:
419:         for e in it:
420:             if len(names) >= ROOT_ENTRIES:
421:                 raise GuardError("ROOT_ENTRY_CAP")
422:             names.append(e.name)
423:             dl.check("directory inventory")
424:     names.sort()
425:     dl.check("after directory inventory sort")
426:     return names
427: 
428: 
429: def walk_fd(anchor, dev, dl, seen=None):
430:     """distinct-inode logical bytes; disappeared/retyped entries are incomplete accounting, never zero."""
431:     seen = set() if seen is None else seen
432:     count = [0]
433: 
434:     def descend(fd, depth):
435:         if depth > 64:
436:             raise GuardError("WALK_DEPTH_CAP")
437:         total, dirs = 0, []
438:         with os.scandir(fd) as it:
439:             for e in it:
440:                 dl.check("walk entry")
441:                 count[0] += 1
442:                 if count[0] > WALK_ENTRIES:
443:                     raise GuardError("WALK_ENTRY_CAP")
444:                 s = os.stat(e.name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
445:                 if s.st_dev != dev:
446:                     raise GuardError("WALK_MOUNT_BOUNDARY %s" % e.name)
447:                 if stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode):
448:                     dirs.append((e.name, s.st_ino))
449:                 elif stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode):
450:                     key = (s.st_dev, s.st_ino)
451:                     if key not in seen:
452:                         seen.add(key)
453:                         total += s.st_size
454:         for name, ino in dirs:
455:             child = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
456:             try:
457:                 if stat_id(child) != (dev, ino):
458:                     raise GuardError("WALK_DIRECTORY_REPLACED %s" % name)
459:                 total += descend(child, depth + 1)
460:             finally:
461:                 os.close(child)
462:             dl.check("after walk child close")
463:         dl.check("after walk directory")
464:         return total
465:     fd = os.open(".", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=anchor)
466:     try:
467:         total = descend(fd, 0)
468:     finally:
469:         os.close(fd)
470:     dl.check("after walk root close")
471:     return total
472: 
473: 
474: def create_file(fd, name):
475:     out = os.open(name, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o644, dir_fd=fd)
476:     return out, stat_id(out)
477: 
478: 
479: def publish(fd, name, data, dl):
480:     out, ident = create_file(fd, name)
481:     try:
482:         write_all(out, data, dl)
483:         os.fsync(out)
484:         dl.check("after publication fsync")
485:     finally:
486:         os.close(out)
487:     dl.check("after publication close")
488:     a, _ = read_file(name, len(data), dl, fd)
489:     if a["sha256"] != hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() or tuple(a["inode"]) != ident:
490:         raise GuardError("PUBLICATION_READBACK_MISMATCH")
491:     return a["sha256"], ident
492: 
493: 
494: def linked_publication(fd, name, data, dl):
495:     h, published_inode = publish(fd, name + ".staged", data, dl)
496:     os.link(name + ".staged", name, src_dir_fd=fd, dst_dir_fd=fd)
497:     dl.check("after settlement link")
498:     a, _ = read_file(name, len(data), dl, fd)
499:     s = os.stat(name + ".staged", dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
500:     if a["sha256"] != h or tuple(a["inode"]) != published_inode or (s.st_dev, s.st_ino) != published_inode or a["nlink"] != 2:
501:         raise GuardError("SETTLEMENT_LINK_MISMATCH")
502:     dl.check("after settlement link readback")
503:     return h

505: 
506: def number(x):
507:     return isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool) and math.isfinite(x)
508: 
509: 
510: def integer(x):
511:     return isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool)
512: 
513: 
514: def hex64(x):
515:     return isinstance(x, str) and len(x) == 64 and all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in x)
516: 
517: 
518: def validate(cfg):
519:     def schema(obj, spec):
520:         if not isinstance(obj, dict) or set(obj) != set(spec):
521:             raise ValueError("missing/extra config keys")
522:         for k, t in spec.items():
523:             ok = number(obj[k]) if t is float else integer(obj[k]) if t is int else isinstance(obj[k], t)
524:             if not ok:
525:                 raise ValueError("invalid config type: " + k)
526:     schema(cfg, CONFIG)
527:     schema(cfg["admission"], ADMISSION)
528:     schema(cfg["helper_budget"], HELPER)
529:     hb = cfg["helper_budget"]
530:     if not (1 <= hb["max_calls"] <= 32 and 0.001 <= hb["call_budget_s"] <= 10 and 0.01 <= hb["term_grace_s"] <= 10):
531:         raise ValueError("helper budget policy")
532:     if not 0.01 <= cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"] <= 300:
533:         raise ValueError("stability freshness policy")
534:     limits = {"max_chunk_seconds": (1, POLICY["chunk"]), "max_wall_s": (0.01, cfg["max_chunk_seconds"]),
535:               "settle_s": (0.01, 600), "sample_s": (0.2, 10), "lane_walk_s": (1, 600),
536:               "owned_compute_settled_by_epoch": (1, 4e9), "rss_trip_bytes": (1, POLICY["rss"]),
537:               "rss_projection_bytes": (0, cfg["rss_trip_bytes"]), "free_trip_bytes": (POLICY["free"], 1 << 60),
538:               "launch_free_min_bytes": (POLICY["launch_free"], 1 << 60),
539:               "output_trip_bytes": (1, POLICY["disk"]), "lane_disk_ceiling_bytes": (1, POLICY["disk"]),
540:               "cpu_slots": (1, 2), "busy_limit": (0 if cfg["purpose"] == "guard-qualification" else 1, 2),
541:               "busy_cpu_fraction": (0.001, 1), "stdout_cap_bytes": (1, 1 << 30),
542:               "stderr_cap_bytes": (1, 1 << 30), "samples_cap_bytes": (1, 1 << 30)}
543:     for k, (lo, hi) in limits.items():
544:         if not lo <= cfg[k] <= hi:
545:             raise ValueError("policy range: " + k)
546:     a = cfg["admission"]
547:     for k in ("reservation_bytes", "outstanding_reservations_bytes", "lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"):
548:         if not 0 <= a[k] <= 1 << 60:
549:             raise ValueError("negative/large reservation: " + k)
550:     if a["reservation_bytes"] > POLICY["disk"]:
551:         raise ValueError("reservation over lane cap")
552:     cap = cfg["output_trip_bytes"] + cfg["stdout_cap_bytes"] + cfg["stderr_cap_bytes"] + cfg["samples_cap_bytes"] + PUBLICATION_CAP
553:     if a["reservation_bytes"] < cap:
554:         raise ValueError("reservation below caps + publication allowance")
555:     if set(cfg["fault_injection"]) - ({"identity_check_fails"} | HELPER_FAULTS) or any(not isinstance(x, bool) for x in cfg["fault_injection"].values()):
556:         raise ValueError("invalid fault injection")
557:     if any(cfg["fault_injection"].values()) and cfg["purpose"] != "guard-qualification":
558:         raise ValueError("fault outside qualification")
559:     if not cfg["argv"] or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in cfg["argv"]) or not norm_abs(cfg["argv"][0]):
560:         raise ValueError("argv invalid")
561:     if not all(isinstance(k, str) and isinstance(v, str) for k, v in cfg["env"].items()) or not norm_abs(cfg["env"].get("TMPDIR")):
562:         raise ValueError("env/TMPDIR invalid")
563:     for k in ("lane", "lane_disk_root"):
564:         if not norm_abs(cfg[k]):
565:             raise ValueError("not normalized absolute: " + k)
566:     for k in ("lane_roots", "output_roots"):
567:         if not cfg[k] or not all(norm_abs(p) for p in cfg[k]) or len(set(cfg[k])) != len(cfg[k]):
568:             raise ValueError("invalid roots: " + k)
569:     if any(r == cfg["lane_disk_root"] or not below(r, cfg["lane_disk_root"]) for r in cfg["output_roots"]):
570:         raise ValueError("output root outside lane-disk root")
571:     if any(r != q and below(r, q) for r in cfg["output_roots"] for q in cfg["output_roots"]):
572:         raise ValueError("nested output roots")
573:     if not below(cfg["env"]["TMPDIR"], cfg["lane_disk_root"]) or any(below(cfg["env"]["TMPDIR"], r) for r in cfg["output_roots"]):
574:         raise ValueError("TMPDIR outside charge or inside output root")
575:     for o in cfg["outputs"]:
576:         if not isinstance(o, dict) or set(o) != {"path", "required"} or not norm_abs(o["path"]) or type(o["required"]) is not bool:
577:             raise ValueError("invalid output")
578:     for p in cfg["pins"]:
579:         if not isinstance(p, dict) or set(p) != {"path", "sha256"} or not norm_abs(p["path"]) or not hex64(p["sha256"]):
580:             raise ValueError("invalid pin")
581:     paths = [cfg[k] for k in ("stdout", "stderr", "samples", "launch_record", "receipt", "settlement_record")] + [o["path"] for o in cfg["outputs"]]
582:     if len(paths) != len(set(paths)) or any(not norm_abs(p) or os.path.dirname(p) not in cfg["output_roots"] or p.endswith(".staged") for p in paths):
583:         raise ValueError("invalid/distinct output paths")
584:     for p in (a["go_path"], a["stability_receipt_path"]):
585:         if not norm_abs(p) or any(below(p, r) for r in cfg["output_roots"]):
586:             raise ValueError("invalid authorization path")
587:     channel = cfg["creation_channel"]
588:     if channel:
589:         keys = {"kind", "scenario", "preparation_path", "preparation_sha256", "target_path"}
590:         if cfg["purpose"] != "guard-qualification" or set(channel) != keys or \
591:                 channel["kind"] != "qualification-creation-v1" or channel["scenario"] not in ("mutate_pin", "mutate_evidence") or \
592:                 len(cfg["output_roots"]) != 1 or not hex64(channel["preparation_sha256"]):
593:             raise ValueError("creation channel is restricted to declared mutation qualification negatives")
594:         run = os.path.dirname(cfg["output_roots"][0])
595:         expected_target = run + "/pinned-scratch.txt" if channel["scenario"] == "mutate_pin" else cfg["launch_record"]
596:         if channel["target_path"] != expected_target or channel["preparation_path"] != run + "/preparation.json":
597:             raise ValueError("creation channel target/preparation is not the fixed current-run fixture")
598: 
599: 
600: def spawn_registered(s, argv, env, role, dl, required_s):
601:     """Spawn with an explicitly empty signal mask while cancellation is protected in the parent.
602:     Registration is the FIRST operation after posix_spawn returns a PID; no Popen finalizer or second reaper exists.
603:     Returned pipe descriptors remain owned even when post-spawn close/deadline work fails."""
604:     allocated = []
605:     record = {"pid": None, "role": role, "identity": None, "leader_reaped": False, "rc": None,
606:               "wait_lost": False, "term_attempted": False, "settled": False, "stdout_fd": None,
607:               "stderr_fd": None, "stdin_fd": None, "spawn_error": None, "parent_close_fds": []}
608:     s.keep.append(record)  # Retain the allocation/pipe ledger even before a PID exists.
609:     if role == "helper":
610:         s.helpers.append(record)
611:     try:
612:         out_read, out_write = os.pipe()
613:         allocated.extend((out_read, out_write))
614:         if role == "helper":
615:             null = os.open("/dev/null", os.O_RDWR)
616:             allocated.append(null)
617:             in_read, err_write = null, null
618:             close_parent = {null, out_write}
619:         else:
620:             in_read, in_write = os.pipe()
621:             err_read, err_write = os.pipe()
622:             allocated.extend((in_read, in_write, err_read, err_write))
623:             record["stdin_fd"], record["stderr_fd"] = in_write, err_read
624:             close_parent = {in_read, out_write, err_write}
625:         record["parent_close_fds"] = list(close_parent)
626:         record["stdout_fd"] = out_read
627:         if any(fd < 3 for fd in allocated):
628:             raise GuardError("STANDARD_DESCRIPTORS_MUST_BE_OPEN")
629:         actions = [(os.POSIX_SPAWN_DUP2, in_read, 0), (os.POSIX_SPAWN_DUP2, out_write, 1),
630:                    (os.POSIX_SPAWN_DUP2, err_write, 2)]
631:         actions += [(os.POSIX_SPAWN_CLOSE, fd) for fd in allocated]
632:         with protected():
633:             dl.check("before protected spawn")
634:             if role == "helper" and (s.failures or s.in_helper is False):
635:                 raise GuardError("HELPER_LAUNCH_REFUSED")
636:             s.authorization_io(dl)  # LAST preparation I/O before the protected fresh-clock spawn commit.
637:             dl.check("after protected spawn authorization")
638:             record["launch_commit"] = s.time_commit("HELPER" if role == "helper" else "LAUNCH", required_s)
639:             record["mono_spawn"], record["epoch_spawn"] = time.monotonic(), time.time()
640:             if role != "helper":
641:                 s.mono_spawn, s.epoch_spawn = record["mono_spawn"], record["epoch_spawn"]
642:                 s.rec.update(mono_spawn=s.mono_spawn, epoch_spawn=s.epoch_spawn,
643:                              mono_child_deadline=s.mono_spawn + s.cfg["max_wall_s"])
644:             pid = os.posix_spawn(argv[0], argv, env, file_actions=actions, setsid=True, setsigmask=(),
645:                                  setsigdef=tuple(CANCEL_SIGNALS | {signal.SIGPIPE, signal.SIGCHLD}))
646:             record["pid"] = pid
647:             if role == "helper":
648:                 s.helper_calls += 1
649:             if role != "helper":
650:                 s.child, s.pid = record, pid
651:         try:
652:             for fd in close_parent:
653:                 os.close(fd)
654:                 allocated.remove(fd)
655:                 record["parent_close_fds"].remove(fd)
656:             dl.check("after spawn/parent pipe closes")
657:         except BaseException as e:
658:             record["spawn_error"] = repr(e)
659:         return record
660:     except BaseException:
661:         if record["pid"] is None:
662:             record["settled"] = True  # No process was created; no leader/descendant can be abandoned.
663:             for fd in allocated:
664:                 try:
665:                     os.close(fd)
666:                 except OSError:
667:                     pass
668:         raise
669: 
670: 
671: class Supervisor:
672:     def __init__(self, cfg, path, digest):
673:         self.cfg, self.config_path, self.config_sha = cfg, path, digest
674:         self.num_tb, self.arg_max = timebase(), argmax()
675:         self.self_sha = read_file(SELF, PIN_CAP, Deadline(IO_S))[0]["sha256"]
676:         self.start_epoch, self.start_mono = time.time(), time.monotonic()
677:         self.fit_epoch = min(self.start_epoch + cfg["max_chunk_seconds"] - 120, cfg["owned_compute_settled_by_epoch"] - 180)
678:         self.fit_mono = self.start_mono + self.fit_epoch - self.start_epoch
679:         self.rec = {"supervisor": VERSION, "supervisor_sha256": self.self_sha, "config_sha256": digest,
680:                     "config_path": path, "supervisor_pid": MY_PID, "uid": MY_UID,
681:                     "clock_note": "*_date: owned /bin/date output or null, never invented; mono_* and epoch_*: raw telemetry",
682:                     "parent_correction_sha256": "fabe57ab4aeacc416f22bdf334c2cc3bbd5fda1d0e777d7279187ae0564155a2",
683:                     "ownership_residual": "all post-baseline unproved identities block; sampled coverage is not an OS sandbox"}
684:         self.failures, self.failure_keys, self.trips = [], set(), {}
685:         self.child, self.keep = None, []
686:         self.pid, self.ident, self.rc = None, None, None
687:         self.lost_wait, self.exit_mono, self.exit_epoch = False, None, None
688:         self.mono_spawn, self.epoch_spawn, self.group = None, None, None
689:         self.stop, self.signal, self.clear = None, None, None
690:         self.cancel_handled = False
691:         self.owned, self.violations = {}, []
692:         self.streams, self.threads, self.prev_cpu = {}, [], {}
693:         self.samples_fd, self.sample_hash = None, hashlib.sha256()
694:         self.sample_count, self.sample_bytes, self.samples_dead = 0, 0, False
695:         self.created, self.root_fds, self.allow = {}, {}, {}
696:         self.lane_fd, self.lane_id, self.tmp_fd, self.tmp_id = None, None, None, None
697:         self.lane_bytes, self.charged, self.min_free = None, None, None
698:         self.maxima = {"lane_rss": 0, "lane_busy": 0, "owned_live": 0, "charged_bytes": 0}
699:         self.loop_status, self.authorization = "NOT_STARTED", None
700:         self.baseline = None
701:         self.helpers, self.helper_log = [], []
702:         self.helper_calls = 0
703:         self.phase = "ADMISSION"
704:         self.in_helper = False
705:         self.clock_observation = None
