708:         self.stability_expiry_mono = None
709:         self.identity_history, self.attempt_history = {}, []
710:         self.history_charge, self.history_halted, self.history_overflow = 0, False, None
711:         self.ack_output_started = False
712:         self.publication_resources, self.ack_resources = None, None
713:         self.publication_payload_bytes, self.last_accounting_observation = None, None
714: 
715:     def deadline(self, seconds, phase=None):
716:         cut = min([self.fit_mono] + [x[1] for x in self.cutoffs()])
717:         if phase is not None:
718:             cut = min(cut, phase.end)
719:         return Deadline(seconds, cut)
720: 
721:     def fail(self, key, detail=None):
722:         if key in self.failure_keys:
723:             return
724:         self.failure_keys.add(key)
725:         self.failures.append({"code": key, "detail": str(detail)[:600] if detail is not None else None, "mono": time.monotonic()})
726:         if self.pid is not None and self.stop is None:
727:             self.request_stop("FAILURE")
728: 
729:     def trip(self, key, detail=None):
730:         if key not in self.trips:
731:             self.trips[key] = {"trip": key, "detail": detail, "mono": time.monotonic(), "epoch": time.time()}
732:         self.request_stop(key)
733: 
734:     def enforce_runtime(self, where):
735:         if self.pid is None or self.mono_spawn is None:
736:             return
737:         now = time.monotonic()
738:         cutoff = self.mono_spawn + self.cfg["max_wall_s"]
739:         if now >= cutoff and "DEADLINE" not in self.trips:
740:             self.runtime_observations.append({"where": where, "mono": now, "mono_deadline": cutoff})
741:             late = now > cutoff + self.cfg["sample_s"] + 0.05
742:             self.trip("DEADLINE")
743:             if late:
744:                 self.fail("MAIN_DEADLINE_ENFORCEMENT_LATE", {"mono_observed": now, "mono_deadline": cutoff})
745: 
746:     def reap(self):
747:         if self.pid is None or self.rc is not None or self.lost_wait:
748:             return
749:         self.enforce_runtime("before_waitpid")
750:         try:
751:             p, st = os.waitpid(self.pid, os.WNOHANG)
752:         except ChildProcessError:
753:             self.lost_wait = True
754:             self.fail("WAIT_STATUS_LOST")
755:             return
756:         if p:
757:             # Publish the reaped state BEFORE any stop path can consider signalling this PID.
758:             self.rc, self.exit_mono, self.exit_epoch = os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(st), time.monotonic(), time.time()
759:         self.enforce_runtime("after_waitpid_including_success")
760: 
761:     def request_stop(self, reason):
762:         if self.stop is not None:
763:             return
764:         self.stop = {"reason": reason, "mono": time.monotonic(), "epoch": time.time()}
765:         self.signal_main_group()
766:         self.stop["date"] = self.date_now("stop")
767: 
768:     def signal_main_group(self):
769:         s = {"sent": False, "mono_check": time.monotonic()}
770:         self.signal = s
771:         if self.ident is None:
772:             s["why_not"] = "NO_IDENTITY"
773:             return
774:         if self.rc is not None:
775:             s["why_not"] = "CHILD_ALREADY_REAPED"
776:             return
777:         if self.lost_wait:
778:             s["why_not"] = "WAIT_STATUS_LOST"
779:             return
780:         if self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("identity_check_fails"):
781:             s["why_not"] = "FAULT_INJECTED"
782:             self.fail("IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED", "fault injected")
783:             return
784:         try:
785:             dl = self.deadline(IO_S)
786:             now = identify(self.pid)
787:             s["identity_observed"] = dict(now)  # Once-only stop witness survives even a full history ledger.
788:             dl.check("after main stop identity")
789:             ok = fingerprint(now) == fingerprint(self.ident)
790:             s["identity_ok"] = ok
791:             if not ok:
792:                 raise GuardError("identity mismatch")
793:             os.killpg(self.group, signal.SIGTERM)
794:             s.update(sent=True, mono_sent=time.monotonic())
795:             dl.check("after main group SIGTERM")
796:         except ProcessLookupError as e:
797:             s["error"] = repr(e)
798:         except BaseException as e:
799:             s["error"] = repr(e)
800:             self.fail("IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED", repr(e))
801: 
802:     def cutoffs(self):
803:         cuts = []
804:         if self.stop is not None:
805:             cuts.append(("GRACE_EXPIRED_AFTER_STOP_REQUEST", self.stop["mono"] + self.cfg["settle_s"]))
806:         if self.exit_mono is not None:
807:             cuts.append(("GRACE_EXPIRED_AFTER_CHILD_EXIT", self.exit_mono + self.cfg["settle_s"]))
808:         return cuts
809: 
810:     def history_abort(self, kind, witness=None):
811:         if not self.history_halted:
812:             self.history_overflow = {"kind": kind, "mono": time.monotonic(), "witness": witness}
813:         self.history_halted = True
814:         self.fail(kind, witness)
815:         raise GuardError(kind)
816: 
817:     def reserve_history(self, kind, new, old=None, witness=None, extra=0):
818:         if self.history_halted:
819:             raise GuardError("HISTORY_ALREADY_HALTED")
820:         def size(value):
821:             return len(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False).encode())
822:         # Deliberate overcharge bounds the complete compact refusal envelope, not just entry counts.
823:         delta = max(0, size(new) - (0 if old is None else size(old)))
824:         charge = 2 * delta + (64 if old is None else 0) + extra
825:         if self.history_charge + charge > 4 << 20:
826:             self.history_abort("HISTORY_BYTE_CAP", witness)
827:         self.history_charge += charge
828: 
829:     def retain_identity(self, p, source, known=False):
830:         stamp, uid = time.monotonic(), p["uniqueid"]
831:         old = self.identity_history.get(uid)
832:         if old is None:
833:             if len(self.identity_history) >= 8192:
834:                 self.history_abort("IDENTITY_HISTORY_CAP", dict(p))
835:             new = {"first_identity": dict(p), "last_identity": dict(p), "mono_first_seen": stamp,
836:                    "mono_last_seen": stamp, "source": source, "observations": 1, "variants": [dict(p)]}
837:         else:
838:             variants = old["variants"]
839:             if fingerprint(p) != fingerprint(old["last_identity"]) and not any(fingerprint(p) == fingerprint(x) for x in variants):
840:                 if len(variants) >= 64:
841:                     self.history_abort("IDENTITY_VARIANT_CAP", dict(p))
842:                 variants = variants + [dict(p)]
843:             new = dict(old, last_identity=dict(p), mono_last_seen=stamp, observations=old["observations"] + 1,
844:                        variants=variants)
845:         self.reserve_history("identity", new, old, dict(p))
846:         self.identity_history[uid] = new
847:         # Do this NOW, before any later PID, edge proof or deadline can abort the scan.
848:         if not known and self.baseline is not None and uid not in self.baseline and uid not in self.owned:
849:             previous = self.unproved_history.get(uid)
850:             if previous is None:
851:                 if len(self.unproved_history) >= 8192:
852:                     self.history_abort("UNPROVED_HISTORY_CAP", dict(p))
853:                 unknown = {"first_identity": dict(p), "last_identity": dict(p),
854:                            "mono_first_seen": stamp, "mono_last_seen": stamp}
855:             else:
856:                 unknown = dict(previous, last_identity=dict(p), mono_last_seen=stamp)
857:             self.reserve_history("unproved", unknown, previous, dict(p))
858:             self.unproved_history[uid] = unknown
859: 
860:     @contextlib.contextmanager
861:     def observation_attempt(self, kind, resources=None):
862:         if self.history_halted:
863:             raise GuardError("HISTORY_ALREADY_HALTED")
864:         if len(self.attempt_history) >= 4096:
865:             self.history_abort("ATTEMPT_HISTORY_CAP", {"kind": kind})
866:         attempt = {"kind": kind, "phase": self.phase, "inside_helper": self.in_helper,
867:                    "mono_start": time.monotonic(), "mono_end": None, "aborted": False, "error": None}
868:         self.reserve_history("attempt", attempt, extra=2048)
869:         self.attempt_history.append(attempt)
870:         try:
871:             yield attempt
872:         except BaseException as e:
873:             attempt.update(aborted=True, error=repr(e)[:600])
874:             if resources is not None:
875:                 resources.update(complete=False, aborted=True, abort_error=repr(e)[:600],
876:                                  mono_aborted=time.monotonic())
877:                 self.enforce_observed_resources(resources)
878:             raise
879:         finally:
880:             attempt["mono_end"] = time.monotonic()
881:             if resources is not None:
882:                 resources["mono_end"] = attempt["mono_end"]
883: 
884:     def enforce_observed_resources(self, c):
885:         if self.phase in ("ADMISSION", "PRE_GO"):
886:             if not self.limits(c, True):
887:                 self.fail("ADMISSION_RESOURCE_VIOLATION",
888:                           {"busy": c["lane_busy"], "rss": c["lane_rss"], "partial": True})
889:         else:
890:             self.limits(c)
891: 
892:     def reconciliation_evidence(self):
893:         return {"owned_identities": list(self.owned.values()), "unproved_history": list(self.unproved_history.values()),
894:                 "identity_history": list(self.identity_history.values()), "coverage_history": self.coverage_history,
895:                 "census_observations": self.census_history, "observation_attempts": self.attempt_history,
896:                 "helpers": self.helper_reports(), "helper_log": self.helper_log, "failures": self.failures,
897:                 "main_identity": self.ident, "main_signal": self.signal, "main_stop": self.stop,
898:                 "baseline_uniqueids": [] if self.baseline is None else sorted(self.baseline),
899:                 "stability_expiry_mono": self.stability_expiry_mono,
900:                 "history_charge_bytes": self.history_charge, "history_cap_bytes": 4 << 20,
901:                 "history_halted": self.history_halted, "history_overflow": self.history_overflow,
902:                 "publication_resources": self.publication_resources, "ack_resources": self.ack_resources,
903:                 "publication_payload_bytes": self.publication_payload_bytes,
904:                 "last_accounting_observation": self.last_accounting_observation,
905:                 "maxima": dict(self.maxima), "min_free": self.min_free}
906: 
907:     def observed_identity(self, pid, source, known=False):
908:         p = identify(pid)
909:         self.retain_identity(p, source, known)
910:         return p
911: 
912:     def identity_stable(self, pid, before):
913:         p = self.observed_identity(pid, "measurement_bracket")
914:         return fingerprint(p) == fingerprint(before)
915: 
916:     def register_owned(self, p, via, root=None):
917:         u = p["uniqueid"]
918:         self.retain_identity(p, "owned_registration", True)
919:         if u not in self.owned:
920:             entry = {"identity": dict(p), "via": via, "root": u if root is None else root,
921:                      "first_seen_mono": time.monotonic()}
922:             self.reserve_history("owned", entry, witness=dict(p))
923:             self.owned[u] = entry
924:             if via not in ("child", "helper"):
925:                 self.violations.append(dict(p))
926: 
927:     def scan_identities(self, dl):
928:         with self.observation_attempt("identity_scan") as attempt:
929:             procs, missing = {}, []
930:             for pid in all_pids(dl):
931:                 dl.check("census identification")
932:                 try:
933:                     p = self.observed_identity(pid, "pid_scan")
934:                     procs[pid] = p
935:                     attempt["identified_count"] = len(procs)
936:                 except OSError as e:
937:                     if self.history_halted:
938:                         raise
939:                     error = [pid, "identify", repr(e)[:600]]
940:                     missing.append(error)
941:                     self.retain_coverage([error], time.monotonic())
942:             live_owned = {p["uniqueid"] for p in procs.values() if p["uniqueid"] in self.owned}
943:             groups = {self.group: self.ident["uniqueid"]} if self.group is not None and self.ident is not None else {}
944:             for h in self.helpers:
945:                 if h.get("identity") is not None:
946:                     groups[h["identity"]["pgid"]] = h["identity"]["uniqueid"]
947:             if self.ident is not None:
948:                 for p in procs.values():
949:                     if p["uniqueid"] == self.ident["uniqueid"] and fingerprint(p) != fingerprint(self.ident):
950:                         self.fail("CHILD_IDENTITY_CHANGED", p)
951:             for p in procs.values():
952:                 if p["pgid"] in groups:
953:                     self.register_owned(p, "owned_group", groups[p["pgid"]])
954:                     live_owned.add(p["uniqueid"])
955:             changed = True
956:             while changed:
957:                 changed = False
958:                 for p in procs.values():
959:                     if p["uniqueid"] in live_owned:
960:                         continue
961:                     parent = procs.get(p["ppid"])
962:                     if parent is None or parent["uniqueid"] not in live_owned:
963:                         continue
964:                     try:
965:                         a = self.observed_identity(parent["pid"], "edge_parent_before")
966:                         b = self.observed_identity(p["pid"], "edge_child")
967:                         c = self.observed_identity(parent["pid"], "edge_parent_after")
968:                         if fingerprint(a) != fingerprint(parent) or fingerprint(c) != fingerprint(parent) or fingerprint(b) != fingerprint(p):
969:                             raise GuardError("CURRENT_PARENT_EDGE_CHANGED")
970:                         root = self.owned[parent["uniqueid"]]["root"]
971:                         self.register_owned(p, "current_parent:%d" % parent["uniqueid"], root)
972:                         live_owned.add(p["uniqueid"])
973:                         changed = True
974:                     except OSError as e:
975:                         if self.history_halted:
976:                             raise
977:                         error = [p["pid"], "parent_edge", repr(e)[:600]]
978:                         missing.append(error)
979:                         self.retain_coverage([error], time.monotonic())
980:             unproved = {p["uniqueid"] for p in procs.values()
981:                         if self.baseline is not None and p["uniqueid"] not in self.baseline and p["uniqueid"] not in live_owned}
982:             dl.check("after identity/ownership scan")
983:             return procs, missing, live_owned, unproved
984: 
985:     def retain_coverage(self, missing, stamp):
986:         for item in missing:
987:             if len(self.coverage_history) >= 16384:
988:                 self.history_abort("COVERAGE_HISTORY_CAP", {"observation": item})
989:             entry = {"mono": stamp, "observation": list(item)}
990:             self.reserve_history("coverage", entry, witness={"observation": item})
991:             self.coverage_history.append(entry)
992: 
993:     def establish_baseline(self):
994:         dl = Deadline(20, self.fit_mono)
995:         for _ in range(3):
996:             procs, missing, _, _ = self.scan_identities(dl)
997:             if not missing:
998:                 self.baseline = {p["uniqueid"] for p in procs.values()}
999:                 self.rec["baseline"] = {"mono_complete": time.monotonic(),
1000:                                         "identities": sorted(procs.values(), key=lambda p: p["pid"])}
1001:                 dl.check("after baseline")
1002:                 return
1003:             time.sleep(0.05)
1004:         raise GuardError("NO_COMPLETE_PRESPAWN_BASELINE")
1005: 
1006:     def census(self, dl):
1007:         if len(self.census_history) >= 4096:
1008:             self.history_abort("CENSUS_HISTORY_CAP", {"phase": self.phase})
1009:         record = {"phase": self.phase, "inside_helper": self.in_helper, "complete": False, "aborted": False,
1010:                   "mono_start": time.monotonic(), "mono_complete": None, "incomplete_n": 0, "incomplete": [],
1011:                   "pids": 0, "owned_live": [], "unproved_live": [], "group_live": [], "owned_zombies": [],
1012:                   "lane_pids": [], "lane_rss": 0, "lane_busy_pids": [], "lane_busy": 0, "measurements": []}
1013:         self.reserve_history("resource_attempt", record, extra=2048)
1014:         self.census_history.append(record)  # Retained BEFORE identity or resource I/O can abort.
1015:         with self.observation_attempt("resource_scan", record):
1016:             procs, missing, live_owned, unproved = self.scan_identities(dl)
1017:             header = dict(record, pids=len(procs), incomplete_n=len(missing), incomplete=missing[:30],
1018:                           owned_live=[dict(p) for p in procs.values() if p["uniqueid"] in live_owned],
1019:                           unproved_live=[dict(p) for p in procs.values() if p["uniqueid"] in unproved],
1020:                           group_live=sorted(p["pid"] for p in procs.values() if self.group is not None and p["pgid"] == self.group),
1021:                           owned_zombies=sorted(p["pid"] for p in procs.values() if p["uniqueid"] in live_owned and p["status"] == SZOMB))
1022:             self.reserve_history("resource_header", header, record, {"phase": self.phase})
1023:             record.update(header)
1024:             cpu = {}
1025:             for pid, p in procs.items():
1026:                 dl.check("census accounting")
1027:                 if p["status"] == SZOMB or not (p["euid"] == MY_UID or p["ruid"] == MY_UID):
1028:                     continue
1029:                 owned = p["uniqueid"] in live_owned or p["uniqueid"] in unproved
1030:                 try:
1031:                     if not self.identity_stable(pid, p):
1032:                         raise GuardError("BEFORE_MEASUREMENT_CHANGED")
1033:                     member = owned
1034:                     if not member:
1035:                         c1 = cwd_of(pid)
1036:                         av = argv_of(pid, self.arg_max)
1037:                         c2 = cwd_of(pid)
1038:                         member = any(below(c, r) for c in (c1, c2) for r in self.cfg["lane_roots"]) or any(
1039:                             r in a for a in av for r in self.cfg["lane_roots"])
1040:                     if not self.identity_stable(pid, p):
1041:                         raise GuardError("MEMBERSHIP_IDENTITY_CHANGED")
1042:                     if not member:
1043:                         continue
1044:                     rss, ticks = task_of(pid)
1045:                     key = (p["uniqueid"], p["idversion"])
1046:                     prior = self.prev_cpu.get(key)
1047:                     start = start_of(pid) if prior is None else None
1048:                     final_identity = identify(pid)  # Acquire/verify first; history charging cannot erase a completed bracket.
1049:                     if fingerprint(final_identity) != fingerprint(p):
1050:                         raise GuardError("TASK_IDENTITY_CHANGED")
1051:                     now_m, now_e = time.monotonic(), time.time()
1052:                     if prior is None:
1053:                         seconds = now_e - start
1054:                         busy = seconds <= 0.05 or ticks * self.num_tb >= seconds * 1e9 * self.cfg["busy_cpu_fraction"]
1055:                     else:
1056:                         seconds = now_m - prior[0]
1057:                         busy = seconds <= 0 or (ticks - prior[1]) * self.num_tb >= seconds * 1e9 * self.cfg["busy_cpu_fraction"]
1058:                     measured = {"identity": dict(p), "identity_after": dict(final_identity),
1059:                                 "rss": rss, "cpu_ticks": ticks, "busy": busy, "mono_observed": now_m}
1060:                     # Totals and observed excesses are latched BEFORE another I/O or any possible abort.
1061:                     record["lane_pids"].append(pid)
1062:                     record["lane_rss"] += rss
1063:                     if busy:
1064:                         record["lane_busy_pids"].append(pid)
1065:                         record["lane_busy"] += 1
1066:                     record["mono_last_observation"] = now_m
1067:                     record["measurements"].append(measured)  # At most one bounded composite beyond a history cap, then abort.
1068:                     cpu[key] = (now_m, ticks)
1069:                     try:
1070:                         self.enforce_observed_resources(record)
1071:                         self.retain_identity(final_identity, "measurement_final_bracket")
1072:                         self.reserve_history("measurement", measured, witness=measured, extra=256)
1073:                     except BaseException:
1074:                         if self.history_halted and self.history_overflow is not None:
1075:                             self.history_overflow["completed_measurement"] = measured
1076:                         self.enforce_observed_resources(record)
1077:                         raise
1078:                 except OSError as e:
1079:                     if self.history_halted:
1080:                         raise
1081:                     error = [pid, "real_or_effective_uid_measurement", repr(e)[:600]]
1082:                     missing.append(error)
1083:                     self.retain_coverage([error], time.monotonic())
1084:                     record["incomplete_n"] = len(missing)
1085:                     record["incomplete"] = missing[:30]
1086:             self.prev_cpu = cpu
1087:             record["lane_pids"].sort()
1088:             record["lane_busy_pids"].sort()
1089:             self.enforce_observed_resources(record)
1090:             dl.check("after census including completed resource observations")
1091:             record.update(complete=not missing, incomplete_n=len(missing), incomplete=missing[:30],
1092:                           mono_complete=time.monotonic())
1093:             return record
1094: 
1095:     def full_census(self, dl):
1096:         c = None
1097:         for _ in range(3):
1098:             c = self.census(dl)
1099:             # Enforce EVERY observation, not only the final successful retry.
1100:             if self.phase in ("ADMISSION", "PRE_GO"):
1101:                 if not self.limits(c, True):
1102:                     self.fail("ADMISSION_RESOURCE_VIOLATION", {"busy": c["lane_busy"], "rss": c["lane_rss"],
1103:                                                                "incomplete": not c["complete"]})
1104:             else:
1105:                 self.limits(c)
1106:             if c["complete"]:
1107:                 break
1108:             dl.check("before census retry")
1109:             time.sleep(min(0.05, max(0, dl.end - time.monotonic())))
1110:         dl.check("after census retries")
1111:         return c
1112: 
1113:     def helper_probe(self, h, dl):
1114:         """Refresh ALL ownership predicates; REAPED alone never marks a helper settled."""
1115:         if h["pid"] is None:
1116:             return
1117:         for fd in list(h["parent_close_fds"]):
1118:             os.close(fd)
1119:             h["parent_close_fds"].remove(fd)
1120:             dl.check("helper parent endpoint close")
1121:         if self.history_halted:
1122:             # Still reap/drain within the bounded cleanup; never claim group/coverage proof from a full ledger.
1123:             if not h["leader_reaped"] and not h["wait_lost"]:
1124:                 try:
1125:                     pid, status = os.waitpid(h["pid"], os.WNOHANG)
1126:                     if pid:
1127:                         h["leader_reaped"], h["rc"], h["mono_reaped"] = True, os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status), time.monotonic()
1128:                 except ChildProcessError:
1129:                     h["wait_lost"] = True
1130:                 dl.check("after cap-path helper reap")
1131:             if h.get("stdout_fd") is not None:
1132:                 os.set_blocking(h["stdout_fd"], False)
1133:                 try:
1134:                     data = os.read(h["stdout_fd"], max(1, 257 - len(h["capture"])))
1135:                     h["capture"] += data
1136:                     if data == b"" or len(h["capture"]) > 256:
1137:                         h["raw_eof"] = data == b""
1138:                         h["capture_overflow"] = len(h["capture"]) > 256
1139:                         os.close(h["stdout_fd"])
1140:                         h["stdout_fd"], h["stdout_closed"] = None, True
1141:                     dl.check("after cap-path helper capture/close")
1142:                 except BlockingIOError:
1143:                     pass
1144:             h["settled"] = False
1145:             raise GuardError("HISTORY_HALTED_HELPER_PROOF_UNAVAILABLE")
1146:         if h.get("identity") is None and not h["leader_reaped"]:
1147:             h["identity"] = self.observed_identity(h["pid"], "helper_initial", True)
1148:             p = h["identity"]
1149:             h["initial_identity_valid"] = p["pgid"] == h["pid"] and p["euid"] == MY_UID and p["ruid"] == MY_UID
1150:             if not h["initial_identity_valid"]:
1151:                 raise GuardError("HELPER_IDENTITY_INVALID")
1152:             self.register_owned(p, "helper")
1153:         if not h["leader_reaped"] and not self.identity_stable(h["pid"], h["identity"]):
1154:             raise GuardError("HELPER_EXEC_OR_CREDENTIAL_CHANGE")
1155:         if not h["leader_reaped"] and not h["wait_lost"]:
1156:             try:
1157:                 pid, status = os.waitpid(h["pid"], os.WNOHANG)
1158:                 if pid:
1159:                     h["leader_reaped"], h["rc"] = True, os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
1160:                     h["mono_reaped"] = time.monotonic()
1161:             except ChildProcessError:
1162:                 h["wait_lost"] = True
1163:                 raise GuardError("HELPER_WAIT_STATUS_LOST")
1164:             dl.check("after helper reap, including a successful reap")
1165:         fd = h.get("stdout_fd")
1166:         if fd is not None:
1167:             os.set_blocking(fd, False)
1168:             for _ in range(2):
1169:                 try:
1170:                     room = 257 - len(h["capture"])
1171:                     if room <= 0:
1172:                         h["capture_overflow"] = True
1173:                         break
1174:                     b = os.read(fd, room)
1175:                 except BlockingIOError:
1176:                     break
1177:                 dl.check("after helper stdout read")
1178:                 if b == b"":
1179:                     h["raw_eof"] = True
1180:                     break
1181:                 h["capture"] += b
1182:                 if len(h["capture"]) > 256:
1183:                     h["capture_overflow"] = True
1184:                     break
1185:             if h["raw_eof"] or h["capture_overflow"]:
1186:                 os.close(fd)
1187:                 h["stdout_fd"], h["stdout_closed"] = None, True
1188:                 dl.check("after helper stdout close")
1189:         c = self.full_census(dl)
1190:         proof = {k: c[k] for k in ("complete", "incomplete_n", "owned_live", "unproved_live", "mono_complete")}
1191:         self.reserve_history("helper_proof", proof, h.get("proof"), {"helper_pid": h["pid"]})
1192:         h["proof"] = proof
1193:         root = None if h.get("identity") is None else h["identity"]["uniqueid"]
1194:         h["group_clear"] = h.get("identity") is not None and not any(p["pgid"] == h["identity"]["pgid"] for p in c["owned_live"])
1195:         h["descendants_clear"] = root is not None and not any(self.owned[p["uniqueid"]]["root"] == root for p in c["owned_live"])
1196:         h["coverage_complete"] = c["complete"] and not c["unproved_live"]
1197:         h["eof_proved"] = h["raw_eof"] and not h["capture_overflow"]
1198:         if h["fault_group"]:
1199:             h["group_clear"] = False
1200:         if h["fault_eof"]:
1201:             h["eof_proved"] = False
1202:         dl.check("after full helper ownership proof")
1203:         h["mono_proof_complete"] = time.monotonic()
1204:         h["settled"] = bool(h.get("initial_identity_valid") and h["leader_reaped"] and not h["wait_lost"] and
1205:                             h["group_clear"] and h["descendants_clear"] and h["coverage_complete"] and
1206:                             h["eof_proved"] and h["stdout_closed"])
1207: 
1208:     def helper_term(self, h, dl):
1209:         if h["term_attempted"] or h["leader_reaped"] or h["wait_lost"]:
1210:             return
1211:         h["term_attempted"] = True
1212:         ident = h.get("identity")
1213:         if ident is None or not h.get("initial_identity_valid"):
1214:             h["signal"] = {"sent": False, "reason": "NO_VERIFIED_IDENTITY"}
1215:             return
1216:         h["signal"] = {"sent": False, "mono_check": time.monotonic()}
1217:         try:
1218:             p = identify(h["pid"])
1219:             h["signal"]["identity_observed"] = dict(p)
1220:             h["signal"]["mono_identity_observed"] = time.monotonic()
1221:             dl.check("after helper stop identity")
1222:             if fingerprint(p) != fingerprint(ident):
1223:                 raise GuardError("HELPER_SIGNAL_IDENTITY_CHANGED")
1224:             os.killpg(ident["pgid"], signal.SIGTERM)
1225:             h["signal"].update(sent=True, identity_ok=True, mono_sent=time.monotonic())
1226:             dl.check("after helper group SIGTERM")
1227:         except BaseException as e:
1228:             h["signal"]["reason"] = repr(e)
1229: 
1230:     def helper_cleanup(self, h):
1231:         """Exception/overrun path: one verified TERM at most; proof/reap/EOF/group stay owned through bounded grace."""
1232:         h["mono_cleanup_start"] = time.monotonic()
1233:         dl = self.deadline(self.cfg["helper_budget"]["term_grace_s"])
1234:         h["mono_cleanup_deadline"] = dl.end
1235:         if h.get("identity") is None and not h["leader_reaped"]:
1236:             try:
1237:                 p = identify(h["pid"])
1238:                 h["identity"] = p
1239:                 h["initial_identity_valid"] = p["pgid"] == h["pid"] and p["euid"] == MY_UID and p["ruid"] == MY_UID
1240:                 if h["initial_identity_valid"]:
1241:                     self.register_owned(p, "helper")
1242:             except BaseException as e:
1243:                 h["identity_error"] = repr(e)
1244:         if time.monotonic() <= dl.end:
1245:             self.helper_term(h, dl)
1246:         while time.monotonic() <= dl.end:
1247:             try:
1248:                 self.helper_probe(h, dl)
1249:                 if h["settled"]:
1250:                     return
1251:             except BaseException as e:
1252:                 h["cleanup_error"] = repr(e)
1253:                 if time.monotonic() > dl.end:
1254:                     break
1255:             time.sleep(min(0.005, max(0, dl.end - time.monotonic())))
1256:         self.fail("HELPER_OWNERSHIP_UNSETTLED", {"pid": h["pid"], "reaped": h["leader_reaped"],
1257:                                                 "group_clear": h.get("group_clear"), "eof": h.get("eof_proved")})
1258: 
1259:     def date_now(self, purpose):
1260:         """Actual /bin/date output only. Logical refusals return null; no synthetic timestamp."""
1261:         entry = {"purpose": purpose, "mono_start": time.monotonic()}
1262:         h = None
1263:         hb = self.cfg["helper_budget"]
1264:         try:
1265:             if self.failures or pending_cancel() or self.in_helper:
1266:                 entry["outcome"] = "REFUSED_FAILURE_CANCEL_OR_REENTRANCY"
1267:                 return None
1268:             if self.authorization is None:
1269:                 entry["outcome"] = "REFUSED_NO_AUTHORIZATION"
1270:                 return None
1271:             if self.helper_calls >= hb["max_calls"]:
1272:                 self.fail("HELPER_CALL_BUDGET")
1273:                 entry["outcome"] = "CALL_BUDGET_REFUSED"
1274:                 return None
1275:             if self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("date_helper_fails"):
1276:                 self.fail("HELPER_FAULT_INJECTED")
1277:                 entry["outcome"] = "FAULT_BEFORE_SPAWN"
1278:                 return None
1279:             if self.baseline is None:
1280:                 self.fail("NO_PREHELPER_BASELINE")
1281:                 entry["outcome"] = "NO_BASELINE"
1282:                 return None
1283:             self.in_helper = True
1284:             call = self.deadline(hb["call_budget_s"])  # BEFORE pipe creation and spawn.
1285:             try:
1286:                 h = spawn_registered(self, ["/bin/date", "+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"], {"PATH": "/usr/bin:/bin"},
1287:                                      "helper", call, hb["call_budget_s"] + hb["term_grace_s"])
1288:             except BaseException:
1289:                 # The allocation ledger is pre-registered; recover any returned PID even if later setup failed.
1290:                 h = next((r for r in reversed(self.helpers) if r.get("pid") is not None and "capture" not in r), None)
1291:                 raise
1292:             h.update(capture=b"", raw_eof=False, stdout_closed=False, capture_overflow=False,
1293:                      initial_identity_valid=False, clock_failed=False,
1294:                      fault_group=bool(self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("helper_group_unproved")),
1295:                      fault_eof=bool(self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("helper_eof_unproved")),
1296:                      mono_call_start=entry["mono_start"], mono_call_deadline=call.end, date=None)
1297:             entry["pid"] = h["pid"]
1298:             if self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("helper_observation_overrun"):
1299:                 h["mono_injection_start"] = time.monotonic()
1300:                 time.sleep(max(0.0, call.end - time.monotonic()) + 0.01)
1301:                 h["mono_injection_end"] = time.monotonic()
1302:                 if h["mono_injection_end"] <= call.end:
1303:                     raise GuardError("OVERRUN_STIMULUS_NOT_ESTABLISHED")
1304:             if h["spawn_error"] is not None:
1305:                 raise GuardError(h["spawn_error"])
1306:             while True:
1307:                 if pending_cancel():
1308:                     raise GuardError("HELPER_CANCELLED_AFTER_COMMIT")
1309:                 self.helper_probe(h, call)
1310:                 call.check("after helper completion, never accept a late successful reap")
1311:                 if h["settled"]:
1312:                     break
1313:                 time.sleep(min(0.005, max(0, call.end - time.monotonic())))
1314:             text = h["capture"].decode("ascii", "strict").strip()
1315:             shape = len(text) == 24 and text[4:5] == "-" and text[7:8] == "-" and text[10:11] == "T" and text[19:20] in ("+", "-")
1316:             digits = text[:4] + text[5:7] + text[8:10] + text[11:13] + text[14:16] + text[17:19] + text[20:24]
1317:             if h["rc"] != 0 or not shape or not digits.isdigit() or text[13:14] != ":" or text[16:17] != ":":
1318:                 raise GuardError("HELPER_BAD_DATE_OUTPUT")
1319:             h["date"] = text
1320:             observation = {"date": text, "mono_before_read": entry["mono_start"],
1321:                            "mono_after_read": time.monotonic(), "epoch_date_floor": parse_date_epoch(text)}
1322:             base_age = observation["epoch_date_floor"] - self.authorization["stability_epoch"] + 1.0
1323:             if base_age < 1.0:
1324:                 raise GuardError("STABILITY_TIMESTAMP_IN_FUTURE")
1325:             candidate = observation["mono_before_read"] + self.cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"] - base_age
1326:             h["stability_expiry_before"] = self.stability_expiry_mono
1327:             h["stability_expiry_candidate"] = candidate
1328:             self.stability_expiry_mono = candidate if self.stability_expiry_mono is None else min(self.stability_expiry_mono, candidate)
1329:             h["stability_expiry_effective"] = self.stability_expiry_mono
1330:             self.clock_observation = observation
1331:             entry["outcome"] = "OK"
1332:             return text
1333:         except BaseException as e:
1334:             entry["outcome"], entry["error"] = "FAILED", repr(e)
1335:             self.fail("HELPER_CALL_FAILED", repr(e))
1336:             if h is not None and h.get("pid") is not None:
1337:                 # Populate absent fields before exceptional cleanup; registered ownership is never discarded.
1338:                 for key, value in {"capture": b"", "raw_eof": False, "stdout_closed": False, "capture_overflow": False,
1339:                                    "initial_identity_valid": False,
1340:                                    "fault_group": bool(self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("helper_group_unproved")),
1341:                                    "fault_eof": bool(self.cfg["fault_injection"].get("helper_eof_unproved")),
1342:                                    "mono_call_start": entry["mono_start"], "mono_call_deadline": call.end,
1343:                                    "date": None}.items():
1344:                     h.setdefault(key, value)
1345:                 h["clock_failed"] = True
1346:                 self.helper_cleanup(h)
1347:             return None
1348:         finally:
1349:             self.in_helper = False
1350:             entry["mono_end"] = time.monotonic()
1351:             self.helper_log.append(entry)
1352: 
1353:     def refresh_helpers(self, dl):
1354:         for h in self.helpers:
1355:             if h.get("pid") is not None and not h["settled"]:
1356:                 try:
1357:                     self.helper_probe(h, dl)
1358:                 except BaseException as e:
1359:                     h["final_proof_error"] = repr(e)
1360:                 if not h["settled"]:
1361:                     self.fail("HELPER_OWNERSHIP_UNSETTLED", h["pid"])
1362: 
1363:     def helper_reports(self):
1364:         keys = ("pid", "identity", "leader_reaped", "rc", "wait_lost", "term_attempted", "settled",
1365:                 "initial_identity_valid", "stdout_closed", "eof_proved", "group_clear", "descendants_clear",
1366:                 "coverage_complete", "clock_failed", "date", "signal", "proof", "spawn_error", "launch_commit",
1367:                 "mono_call_start", "mono_call_deadline", "mono_cleanup_start", "mono_cleanup_deadline",
1368:                 "mono_proof_complete", "mono_reaped", "mono_injection_start", "mono_injection_end",
1369:                 "stability_expiry_before", "stability_expiry_candidate", "stability_expiry_effective")
1370:         return [{**{k: h.get(k) for k in keys}, "capture_hex": h.get("capture", b"").hex()}
1371:                 for h in self.helpers if h.get("pid") is not None]
1372: 
1373:     def open_anchors(self):
1374:         dl = Deadline(IO_S)
1375:         root = self.cfg["lane_disk_root"]
1376:         self.lane_fd = absolute_directory(root, dl)
1377:         self.lane_id = stat_id(self.lane_fd)
1378:         for r in self.cfg["output_roots"]:
1379:             fd = directory_at(self.lane_fd, os.path.relpath(r, root), dl)
1380:             self.root_fds[r] = (fd, stat_id(fd))
1381:             self.allow[r] = set()
1382:         tmp = self.cfg["env"]["TMPDIR"]
1383:         self.tmp_fd = directory_at(self.lane_fd, os.path.relpath(tmp, root), dl)
1384:         self.tmp_id = stat_id(self.tmp_fd)
1385:         for p in [self.cfg[k] for k in ("stdout", "stderr", "samples", "launch_record", "receipt", "settlement_record")] + [o["path"] for o in self.cfg["outputs"]]:
1386:             self.allow[os.path.dirname(p)].add(os.path.basename(p))
1387:         self.allow[os.path.dirname(self.cfg["settlement_record"])].add(os.path.basename(self.cfg["settlement_record"]) + ".staged")
1388:         self.verify_anchors(dl)
1389: 
1390:     def verify_anchors(self, dl):
1391:         root = self.cfg["lane_disk_root"]
1392:         fd = absolute_directory(root, dl)
1393:         try:
1394:             if stat_id(fd) != self.lane_id or stat_id(self.lane_fd) != self.lane_id:
1395:                 raise GuardError("LANE_ANCHOR_CHANGED")
1396:         finally:
1397:             os.close(fd)
1398:         dl.check("after lane identity close")
1399:         locations = [(r, fd, ident) for r, (fd, ident) in self.root_fds.items()] + [(self.cfg["env"]["TMPDIR"], self.tmp_fd, self.tmp_id)]
1400:         for path, held, ident in locations:
1401:             fd = directory_at(self.lane_fd, os.path.relpath(path, root), dl)
1402:             try:
1403:                 if stat_id(fd) != ident or stat_id(held) != ident or ident[0] != self.lane_id[0]:
1404:                     raise GuardError("CHARGED_ANCHOR_CHANGED " + path)
1405:             finally:
1406:                 os.close(fd)
1407:             dl.check("after charged anchor close")
1408:         lane = absolute_directory(self.cfg["lane"], dl)
1409:         try:
1410:             if os.fstat(lane).st_dev != self.lane_id[0]:
1411:                 raise GuardError("LANE_CWD_DIFFERENT_VOLUME")
1412:         finally:
1413:             os.close(lane)
1414:         for root in self.cfg["lane_roots"]:
1415:             fd = absolute_directory(root, dl)
1416:             os.close(fd)
1417:             dl.check("after lane root close")
1418: 
1419:     def location(self, path):
1420:         return self.root_fds[os.path.dirname(path)][0], os.path.basename(path)
1421: 
1422:     def free(self, dl):
1423:         dl.check("before fstatvfs")
1424:         s = os.fstatvfs(self.lane_fd)
1425:         value = s.f_bavail * s.f_frsize
1426:         dl.check("after fstatvfs")
1427:         return value
1428: 
1429:     def accounting(self, dl, lane=True):
1430:         self.verify_anchors(dl)
1431:         seen = set()
1432:         charge = sum(walk_fd(fd, self.lane_id[0], dl, seen) for fd, _ in self.root_fds.values())
1433:         charge += walk_fd(self.tmp_fd, self.lane_id[0], dl, seen)
1434:         total = walk_fd(self.lane_fd, self.lane_id[0], dl) if lane else self.lane_bytes
1435:         free = self.free(dl)
1436:         self.charged, self.lane_bytes = charge, total
1437:         self.min_free = free if self.min_free is None else min(self.min_free, free)
1438:         self.maxima["charged_bytes"] = max(charge, self.maxima["charged_bytes"])
1439:         resources = {"charged_bytes": charge, "lane_bytes": total, "free": free}
1440:         self.last_accounting_observation = {"resources": dict(resources), "mono_observed": time.monotonic()}
1441:         dl.check("after accounting")
1442:         return resources
1443: 
1444:     def reservation_fits(self, a, payload=0):
1445:         adm = self.cfg["admission"]
1446:         remaining = max(0, adm["reservation_bytes"] - a["charged_bytes"] - payload)
1447:         return a["charged_bytes"] + payload <= adm["reservation_bytes"] and \
1448:             a["free"] - payload - adm["outstanding_reservations_bytes"] - remaining >= self.cfg["free_trip_bytes"] and \
1449:             a["lane_bytes"] + payload + adm["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"] + remaining <= self.cfg["lane_disk_ceiling_bytes"]
1450: 
1451:     def resource_age(self, c):
1452:         if time.monotonic() - c["mono_complete"] > max(1.0, self.cfg["sample_s"]):
1453:             raise GuardError("RESOURCE_CENSUS_STALE_AT_COMMIT")
1454: 
1455:     def limits(self, c, admission=False):
1456:         busy = c["lane_busy"]
1457:         if admission:
1458:             if self.pid in c["lane_busy_pids"]:
1459:                 busy -= 1
1460:             return busy + self.cfg["cpu_slots"] <= POLICY["busy"] and c["lane_rss"] + self.cfg["rss_projection_bytes"] <= self.cfg["rss_trip_bytes"]
1461:         if c["lane_busy"] > self.cfg["busy_limit"]:
1462:             self.trip("BUSY", {"count": c["lane_busy"], "pids": c["lane_busy_pids"]})
1463:         if c["lane_rss"] > self.cfg["rss_trip_bytes"]:
1464:             self.trip("RSS", c["lane_rss"])
1465:         self.maxima["lane_busy"] = max(self.maxima["lane_busy"], c["lane_busy"])
1466:         self.maxima["lane_rss"] = max(self.maxima["lane_rss"], c["lane_rss"])
1467:         self.maxima["owned_live"] = max(self.maxima["owned_live"], len(c["owned_live"]))
1468:         return True
1469: 
1470:     def disk_trips(self, a):
1471:         if a["free"] < self.cfg["free_trip_bytes"]:
1472:             self.trip("FREE", a["free"])
1473:         if a["charged_bytes"] > self.cfg["output_trip_bytes"]:
1474:             self.trip("OUTPUT", a["charged_bytes"])
1475:         unused = max(0, self.cfg["admission"]["reservation_bytes"] - a["charged_bytes"])
1476:         if a["lane_bytes"] + self.cfg["admission"]["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"] + unused > self.cfg["lane_disk_ceiling_bytes"]:
1477:             self.trip("LANE_CEILING", a["lane_bytes"])
1478:         if any(v["overflow"] for v in self.streams.values()):
1479:             self.trip("STREAM_CAP", {k: [v["bytes_seen"], v["bytes_written"]] for k, v in self.streams.items()})
1480: 
1481:     def authorization_io(self, dl):
1482:         adm = self.cfg["admission"]
1483:         ga, gb = read_file(adm["go_path"], SMALL, dl, keep=True)
1484:         sa, sb = read_file(adm["stability_receipt_path"], SMALL, dl, keep=True)
1485:         go, sr = parse_json(gb), parse_json(sb)
1486:         if not isinstance(go, dict) or go.get("decision") != "GO" or go.get("item") != self.cfg["item"]:
1487:             raise GuardError("GO_NOT_FOR_ITEM")
1488:         expected = {k: self.cfg[k] for k in BOUND_KEYS[:5]}
1489:         expected.update({k: adm[k] for k in BOUND_KEYS[5:]})
1490:         entries = go.get("configs")
1491:         if not isinstance(entries, dict) or self.config_sha not in entries:
1492:             raise GuardError("GO_DOES_NOT_NAME_THIS_CONFIG")
1493:         if entries != {self.config_sha: expected}:
1494:             raise GuardError("GO_BINDING_MISMATCH")
1495:         channel = self.cfg["creation_channel"]
1496:         if channel:
1497:             negative = go.get("qualification_negative")
1498:             if not isinstance(negative, dict) or set(negative) != {"scenario", "kind", "creation_channel"} or \
1499:                     negative["scenario"] != channel["scenario"] or negative["kind"] != "mutation" or negative["creation_channel"] is not True:
1500:                 raise GuardError("MUTATION_NEGATIVE_GO_NOT_EXPLICIT")
1501:             pa, _ = read_file(channel["preparation_path"], SMALL, dl)
1502:             if pa["sha256"] != channel["preparation_sha256"]:
1503:                 raise GuardError("CREATION_PREPARATION_BINDING_CHANGED")
1504:         if not number(go.get("not_after_epoch")) or not hex64(go.get("stability_receipt_sha256")):
1505:             raise GuardError("GO_FRESHNESS_FIELDS_INVALID")
1506:         if go["stability_receipt_sha256"] != sa["sha256"] or not isinstance(sr, dict) or sr.get("verdict") != "PASS":
1507:             raise GuardError("STABILITY_DIGEST_OR_VERDICT_MISMATCH")
1508:         stability_epoch = parse_date_epoch(sr.get("now_date"))
1509:         out = {"go_sha256": ga["sha256"], "stability_receipt_sha256": sa["sha256"],
1510:                "not_after_epoch": go["not_after_epoch"], "stability_epoch": stability_epoch,
1511:                "stability_date": sr["now_date"]}
1512:         if self.authorization is not None and self.authorization != out:
1513:             raise GuardError("AUTHORIZATION_CHANGED")
1514:         self.authorization = out
1515:         dl.check("after authorization I/O")
1516:         return out
1517: 
1518:     def stability_age(self, mono):
1519:         if self.authorization is None or self.clock_observation is None or self.stability_expiry_mono is None:
1520:             raise GuardError("STABILITY_AGE_UNVERIFIABLE")
1521:         obs = self.clock_observation
1522:         # Include whole-second resolution and elapsed time from BEFORE the actual reading/cleanup.
1523:         base = obs["epoch_date_floor"] - self.authorization["stability_epoch"]
1524:         if base < 0:
1525:             raise GuardError("STABILITY_TIMESTAMP_IN_FUTURE")
1526:         upper = max(base + 1.0 + max(0.0, mono - obs["mono_before_read"]),
1527:                     self.cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"] + mono - self.stability_expiry_mono)
1528:         if upper > self.cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"]:
1529:             raise GuardError("STABILITY_STALE")
1530:         return upper
1531: 
1532:     def creation_manifest(self, dl):
1533:         """Only explicitly declared mutation negatives receive this fixed extra JSON line; ordinary GO stays exact."""
1534:         channel = self.cfg["creation_channel"]
1535:         if not channel:
1536:             return b""
1537:         pa, raw = read_file(channel["preparation_path"], SMALL, dl, keep=True)
1538:         prep = parse_json(raw)
1539:         required = {"kind", "scenario", "run", "output_root", "fixture", "source_pins", "python", "allocation", "negative_control"}
1540:         if pa["sha256"] != channel["preparation_sha256"] or not isinstance(prep, dict) or set(prep) != required or \
1541:                 prep["kind"] != "qualification-creation-preparation-v1" or prep["scenario"] != channel["scenario"] or prep["negative_control"] is not True:
1542:             raise GuardError("CREATION_PREPARATION_SCHEMA_OR_BINDING")
1543:         pins = prep["source_pins"]
1544:         if not isinstance(pins, dict) or set(pins) != {"supervisor", "child", "writer", "checker"} or any(
1545:                 not isinstance(v, dict) or set(v) != {"path", "sha256"} or not norm_abs(v["path"]) or not hex64(v["sha256"])
1546:                 for v in pins.values()) or pins["supervisor"] != {"path": SELF, "sha256": self.self_sha}:
1547:             raise GuardError("CREATION_PREPARATION_SOURCE_PINS")
1548:         expected_allocation = {"bytes": self.cfg["admission"]["reservation_bytes"], "cpu_slots": self.cfg["cpu_slots"],
1549:                                "outstanding_reservations_bytes": self.cfg["admission"]["outstanding_reservations_bytes"],
1550:                                "lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes": self.cfg["admission"]["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"]}
1551:         if prep["allocation"] != expected_allocation or not isinstance(prep["python"], dict) or set(prep["python"]) != {"path", "sha256"} or \
1552:                 prep["python"]["path"] != self.cfg["argv"][0] or not hex64(prep["python"]["sha256"]):
1553:             raise GuardError("CREATION_PREPARATION_ALLOCATION_OR_INTERPRETER")
1554:         out = self.cfg["output_roots"][0]
1555:         run = os.path.dirname(out)
1556:         rfd = directory_at(self.lane_fd, os.path.relpath(run, self.cfg["lane_disk_root"]), dl)
1557:         try:
1558:             if prep["run"] != {"path": run, "inode": list(stat_id(rfd))} or \
1559:                     prep["output_root"] != {"path": out, "inode": list(self.root_fds[out][1])}:
1560:                 raise GuardError("CREATION_RUN_ANCHOR_CHANGED")
1561:             if channel["scenario"] == "mutate_pin":
1562:                 expected = prep["fixture"]
1563:                 if not isinstance(expected, dict) or set(expected) != {"path", "inode", "sha256", "bytes", "nlink"} or \
1564:                         expected["path"] != channel["target_path"] or not integer(expected["nlink"]) or expected["nlink"] != 1 or \
1565:                         not integer(expected["bytes"]) or expected["bytes"] < 1 or not hex64(expected["sha256"]) or \
1566:                         not isinstance(expected["inode"], list) or len(expected["inode"]) != 2 or not all(integer(v) and v >= 0 for v in expected["inode"]):
1567:                     raise GuardError("PIN_CREATION_MANIFEST_SCHEMA")
1568:                 actual, _ = read_file("pinned-scratch.txt", SMALL, dl, rfd)
1569:             else:
1570:                 if prep["fixture"] is not None:
1571:                     raise GuardError("LAUNCH_MUTATION_HAS_UNDECLARED_PIN_FIXTURE")
1572:                 target = self.cfg["launch_record"]
1573:                 expected = {"path": target, "inode": list(self.created[target]),
1574:                             "sha256": self.rec["launch_record_sha256"], "bytes": None, "nlink": 1}
1575:                 fd, name = self.location(target)
1576:                 actual, _ = read_file(name, SMALL, dl, fd)
1577:                 expected["bytes"] = actual["bytes"]
1578:             for k in ("inode", "sha256", "bytes", "nlink"):
1579:                 if actual[k] != expected[k]:
1580:                     raise GuardError("ORIGINAL_CREATION_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_GO")
1581:             manifest = {"kind": "qualification-creation-manifest-v1", "scenario": channel["scenario"],
1582:                         "preparation_sha256": pa["sha256"], "run": prep["run"], "output_root": prep["output_root"],
1583:                         "target": expected}
1584:             line = (json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()
1585:             if len(line) > 4096:
1586:                 raise GuardError("CREATION_MANIFEST_BYTE_CAP")
1587:             self.rec["go_creation_manifest"] = manifest
1588:             self.rec["go_creation_manifest_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest()
1589:             dl.check("after original-creation manifest")
1590:             return line
1591:         finally:
1592:             os.close(rfd)
1593:             dl.check("after creation manifest anchor close")
1594: 
1595:     def time_commit(self, phase, required_s=0):
1596:         """Fresh raw clocks after LAST binding I/O, while cancellation is protected."""
1597:         epoch, mono = time.time(), time.monotonic()
1598:         if pending_cancel():
1599:             raise GuardError("CANCEL_AT_" + phase)
1600:         if self.authorization is None or epoch > self.authorization["not_after_epoch"]:
1601:             raise GuardError("GO_EXPIRED_AT_" + phase)
1602:         if epoch + required_s > self.fit_epoch or mono + required_s > self.fit_mono:
1603:             raise GuardError("FIT_FAILED_AT_" + phase)
1604:         age = None if phase == "HELPER" and self.clock_observation is None else self.stability_age(mono)
1605:         return {"phase": phase, "mono": mono, "epoch": epoch, "required_s": required_s,
1606:                 "stability_age_upper_s": age}
1607: 
1608:     def completion_check(self, phase, dl):
1609:         if phase == "GO":
1610:             observed = time.monotonic()
1611:             age = observed - self.mono_spawn
1612:             remaining = self.mono_spawn + self.cfg["max_wall_s"] - observed
1613:             bound = min(60.0, self.cfg["max_wall_s"] / 2.0)
1614:             self.rec["go_delivery_completion"] = {"mono": observed, "spawn_age_s": age,
1615:                                                   "spawn_age_bound_s": bound, "remaining_main_runtime_s": remaining}
1616:             late = age > bound or remaining <= 0
1617:             if age > bound:
1618:                 self.fail("GO_COMPLETION_AGE_EXCEEDED", self.rec["go_delivery_completion"])
1619:             if remaining <= 0:
1620:                 self.fail("GO_COMPLETION_MAIN_RUNTIME_EXPIRED", self.rec["go_delivery_completion"])
1621:                 self.enforce_runtime("actual_GO_completion")
1622:             if late:
1623:                 raise GuardError("LATE_ACTUAL_GO_DELIVERY_OWNED_STOP")
1624:         dl.check("after " + phase + " completion")
1625:         if set(signal.sigpending()) & CANCEL_SIGNALS or (CANCEL["signal"] is not None and not self.cancel_handled):
1626:             raise GuardError("CANCEL_DURING_" + phase + "_COMPLETION")
1627:         epoch, mono = time.time(), time.monotonic()
1628:         if self.authorization is None or epoch > self.authorization["not_after_epoch"]:
1629:             raise GuardError("AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRED_DURING_" + phase)
1630:         if epoch > self.fit_epoch or mono > self.fit_mono:
1631:             raise GuardError("FIT_EXPIRED_DURING_" + phase)
1632:         self.stability_age(mono)
1633: 
1634:     def sample(self, obj):
1635:         if self.samples_dead or self.samples_fd is None:
1636:             return
1637:         data = bytes_json(obj)
1638:         if self.sample_bytes + len(data) > self.cfg["samples_cap_bytes"]:
1639:             self.samples_dead = True
1640:             self.fail("SAMPLES_CAP")
1641:             return
1642:         try:
1643:             write_all(self.samples_fd, data, self.deadline(10))
1644:             self.sample_hash.update(data)
1645:             self.sample_count += 1
1646:             self.sample_bytes += len(data)
1647:         except OSError as e:
1648:             self.samples_dead = True
1649:             self.fail("SAMPLES_WRITE_FAILED", e)
1650: 
1651:     def cheap_clear(self):
1652:         return self.rc is not None and len(self.streams) == 2 and all(v["closed"] and v["error"] is None for v in self.streams.values()) and len(self.threads) == 2 and all(not t.is_alive() for t in self.threads) and all(h["settled"] for h in self.helpers)
1653: 
1654:     def clearance(self):
1655:         self.reap()
1656:         if not self.cheap_clear():
1657:             return False
1658:         c = self.full_census(self.deadline(CENSUS_S))
1659:         self.limits(c)
1660:         if not c["complete"] or c["owned_live"] or c["unproved_live"] or c["group_live"] or not self.cheap_clear():
1661:             return False
1662:         # ALL predicates have now been verified. Do not use the earlier census timestamp as clearance time.
1663:         now = time.monotonic()
1664:         for name, cut in self.cutoffs():
1665:             if now > cut:
1666:                 self.fail(name, "all-clear verified after cutoff")
1667:                 self.loop_status = name
1668:                 return True
1669:         self.clear = {"mono_all_predicates_verified": now, "census": c}
1670:         self.loop_status = "EXITED"
1671:         return True
1672: 
1673: 
1674: def sink(sup, name, pipe):
1675:     path, cap = sup.cfg[name], sup.cfg[name + "_cap_bytes"]
1676:     fd, base = sup.location(path)
1677:     out, ident = create_file(fd, base)
1678:     sup.created[path] = ident
1679:     rec = {"bytes_seen": 0, "bytes_written": 0, "overflow": False, "closed": False, "error": None, "sha256_written": None}
1680:     sup.streams[name] = rec
1681: 
1682:     def run():
1683:         h = hashlib.sha256()
1684:         try:
1685:             while True:
1686:                 chunk = pipe.read1(65536)
1687:                 if not chunk:
1688:                     break
1689:                 rec["bytes_seen"] += len(chunk)
1690:                 room = max(0, cap - rec["bytes_written"])
1691:                 if room:
1692:                     b = chunk[:room]
1693:                     write_all(out, b, sup.deadline(IO_S))
1694:                     h.update(b)
1695:                     rec["bytes_written"] += len(b)
1696:                 if len(chunk) > room:
1697:                     rec["overflow"] = True
1698:         except BaseException as e:
1699:             rec["error"] = repr(e)
1700:         finally:
1701:             dl = sup.deadline(IO_S)
1702:             errors = []
1703:             for label, action in (("fsync", lambda: os.fsync(out)), ("close", lambda: os.close(out)), ("pipe_close", pipe.close)):
1704:                 try:
1705:                     action()
1706:                     dl.check("sink " + label)
1707:                 except BaseException as e:
1708:                     errors.append(repr(e))
1709:             if errors:
1710:                 rec["error"] = (rec["error"] or "") + ";".join(errors)
1711:             rec["sha256_written"] = h.hexdigest()
1712:             rec["mono_close_completed"] = time.monotonic()
1713:             rec["closed"] = rec["error"] is None
1714:     # Every sink permanently inherits blocked cancellation signals; only the main thread receives them.
1715:     with protected():
1716:         t = threading.Thread(target=run, name="capture-" + name, daemon=True)
1717:         t.start()
1718:     return t
1719: 
1720: 
1721: def admission(s):
1722:     dl = Deadline(90, s.fit_mono)
1723:     reasons, rec = [], {}
1724:     try:
1725:         s.verify_anchors(dl)
1726:         for root, (fd, ident) in s.root_fds.items():
1727:             if list_directory(fd, dl):
1728:                 raise GuardError("OUTPUT_ROOT_NOT_EMPTY " + root)
1729:         for pin in s.cfg["pins"]:
1730:             if read_file(pin["path"], PIN_CAP, dl)[0]["sha256"] != pin["sha256"]:
1731:                 raise GuardError("PIN_MISMATCH " + pin["path"])
1732:         rec["authorization"] = s.authorization_io(dl)
1733:         rec["date"] = s.date_now("admission")
1734:         if rec["date"] is None:
1735:             raise GuardError("DATE_HELPER_UNAVAILABLE")
1736:         a = s.accounting(dl)
1737:         rec["resources"] = a
1738:         res = s.cfg["admission"]["reservation_bytes"]
1739:         if a["free"] < s.cfg["launch_free_min_bytes"] + res or a["free"] - res - s.cfg["admission"]["outstanding_reservations_bytes"] < POLICY["free"]:
1740:             raise GuardError("LAUNCH_FREE_FAILED")
1741:         if a["lane_bytes"] + res + s.cfg["admission"]["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"] > s.cfg["lane_disk_ceiling_bytes"]:
1742:             raise GuardError("LANE_CEILING_AT_ADMISSION")
1743:         rec["censuses"] = []
1744:         for n in range(2):
1745:             c = s.full_census(dl)
1746:             rec["censuses"].append(c)
1747:             if not c["complete"] or c["owned_live"] or c["unproved_live"] or not s.limits(c, True) or s.failures:
1748:                 raise GuardError("ADMISSION_CENSUS_INCOMPLETE_OWNERSHIP_OR_LIMITS")
1749:             if n == 0:
1750:                 time.sleep(2)
1751:         dl.check("after admission")
1752:         with protected():
1753:             rec["checked"] = s.time_commit("ADMISSION", s.cfg["max_wall_s"] + s.cfg["settle_s"] + FINALIZE_S)
1754:     except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as e:
1755:         reasons.append(str(e))
1756:         if CANCEL["signal"] is not None:
1757:             s.cancel_handled = True
1758:     rec["reasons"] = reasons
1759:     return rec
1760: 
1761: 
1762: def launch_and_go(s):
1763:     s.phase = "PRE_GO"
1764:     s.rec["start_date"] = s.date_now("start")
1765:     if s.rec["start_date"] is None or s.failures:
1766:         raise GuardError("START_DATE_UNAVAILABLE")
1767:     dl = s.deadline(IO_S)
1768:     fd, base = s.location(s.cfg["samples"])
1769:     s.samples_fd, ident = create_file(fd, base)
1770:     s.created[s.cfg["samples"]] = ident
1771:     s.authorization_io(dl)
1772:     c = s.full_census(dl)
1773:     s.rec["launch_census"] = c
1774:     if not c["complete"] or c["owned_live"] or c["unproved_live"] or not s.limits(c, True) or s.failures:
1775:         raise GuardError("LAUNCH_CENSUS_INCOMPLETE_OWNERSHIP_OR_LIMITS")
1776:     a = s.accounting(dl)
1777:     if a["free"] < s.cfg["launch_free_min_bytes"] + s.cfg["admission"]["reservation_bytes"] or not s.reservation_fits(a):
1778:         raise GuardError("LAUNCH_RESOURCES_FAILED")
1779:     s.resource_age(c)
1780:     dl.check("after launch preparation")
1781:     child = spawn_registered(s, s.cfg["argv"], dict(s.cfg["env"]), "child", dl,
1782:                              s.cfg["max_wall_s"] + s.cfg["settle_s"] + FINALIZE_S)
1783:     s.rec["launch_commit"] = child["launch_commit"]
1784:     try:
1785:         initial = identify(s.pid)
1786:         s.rec["main_initial_identity_observed"] = dict(initial)
1787:         s.rec["mono_main_initial_identity_observed"] = time.monotonic()
1788:         if initial["pgid"] != s.pid or initial["euid"] != MY_UID or initial["ruid"] != MY_UID:
1789:             raise GuardError("CHILD_IDENTITY_OR_GROUP_INVALID")
1790:         s.ident, s.group = initial, initial["pgid"]  # Safe cleanup state precedes any fallible ledger retention.
1791:         s.retain_identity(initial, "main_initial", True)
1792:         s.register_owned(initial, "child")
1793:         if child["spawn_error"] is not None:
1794:             raise GuardError(child["spawn_error"])
1795:         for name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
1796:             pipe = os.fdopen(child[name + "_fd"], "rb", buffering=65536)
1797:             child[name + "_fd"] = None
1798:             child[name] = pipe
1799:             s.threads.append(sink(s, name, pipe))
1800:         if CANCEL["signal"] is not None:
1801:             s.cancel_handled = True
1802:             s.trip("SUPERVISOR_CANCEL", CANCEL["signal"])
1803:         launch = {"supervisor": VERSION, "supervisor_sha256": s.self_sha, "config_sha256": s.config_sha,
1804:                   "supervisor_pid": MY_PID, "identity": s.ident, "argv": s.cfg["argv"], "cwd": s.cfg["lane"],
1805:                   "env": s.cfg["env"], "mono_spawn": s.mono_spawn, "epoch_spawn": s.epoch_spawn,
1806:                   "start_date": s.rec["start_date"], "baseline_count": len(s.baseline),
1807:                   "authorization": s.authorization, "launch_commit": s.rec["launch_commit"],
1808:                   "cpu_slots": s.cfg["cpu_slots"], "reservation_bytes": s.cfg["admission"]["reservation_bytes"]}
1809:         fd, name = s.location(s.cfg["launch_record"])
1810:         launch_sha, launch_inode = publish(fd, name, bytes_json(launch), s.deadline(IO_S))
1811:         s.rec["launch_record_sha256"] = launch_sha
1812:         s.created[s.cfg["launch_record"]] = launch_inode
1813:         gd = s.deadline(IO_S)
1814:         c = s.full_census(gd)
1815:         s.rec["pre_go_census"] = c
1816:         live = [p["uniqueid"] for p in c["owned_live"]]
1817:         if not c["complete"] or c["unproved_live"] or live != [s.ident["uniqueid"]] or not s.limits(c, True):
1818:             raise GuardError("GO_CENSUS_INCOMPLETE_OWNERSHIP_OR_LIMITS")
1819:         a = s.accounting(gd)
1820:         if a["free"] < s.cfg["launch_free_min_bytes"] + s.cfg["admission"]["reservation_bytes"] or not s.reservation_fits(a):
1821:             raise GuardError("GO_RESOURCES_FAILED")
1822:         gd.check("after GO preparation")
1823:         with protected():
1824:             creation_line = s.creation_manifest(gd)
1825:             s.authorization_io(gd)  # Last I/O, after census/accounting, before commit clocks.
1826:             gd.check("after GO authorization read")
1827:             s.resource_age(c)
1828:             remaining = max(0, s.mono_spawn + s.cfg["max_wall_s"] - time.monotonic())
1829:             spawn_age = time.monotonic() - s.mono_spawn
1830:             if spawn_age > min(60.0, s.cfg["max_wall_s"] / 2.0):
1831:                 raise GuardError("SPAWN_TO_GO_AGE_EXCEEDED")
1832:             if remaining <= 0 or s.stop is not None or s.failures:
1833:                 raise GuardError("GO_STOP_OR_DEADLINE")
1834:             s.rec["go_commit"] = s.time_commit("GO", remaining + s.cfg["settle_s"] + FINALIZE_S)
1835:             s.rec["go_write_attempted"] = True
1836:             write_all(child["stdin_fd"], b"GO\n", gd)
1837:             if creation_line:
1838:                 write_all(child["stdin_fd"], creation_line, gd)
1839:             s.rec["go_written_mono"] = time.monotonic()
1840:             os.close(child["stdin_fd"])
1841:             child["stdin_fd"] = None
1842:             s.rec["go_delivered"] = True  # Bytes were delivered; a later completion failure is not hidden.
1843:             s.completion_check("GO", gd)
1844:             s.resource_age(c)
1845:     except BaseException as e:
1846:         s.fail("POST_SPAWN_FAILURE", repr(e))
1847:     finally:
1848:         if child.get("stdin_fd") is not None:
1849:             try:
1850:                 os.close(child["stdin_fd"])
1851:                 child["stdin_fd"] = None
1852:             except OSError as e:
1853:                 s.fail("STDIN_CLOSE_FAILED", repr(e))
1854:     if not s.rec.get("go_delivered"):
1855:         s.fail("GO_NOT_DELIVERED")
1856:     s.phase = "RUNNING"
1857:     observe(s)
1858: 
1859: 
1860: def observe(s):
1861:     s.loop_status = "RUNNING"
1862:     while True:
1863:         try:
1864:             s.reap()
1865:             if CANCEL["signal"] is not None:
1866:                 s.cancel_handled = True
1867:                 s.trip("SUPERVISOR_CANCEL", CANCEL["signal"])
1868:             for name, cut in s.cutoffs():
1869:                 if time.monotonic() > cut:
1870:                     s.fail(name)
1871:                     s.loop_status = name
1872:                     return
1873:             dl = s.deadline(IO_S)
1874:             c = s.census(dl)
1875:             s.limits(c)
1876:             a = s.accounting(dl)
1877:             s.disk_trips(a)
1878:             for name, v in s.streams.items():
1879:                 if v["error"] is not None:
1880:                     s.fail("STREAM_ERROR_" + name, v["error"])
1881:             s.reap()
1882:             if s.rc is None and time.monotonic() >= s.mono_spawn + s.cfg["max_wall_s"]:
1883:                 s.trip("DEADLINE")
1884:             s.sample({"mono": time.monotonic(), "epoch": time.time(), "rc": s.rc, "census": c,
1885:                       "resources": a, "streams": {k: dict(v) for k, v in s.streams.items()},
1886:                       "stop": None if s.stop is None else s.stop["reason"], "failure_codes": sorted(s.failure_keys),
1887:                       "trips": list(s.trips)})
1888:             if s.clearance():
1889:                 return
1890:         except BaseException as e:
1891:             s.fail("OBSERVATION_FAILED", repr(e))
1892:             s.enforce_runtime("observation_exception_after_native_return")
1893:         for name, cut in s.cutoffs():
1894:             if time.monotonic() > cut:
1895:                 s.fail(name)
1896:                 s.loop_status = name
1897:                 return
1898:         remaining = [cut - time.monotonic() for _, cut in s.cutoffs()]
1899:         time.sleep(max(0, min([s.cfg["sample_s"]] + remaining)))
1900: 
1901: 
1902: def exception_code(e):
1903:     if isinstance(e, GuardError) and e.args and isinstance(e.args[0], str):
1904:         return e.args[0].split(" ", 1)[0]
1905:     if isinstance(e, OSError):
1906:         return "OS_ERROR_%s" % e.errno
1907:     return type(e).__name__
1908: 
1909: 
1910: def refuse(s, code, reason, extra=None):
1911:     fields = dict(extra or {})
1912:     fields["reconciliation"] = s.reconciliation_evidence()
1913:     return unsuccessful(code, reason, fields)
1914: 
1915: 
1916: def unsuccessful(code, reason, extra=None):
1917:     obj = {"supervisor": VERSION, "acknowledged": False, "exit_code": code, "reason": reason,
1918:            "clock_note": "raw monotonic/epoch telemetry only"}
1919:     if extra:
1920:         obj.update(extra)
1921:     sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(obj, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
1922:     sys.stdout.flush()
1923:     return code
1924: 
1925: 
1926: def finalize(s):
1927:     phase = Deadline(FINALIZE_S, s.fit_mono)
1928:     try:
1929:         return final_body(s, phase)
1930:     except BaseException as e:
1931:         if s.ack_output_started:
1932:             return 14  # A begun ACK never falls back to a second stdout summary, even if stderr reporting failed.
1933:         sys.stderr.write("FINALIZATION_FAILED %r\n" % e)
1934:         return refuse(s, 13, "FINALIZATION_FAILED", {"config_sha256": s.config_sha, "error": repr(e), "error_code": exception_code(e)})
1935: 
1936: 
1937: def final_body(s, phase):
1938:     s.phase = "FINAL"
1939:     s.rec["end_date"] = s.date_now("end")
1940:     s.refresh_helpers(Deadline(CENSUS_S, phase.end))
1941:     s.reap()
1942:     c = s.full_census(Deadline(CENSUS_S, phase.end))
1943:     s.rec["final_census"] = c
1944:     if not c["complete"]:
1945:         s.fail("FINAL_CENSUS_INCOMPLETE", c["incomplete"])
1946:     if c["owned_live"]:
1947:         s.fail("OWNED_LIVE", c["owned_live"])
1948:     if c["unproved_live"]:
1949:         s.fail("UNPROVED_LIVE", c["unproved_live"])

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Rebound qualification child, source only; no execution authority.
3: 
4: The 22 writer-selected controls retain their workloads and thresholds. busy2 alone
5: spins two processes, requires its own ownership-negative GO and reserves two CPU
6: slots at threshold 1; no busy3 exists. Other forking controls are ownership negatives,
7: not science. All processes inherit the SAME module-entry monotonic life deadline.
8: Every reap is WNOHANG polling within that absolute lifetime, with time reserved for
9: failure evidence. An unresolved join is recorded and fails; it never escalates.
10: 
11: Ordinary stdin is EXACTLY GO\\n then EOF. Only the two mutation negatives accept
12: one additional canonical, at-most-4096-byte creation JSON line then EOF. That line
13: binds approved preparation and ORIGINAL created directory/target inodes. Mutation
14: opens no-create/no-follow, verifies original inode/hash/size/nlink before appending
15: one byte, and reports the exact line digest INCLUDING its newline. External parent
16: approval, not circular static child/writer/checker hashes, is the trust root.
17: 
18: Incarnation fields and libproc ABI match the frozen supervisor. Identity reads are
19: bracketed; unavailable identities stay null with actual error evidence. Child reports
20: do not themselves establish supervisor ownership or release any allocation. Parent
21: must join every relevant incarnation to retained supervisor evidence and independently
22: prove all known/reported/unproved identities ended. Mandatory owned /bin/date, complete
23: mixed-UID/known/unproved fail-closed coverage and correction fabe57ab...4155a2 remain.

62: def check_deadline(deadline, operation):
63:     if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
64:         raise OSError("COOPERATIVE_DEADLINE " + operation)

67: def integer(value):
68:     return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
69:

71: def hex64(value):
72:     return isinstance(value, str) and len(value) == 64 and all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in value)

75: def pairs(items):
76:     obj = {}
77:     for key, value in items:
78:         if key in obj:
79:             raise ValueError("duplicate JSON key %r" % key)
80:         obj[key] = value
81:     return obj

84: def json_float(text):
85:     value = float(text)
86:     if not math.isfinite(value):
87:         raise ValueError("nonfinite JSON number")
88:     return value
89: 
90: 
91: def json_constant(text):
92:     raise ValueError("nonfinite JSON constant " + text)

95: def parse_json(raw):
96:     return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"), object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_float=json_float,
97:                       parse_constant=json_constant)

100: def json_line(obj):
101:     return (json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()

104: def complete_write(fd, data, deadline):
105:     old = os.get_blocking(fd)
106:     try:
107:         os.set_blocking(fd, False)
108:         view = memoryview(data)
109:         while view:
110:             check_deadline(deadline, "before write")
111:             try:
112:                 n = os.write(fd, view)
113:             except BlockingIOError:
114:                 select.select([], [fd], [], max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
115:                 continue
116:             if n <= 0:
117:                 raise OSError("zero-progress write")
118:             view = view[n:]
119:         check_deadline(deadline, "after write")
120:     finally:
121:         os.set_blocking(fd, old)
122: 
123: 
124: def read_pipe(fd, cap, deadline):
125:     """Read through EOF, never accept a prefix merely because a newline arrived."""
126:     proof = {"mono_start": time.monotonic(), "deadline_mono": deadline, "eof": False, "mono_eof": None,
127:              "bytes": 0, "sha256": None, "error": None}
128:     raw = bytearray()
129:     old = os.get_blocking(fd)
130:     try:
131:         os.set_blocking(fd, False)
132:         while True:
133:             check_deadline(deadline, "pipe read")
134:             ready, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
135:             if not ready:
136:                 check_deadline(deadline, "pipe readiness")
137:                 continue
138:             try:
139:                 part = os.read(fd, min(4096, cap + 1 - len(raw)))
140:             except BlockingIOError:
141:                 continue
142:             if not part:
143:                 proof.update(eof=True, mono_eof=time.monotonic())
144:                 check_deadline(deadline, "pipe EOF")
145:                 break
146:             raw.extend(part)
147:             if len(raw) > cap:
148:                 raise ValueError("pipe byte cap exceeded")
149:     except Exception as error:
150:         proof["error"] = repr(error)
151:     finally:
152:         os.set_blocking(fd, old)
153:     proof.update(bytes=len(raw), sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), mono_end=time.monotonic())
154:     return bytes(raw), proof
155: 
156: 
157: def wait_go(name, deadline):
158:     mutation = name in MUTATIONS
159:     raw, proof = read_pipe(0, 4099 if mutation else 3, deadline)
160:     protocol = dict(proof, creation_line_bytes=None, creation_manifest_sha256=None)
161:     if proof["error"] is not None or not proof["eof"]:
162:         raise ValueError("GO_READ_REFUSED " + json.dumps(protocol, sort_keys=True))
163:     if not mutation:
164:         if raw != b"GO\n":
165:             raise ValueError("ORDINARY_GO_NOT_EXACT " + json.dumps(protocol, sort_keys=True))
166:         return protocol, None
167:     line = raw[3:]
168:     if raw[:3] != b"GO\n" or not 1 <= len(line) <= 4096 or not line.endswith(b"\n") or line.count(b"\n") != 1:
169:         raise ValueError("MUTATION_GO_NOT_EXACT " + json.dumps(protocol, sort_keys=True))
170:     manifest = parse_json(line)
171:     if json_line(manifest) != line:
172:         raise ValueError("CREATION_LINE_NOT_CANONICAL")
173:     protocol.update(creation_line_bytes=len(line), creation_manifest_sha256=hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest())
174:     return protocol, manifest
