177: def directory(path, deadline):
178:     fd = os.open("/", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
179:     try:
180:         for component in path.lstrip("/").split("/"):
181:             check_deadline(deadline, "directory traversal")
182:             if component in ("", ".", ".."):
183:                 raise ValueError("invalid directory component")
184:             nxt = os.open(component, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
185:             os.close(fd)
186:             fd = nxt
187:         check_deadline(deadline, "directory completion")
188:         return fd
189:     except BaseException:
190:         os.close(fd)
191:         raise
192: 
193: 
194: def inode(fd):
195:     value = os.fstat(fd)
196:     return [value.st_dev, value.st_ino]
197: 
198: 
199: def create_json(outfd, name, obj, deadline):
200:     data = json_line(obj)
201:     if len(data) > 1 << 18:
202:         raise ValueError("child evidence byte cap")
203:     check_deadline(deadline, "before exclusive child output")
204:     fd = os.open(name, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, 0o644, dir_fd=outfd)
205:     try:
206:         complete_write(fd, data, deadline)
207:         os.fsync(fd)
208:         check_deadline(deadline, "after child output fsync")
209:     finally:
210:         os.close(fd)
211:     check_deadline(deadline, "after child output close")
212: 
213: 
214: def pidinfo(pid, flavor, size, deadline):
215:     check_deadline(deadline, "before proc_pidinfo")
216:     buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(size)
217:     ctypes.set_errno(0)
218:     got = LP.proc_pidinfo(pid, flavor, 1 if flavor in (13, 17) else 0, buf, size)
219:     error = ctypes.get_errno()
220:     check_deadline(deadline, "after proc_pidinfo")
221:     if got != size:
222:         raise OSError(error or errno.EIO, "PIDINFO %d flavor %d bytes %d" % (pid, flavor, got))
223:     return buf.raw
224: 
225: 
226: def unique(pid, deadline):
227:     raw = pidinfo(pid, 17, 56, deadline)
228:     return [struct.unpack_from("=Q", raw, 16)[0], struct.unpack_from("=i", raw, 32)[0]]
229: 
230: 
231: def short(pid, deadline):
232:     raw = pidinfo(pid, 13, 64, deadline)
233:     p, pp, pg, status = struct.unpack_from("=IIII", raw)
234:     flags, uid = struct.unpack_from("=II", raw, 32)
235:     return {"pid": p, "ppid": pp, "pgid": pg, "status": status, "euid": uid,
236:             "ruid": struct.unpack_from("=I", raw, 44)[0], "traced": bool(flags & 2),
237:             "comm": raw[16:32].split(b"\0")[0].decode("utf-8", "replace")}
238: 
239: 
240: def fingerprint(identity):
241:     return tuple(identity[key] for key in IDENTITY_KEYS)
242: 
243: 
244: def observe(pid, role, phase, deadline):
245:     """Keep partial raw brackets even when identification fails; never fill missing identity fields."""
246:     record = {"role": role, "phase": phase, "observer": os.getpid(), "pid": pid,
247:               "mono_start": time.monotonic(), "observed_mono": None, "identity": None, "error": None, "bracket": {}}
248:     bracket = record["bracket"]
249:     try:
250:         bracket["unique_before"] = unique(pid, deadline)
251:         bracket["short_before"] = short(pid, deadline)
252:         bracket["unique_middle"] = unique(pid, deadline)
253:         bracket["short_after"] = short(pid, deadline)
254:         bracket["unique_after"] = unique(pid, deadline)
255:         if not bracket["unique_before"] == bracket["unique_middle"] == bracket["unique_after"]:
256:             raise ValueError("IDENTITY_CHANGED %d" % pid)
257:         a, b = dict(bracket["short_before"]), dict(bracket["short_after"])
258:         for item in (a, b):
259:             item.update(uniqueid=bracket["unique_before"][0], idversion=bracket["unique_before"][1])
260:         if a["pid"] != pid or b["pid"] != pid or fingerprint(a) != fingerprint(b):
261:             raise ValueError("IDENTITY_METADATA_CHANGED %d" % pid)
262:         record["identity"] = b
263:     except Exception as error:
264:         record["error"] = repr(error)
265:     record["observed_mono"] = time.monotonic()
266:     return record
267: 
268: 
269: def edge_observation(pid, role, phase, deadline):
270:     parent_before = observe(os.getpid(), "main" if role == "descendant" else "intermediate", phase + "_parent_before", deadline)
271:     child = observe(pid, role, phase, deadline)
272:     parent_after = observe(os.getpid(), parent_before["role"], phase + "_parent_after", deadline)
273:     proof = {"phase": phase, "parent_before": parent_before, "child": child, "parent_after": parent_after,
274:              "current_parent_verified": False, "same_group_verified": False, "error": None}
275:     a, b, c = parent_before["identity"], child["identity"], parent_after["identity"]
276:     if a is None or b is None or c is None:
277:         proof["error"] = "CURRENT_PARENT_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE"
278:     elif fingerprint(a) != fingerprint(c) or b["ppid"] != a["pid"]:
279:         proof["error"] = "CURRENT_PARENT_EDGE_CHANGED"
280:     else:
281:         proof.update(current_parent_verified=True, same_group_verified=b["pgid"] == a["pgid"])
282:     return child, proof
283: 
284: 
285: def sleep_to(deadline):
286:     while True:
287:         remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
288:         if remaining <= 0:
289:             return
290:         time.sleep(min(0.05, remaining))
291: 
292: 
293: def spin_to(deadline):
294:     n = 0
295:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
296:         n += 1
297: 
298: 
299: def wait_reap(pid, role, deadline, life_deadline):
300:     report = {"role": role, "observer": os.getpid(), "pid": pid, "deadline_mono": deadline,
301:               "mono_life_deadline": life_deadline, "mono_start": time.monotonic(), "mono_end": None,
302:               "wait_options": ["WNOHANG"], "polls": 0, "interruptions": 0, "resolved": False,
303:               "wait_status": None, "exit_code": None, "mono_reaped": None, "error": None,
304:               "unresolved_observation": None}
305:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
306:         report["polls"] += 1
307:         try:
308:             found, status = os.waitpid(pid, os.WNOHANG)
309:         except InterruptedError:
310:             report["interruptions"] += 1
311:             continue
312:         except Exception as error:
313:             report["error"] = repr(error)
314:             break
315:         if found:
316:             report.update(resolved=True, wait_status=status, exit_code=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status),
317:                           mono_reaped=time.monotonic())
318:             if report["mono_reaped"] >= deadline:
319:                 report["error"] = "WAIT_COMPLETED_AFTER_ABSOLUTE_DEADLINE"
320:             break
321:         time.sleep(min(POLL_S, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
322:     if not report["resolved"]:
323:         if report["error"] is None:
324:             report["error"] = "WAIT_UNRESOLVED_AT_ABSOLUTE_DEADLINE"
325:         report["unresolved_observation"] = observe(pid, role, "unresolved_join", life_deadline)
326:     report["mono_end"] = time.monotonic()
327:     return report
328: 
329: 
330: def join_ok(report):
331:     return report["resolved"] and report["error"] is None and report["exit_code"] == 0
332: 
333: 
334: def fork_body(body, deadline):
335:     pid = os.fork()
336:     if pid == 0:
337:         try:
338:             body()
339:         except BaseException as error:
340:             try:
341:                 complete_write(2, ("DESCENDANT_ERROR %r\n" % error).encode(), deadline)
342:             except BaseException:
343:                 pass
344:             os._exit(1)
345:         os._exit(0)
346:     return pid
347: 
348: 
349: def identity_wait(pid, role, phase, deadline, predicate):
350:     proof = {"role": role, "pid": pid, "phase": phase, "observer": os.getpid(), "deadline_mono": deadline,
351:              "mono_start": time.monotonic(), "mono_end": None, "attempts": 0, "first_observation": None,
352:              "last_observation": None, "errors": [], "matched": False}
353:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
354:         item = observe(pid, role, phase, deadline)
355:         proof["attempts"] += 1
356:         if proof["first_observation"] is None:
357:             proof["first_observation"] = item
358:         proof["last_observation"] = item
359:         if item["error"] is not None:
360:             proof["errors"].append(item)
361:             if len(proof["errors"]) >= 8:
362:                 break  # Fail on a bounded evidence cap, never silently discard failed reads.
363:         elif predicate(item["identity"]):
364:             proof["matched"] = True
365:             break
366:         time.sleep(min(POLL_S, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
367:     proof["mono_end"] = time.monotonic()
368:     return proof
369: 
370: 
371: def stat_signature(value):
372:     return (value.st_dev, value.st_ino, value.st_size, value.st_nlink, value.st_mtime_ns, value.st_ctime_ns)
373: 
374: 
375: def snapshot_fd(fd, path, cap, deadline):
376:     check_deadline(deadline, "before regular snapshot")
377:     before = os.fstat(fd)
378:     if not stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) or before.st_nlink != 1 or not 0 <= before.st_size <= cap:
379:         raise ValueError("not a bounded single-link regular file")
380:     raw = bytearray()
381:     while len(raw) < before.st_size:
382:         check_deadline(deadline, "regular snapshot read")
383:         part = os.pread(fd, min(65536, before.st_size - len(raw)), len(raw))
384:         if not part:
385:             raise OSError("short regular snapshot")
386:         raw.extend(part)
387:     if os.pread(fd, 1, before.st_size) or stat_signature(os.fstat(fd)) != stat_signature(before):
388:         raise ValueError("regular file changed during snapshot")
389:     check_deadline(deadline, "after regular snapshot")
390:     return {"path": path, "inode": [before.st_dev, before.st_ino], "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
391:             "bytes": len(raw), "nlink": before.st_nlink}, bytes(raw)
392: 
393: 
394: def read_regular_at(rootfd, name, path, deadline):
395:     fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=rootfd)
396:     try:
397:         facts, raw = snapshot_fd(fd, path, SMALL, deadline)
398:         named = os.stat(name, dir_fd=rootfd, follow_symlinks=False)
399:         if [named.st_dev, named.st_ino] != facts["inode"] or not stat.S_ISREG(named.st_mode) or named.st_nlink != 1:
400:             raise ValueError("regular pathname replaced during read")
401:         return facts, raw
402:     finally:
403:         os.close(fd)
404: 
405: 
406: def anchor_schema(value, path):
407:     return (isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {"path", "inode"} and value["path"] == path and
408:             isinstance(value["inode"], list) and len(value["inode"]) == 2 and
409:             all(integer(v) and v >= 0 for v in value["inode"]))
410: 
411: 
412: def target_schema(value, path):
413:     return (isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {"path", "inode", "sha256", "bytes", "nlink"} and
414:             anchor_schema({"path": value["path"], "inode": value["inode"]}, path) and hex64(value["sha256"]) and
415:             integer(value["bytes"]) and 0 < value["bytes"] <= SMALL and integer(value["nlink"]) and value["nlink"] == 1)
416: 
417: 
418: def verify_anchors(run, rfd, ofd, expected_run, expected_out, deadline):
419:     fresh = directory(run, deadline)
420:     fresh_out = None
421:     try:
422:         fresh_out = os.open("out", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fresh)
423:         actual_run, actual_out = {"path": run, "inode": inode(rfd)}, {"path": run + "/out", "inode": inode(ofd)}
424:         if actual_run != expected_run or actual_out != expected_out or inode(fresh) != expected_run["inode"] or \
425:                 inode(fresh_out) != expected_out["inode"] or actual_run["inode"][0] != actual_out["inode"][0]:
426:             raise ValueError("ORIGINAL_APPROVED_DIRECTORY_ANCHOR_CHANGED")
427:         check_deadline(deadline, "anchor verification")
428:         return actual_run, actual_out
429:     finally:
430:         if fresh_out is not None:
431:             os.close(fresh_out)
432:         os.close(fresh)
433: 
434: 
435: def mutate_original(name, run, rfd, ofd, manifest, protocol, deadline):
436:     keys = {"kind", "scenario", "preparation_sha256", "run", "output_root", "target"}
437:     target_path = run + ("/pinned-scratch.txt" if name == "mutate_pin" else "/out/launch.json")
438:     if not isinstance(manifest, dict) or set(manifest) != keys or manifest["kind"] != "qualification-creation-manifest-v1" or \
439:             manifest["scenario"] != name or not hex64(manifest["preparation_sha256"]) or \
440:             not anchor_schema(manifest["run"], run) or not anchor_schema(manifest["output_root"], run + "/out") or \
441:             not target_schema(manifest["target"], target_path):
442:         raise ValueError("CREATION_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_OR_FIXED_PATH")
443:     actual_run, actual_out = verify_anchors(run, rfd, ofd, manifest["run"], manifest["output_root"], deadline)
444:     prep_facts, prep_raw = read_regular_at(rfd, "preparation.json", run + "/preparation.json", deadline)
445:     if prep_facts["sha256"] != manifest["preparation_sha256"]:
446:         raise ValueError("APPROVED_PREPARATION_SHA256_MISMATCH")
447:     prep = parse_json(prep_raw)
448:     prep_keys = {"kind", "scenario", "run", "output_root", "fixture", "source_pins", "python", "allocation", "negative_control"}
449:     if not isinstance(prep, dict) or set(prep) != prep_keys or prep["kind"] != "qualification-creation-preparation-v1" or \
450:             prep["scenario"] != name or prep["negative_control"] is not True or prep["run"] != manifest["run"] or \
451:             prep["output_root"] != manifest["output_root"]:
452:         raise ValueError("APPROVED_PREPARATION_SEMANTICS")
453:     pins = prep["source_pins"]
454:     if not isinstance(pins, dict) or set(pins) != {"supervisor", "child", "writer", "checker"} or any(
455:             not isinstance(v, dict) or set(v) != {"path", "sha256"} or not isinstance(v["path"], str) or
456:             not v["path"].startswith("/") or os.path.normpath(v["path"]) != v["path"] or not hex64(v["sha256"])
457:             for v in pins.values()) or any(pins[k]["path"] != path for k, path in SOURCE_PATHS.items()):
458:         raise ValueError("APPROVED_PREPARATION_SOURCE_PIN_METADATA")
459:     python, allocation = prep["python"], prep["allocation"]
460:     if not isinstance(python, dict) or set(python) != {"path", "sha256"} or not isinstance(python["path"], str) or \
461:             not python["path"].endswith("/Resources/Python.app/Contents/MacOS/Python") or \
462:             os.path.realpath(python["path"]) != python["path"] or python["path"] != os.path.realpath(sys.executable) or \
463:             not hex64(python["sha256"]):
464:         raise ValueError("APPROVED_PREPARATION_ACTUAL_PYTHON_APP")
465:     allocation_keys = {"bytes", "cpu_slots", "outstanding_reservations_bytes", "lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"}
466:     if not isinstance(allocation, dict) or set(allocation) != allocation_keys or any(
467:             not integer(v) or not 0 <= v <= 1 << 60 for v in allocation.values()) or \
468:             allocation["bytes"] != 20971520 or allocation["cpu_slots"] != 1:
469:         raise ValueError("APPROVED_PREPARATION_ALLOCATION")
470:     target = manifest["target"]
471:     if name == "mutate_pin":
472:         if prep["fixture"] != target or target["sha256"] != hashlib.sha256(FIXTURE).hexdigest() or target["bytes"] != len(FIXTURE):
473:             raise ValueError("ORIGINAL_CREATED_PIN_NOT_APPROVED_FIXTURE")
474:         rootfd, basename = rfd, "pinned-scratch.txt"
475:     else:
476:         if prep["fixture"] is not None:
477:             raise ValueError("LAUNCH_MUTATION_HAS_PIN_FIXTURE")
478:         rootfd, basename = ofd, "launch.json"
479:     # No O_CREAT, no pathname supplied by argv, no link/rename operations.
480:     fd = os.open(basename, os.O_RDWR | os.O_APPEND | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=rootfd)
481:     try:
482:         original, original_raw = snapshot_fd(fd, target_path, SMALL, deadline)
483:         if original != target or (name == "mutate_pin" and original_raw != FIXTURE):
484:             raise ValueError("ORIGINAL_CREATION_INODE_HASH_SIZE_LINK_MISMATCH")
485:         verify_anchors(run, rfd, ofd, manifest["run"], manifest["output_root"], deadline)
486:         named = os.stat(basename, dir_fd=rootfd, follow_symlinks=False)
487:         named_before = [named.st_dev, named.st_ino]
488:         if named_before != original["inode"] or not stat.S_ISREG(named.st_mode) or named.st_nlink != 1 or named.st_size != original["bytes"]:
489:             raise ValueError("ORIGINAL_TARGET_PATH_CHANGED_BEFORE_APPEND")
490:         # Re-snapshot on the SAME descriptor after anchor/name I/O and before the write.
491:         checked, checked_raw = snapshot_fd(fd, target_path, SMALL, deadline)
492:         if checked != original or checked_raw != original_raw:
493:             raise ValueError("ORIGINAL_TARGET_CHANGED_BEFORE_APPEND")
494:         mono_verified = time.monotonic()
495:         complete_write(fd, b"x", deadline)
496:         mono_append = time.monotonic()
497:         os.fsync(fd)
498:         check_deadline(deadline, "mutation fsync")
499:         mono_fsync = time.monotonic()
500:         after, after_raw = snapshot_fd(fd, target_path, SMALL + 1, deadline)
501:         named = os.stat(basename, dir_fd=rootfd, follow_symlinks=False)
502:         named_after = [named.st_dev, named.st_ino]
503:         if after["inode"] != original["inode"] or after["bytes"] != original["bytes"] + 1 or after_raw != original_raw + b"x" or \
504:                 named_after != original["inode"] or named.st_nlink != 1 or not stat.S_ISREG(named.st_mode):
505:             raise ValueError("MUTATION_POST_APPEND_FACTS_CHANGED")
506:         return {"manifest": manifest, "manifest_sha256": protocol["creation_manifest_sha256"],
507:                 "manifest_line_bytes": protocol["creation_line_bytes"], "preparation_sha256": prep_facts["sha256"],
508:                 "preparation_facts": prep_facts, "run": actual_run, "output_root": actual_out,
509:                 "original": original, "after": after, "name_inode_before": named_before, "name_inode_after": named_after,
510:                 "append_bytes": 1, "mono_verified": mono_verified, "mono_append": mono_append,
511:                 "mono_fsync_completed": mono_fsync, "mono_after_verified": time.monotonic()}
512:     finally:
513:         os.close(fd)
514: 
515: 
516: def task_observation(pid, deadline):
517:     before = observe(pid, "main", "rss_measurement_before", deadline)
518:     record = {"pid": pid, "identity_before": before, "identity_after": None, "rss_bytes": None,
519:               "mono_start": time.monotonic(), "mono_end": None, "error": None}
520:     try:
521:         if before["identity"] is None:
522:             raise ValueError("RSS_IDENTITY_BEFORE_UNAVAILABLE")
523:         raw = pidinfo(pid, 4, 96, deadline)
524:         rss = struct.unpack_from("=Q", raw, 8)[0]
525:         after = observe(pid, "main", "rss_measurement_after", deadline)
526:         record["identity_after"] = after
527:         if after["identity"] is None or fingerprint(before["identity"]) != fingerprint(after["identity"]):
528:             raise ValueError("RSS_IDENTITY_BRACKET_CHANGED")
529:         record["rss_bytes"] = rss
530:     except Exception as error:
531:         record["error"] = repr(error)
532:     record["mono_end"] = time.monotonic()
533:     return record

535: 
536: def intermediate_report(name, reportfd, deadline, inner_deadline, join_deadline, reserve):
537:     report = {"kind": "qualification-intermediate-report-v1", "scenario": name, "intermediate": os.getpid(),
538:               "grandchild": None, "descendants": [], "joins": [], "identity_waits": [], "ownership_edges": [],
539:               "transports": {}, "evidence_failures": [], "error": None}
540:     try:
541:         if name == "ownership_double":
542:             os.setsid()
543:         own = observe(os.getpid(), "intermediate", "self_post_setup", inner_deadline)
544:         report["descendants"].append(own)
545:         if own["identity"] is None:
546:             raise ValueError("INTERMEDIATE_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE")
547:         if name == "zombie_hold":
548:             def zombie():
549:                 os.close(reportfd)
550:             kid = fork_body(zombie, deadline)
551:             report["grandchild"] = kid
552:             held = identity_wait(kid, "zombie", "zombie_hold_start", inner_deadline, lambda p: p["status"] == SZOMB)
553:             report["identity_waits"].append(held)
554:             if held["last_observation"] is not None:
555:                 report["descendants"].append(held["last_observation"])
556:             if not held["matched"] or held["errors"]:
557:                 report["evidence_failures"].append("ZOMBIE_HELD_IDENTITY_NOT_PROVED")
558:             hold_start = time.monotonic()
559:             hold_until = min(hold_start + 3.0, inner_deadline - reserve)
560:             sleep_to(hold_until)
561:             end = observe(kid, "zombie", "zombie_hold_end", inner_deadline)
562:             report["descendants"].append(end)
563:             first = held["last_observation"]["identity"] if held["last_observation"] is not None else None
564:             if first is None or end["identity"] is None or end["identity"]["status"] != SZOMB or fingerprint(first) != fingerprint(end["identity"]):
565:                 report["evidence_failures"].append("ZOMBIE_HOLD_END_IDENTITY_NOT_PROVED")
566:             report["zombie_hold"] = {"mono_start": hold_start, "mono_hold_until": hold_until, "mono_end": time.monotonic()}
567:             joined = wait_reap(kid, "zombie", inner_deadline, deadline)
568:             report["joins"].append(joined)
569:             if not join_ok(joined):
570:                 report["evidence_failures"].append("ZOMBIE_JOIN_UNRESOLVED_OR_NONZERO")
571:         else:
572:             exec_read, exec_write = os.pipe()
573:             gate_read, gate_write = os.pipe()
574:             os.set_inheritable(exec_write, False)
575:             def exec_descendant():
576:                 os.close(exec_read)
577:                 os.close(gate_write)
578:                 os.close(reportfd)
579:                 try:
580:                     gate, gate_proof = read_pipe(gate_read, 1, inner_deadline)
581:                     os.close(gate_read)
582:                     if gate != b"S" or not gate_proof["eof"] or gate_proof["error"] is not None:
583:                         raise ValueError("EXEC_START_GATE_REFUSED")
584:                     os.chdir("/")
585:                     devnull = os.open("/dev/null", os.O_RDWR)
586:                     for standard in (0, 1, 2):
587:                         os.dup2(devnull, standard)
588:                     os.closerange(3, exec_write)
589:                     os.closerange(exec_write + 1, 65536)
590:                     check_deadline(deadline, "before fixed sleep exec")
591:                     remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
592:                     os.execv("/bin/sleep", ["sleep", "%.6f" % remaining])
593:                     raise RuntimeError("exec unexpectedly returned")
594:                 except BaseException as error:
595:                     try:
596:                         complete_write(exec_write, ("EXEC_ERROR %r\n" % error).encode()[:2048], deadline)
597:                     except BaseException:
598:                         pass
599:                     raise
600:             kid = fork_body(exec_descendant, deadline)
601:             report["grandchild"] = kid
602:             os.close(exec_write)
603:             os.close(gate_read)
604:             try:
605:                 pre, edge = edge_observation(kid, "exec_descendant", "pre_exec", inner_deadline)
606:                 report["descendants"].append(pre)
607:                 report["ownership_edges"].append(edge)
608:                 if pre["identity"] is None or not edge["current_parent_verified"]:
609:                     report["evidence_failures"].append("PRE_EXEC_CURRENT_PARENT_NOT_PROVED")
610:                 complete_write(gate_write, b"S", inner_deadline)
611:             finally:
612:                 os.close(gate_write)
613:             try:
614:                 exec_raw, exec_proof = read_pipe(exec_read, 2048, inner_deadline)
615:             finally:
616:                 os.close(exec_read)

647: def main():

649:         return 2
650:     name, life = sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2])

657:     try:

679:                                 "poll_interval_s": POLL_S, "deadline_extended": False}, **state}
680:     def result(extra=None):

682:         result_attempted = True

693:     try:

707:             return status()

718:         if name == "flood":
719:             os.set_blocking(1, False)
720:             line = b"x" * 65535 + b"\n"
721:             view = memoryview(line)
722:             while time.monotonic() < work_end:
723:                 try:
724:                     n = os.write(1, view)

749:                 for index in range(0, len(block), 4096):

125: def hex64(x):
126:     return isinstance(x, str) and len(x) == 64 and all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in x)

129: def fixed_source_pins(source_pins):
130:     """PURE. Exact {supervisor,child,writer,checker} -> {path,sha256} at the fixed paths; the supervisor must be the
131:     frozen v15 bytes. Other digests are the actual bytes the caller hashed; the parent's external approval trusts them."""
132:     if not isinstance(source_pins, dict) or set(source_pins) != set(SOURCES) or any(
133:             not isinstance(source_pins[k], dict) or set(source_pins[k]) != {"path", "sha256"} or
134:             source_pins[k]["path"] != SOURCES[k] or not hex64(source_pins[k]["sha256"]) for k in SOURCES):
135:         raise ValueError("source pins are not exactly the four fixed package source paths")
136:     if source_pins["supervisor"]["sha256"] != SUPERVISOR_SHA:
137:         raise ValueError("supervisor bytes are not the frozen owned_supervisor_v15 source")
138:     return {k: {"path": SOURCES[k], "sha256": source_pins[k]["sha256"]} for k in SOURCES}

147:             not python.endswith(PYTHON_SUFFIX) or not hex64(python_sha256):
148:         raise ValueError("interpreter pin is not an actual Python.app executable digest")
149:     for total, top in ((outstanding, 1 << 60), (lane_outstanding, LANE_CEILING)):
150:         if not isinstance(total, int) or isinstance(total, bool) or not 0 <= total <= top:
151:             raise ValueError("parent outstanding reservation total out of range")
152:     return {"bytes": RESERVATION, "cpu_slots": 2 if name == "busy2" else 1,

169: def preparation_for(name, run_inode, output_root_inode, fixture_inode, source_pins, python, python_sha256,
170:                     outstanding, lane_outstanding):
171:     """PURE. Exact qualification-creation-preparation-v1 object; it never contains a config or GO digest. Inodes are
172:     [st_dev, st_ino] from fstat of the writer's CREATED descriptors. fixture_inode is required for mutate_pin and None
173:     otherwise; its sha256/bytes/nlink are the FIXTURE facts verified on the created descriptor before close."""
174:     def identity(x):
175:         return isinstance(x, list) and len(x) == 2 and all(
176:             isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and v >= 0 for v in x)
177: 
178:     allocation = pinned_inputs(name, source_pins, python, python_sha256, outstanding, lane_outstanding)
179:     if not identity(run_inode) or not identity(output_root_inode) or run_inode == output_root_inode or \
180:             run_inode[0] != output_root_inode[0]:
181:         raise ValueError("run/output-root creation identities")
182:     run = QROOT + "/" + name
183:     fixture = None
184:     if name == "mutate_pin":
185:         if not identity(fixture_inode) or fixture_inode[0] != run_inode[0] or \
186:                 fixture_inode in (run_inode, output_root_inode):
187:             raise ValueError("mutate_pin requires its created fixture identity")
188:         fixture = {"path": run + "/pinned-scratch.txt", "inode": list(fixture_inode),
189:                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(FIXTURE).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(FIXTURE), "nlink": 1}
190:     elif fixture_inode is not None:
191:         raise ValueError("only mutate_pin has a creation fixture")
192:     return {"kind": "qualification-creation-preparation-v1", "scenario": name,
193:             "run": {"path": run, "inode": list(run_inode)},
194:             "output_root": {"path": run + "/out", "inode": list(output_root_inode)}, "fixture": fixture,
195:             "source_pins": fixed_source_pins(source_pins), "python": {"path": python, "sha256": python_sha256},
196:             "allocation": allocation, "negative_control": name not in POSITIVE}

206:         raise ValueError("creation_channel must be {} or exactly this mutation's prepared channel")

215:     call = 0.01 if name == "helper_overrun" else 0.2 if name in ("helper_group", "helper_eof") else 5.0

224:             "output_roots": [out], "pins": files, "max_wall_s": wall, "settle_s": 20, "sample_s": 1.0,
225:             "max_chunk_seconds": 10800, "owned_compute_settled_by_epoch": 1792731393.0,

228:             "lane_disk_root": LANE, "lane_disk_ceiling_bytes": LANE_CEILING, "lane_walk_s": 5.0,

249: def negative_designation(name):
250:     """PURE. Explicit negative authority the parent must supply: exact approval flags and the exact three-key GO
251:     qualification_negative object (None means the GO omits it)."""
252:     if name not in SC:
253:         raise ValueError("unknown qualification scenario")
254:     kind = "mutation" if name in MUTATIONS else "ownership" if name in NEGATIVE_PROCESS else \
255:         "helper_injection" if name in HELPER_INJECTIONS else None
256:     go = None if kind is None else {"scenario": name, "kind": kind, "creation_channel": name in MUTATIONS}
257:     return {"negative_control": name not in POSITIVE, "go_qualification_negative": go,
258:             "approval": {"ownership_negative": name in NEGATIVE_PROCESS,

262: 
263: def canonical(obj):
264:     return (json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, indent=1, allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()
265: 
266: 
267: class Budget:
268:     """Cooperative monotonic I/O budget: refusal only, never a signal, kill or tool timeout; no value is recorded."""
269: 
270:     def __init__(self, seconds):
271:         self.end = time.monotonic() + seconds
272: 
273:     def check(self, where):
274:         if time.monotonic() > self.end:
275:             raise OSError("preparation I/O budget exceeded " + where)
276: 
277: 
278: def decimal(text):
279:     if not (0 < len(text) <= 20 and text.isascii() and text.isdigit() and (text == "0" or text[0] != "0")):
280:         raise ValueError("reservation totals must be canonical nonnegative decimal integers")
281:     return int(text)
282: 
283: 
284: def open_directory(path, budget):
285:     """New descriptor by no-follow component traversal from /; every component must be a real directory."""
286:     if not path.startswith("/") or path == "/" or os.path.normpath(path) != path:
287:         raise ValueError("directory path is not a normalized absolute path")
288:     fd = os.open("/", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
289:     try:
290:         for component in path[1:].split("/"):
291:             if component in ("", ".", ".."):
292:                 raise ValueError("invalid directory component")
293:             nxt = os.open(component, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
294:             os.close(fd)
295:             fd = nxt
296:             budget.check("directory traversal")
297:         return fd
298:     except BaseException:
299:         os.close(fd)
300:         raise
301: 
302: 
303: def read_bounded(dir_fd, name, cap, budget, keep=False):
304:     """Bounded no-follow regular-file read relative to a retained directory descriptor; any change refuses."""
305:     st = os.stat(name, dir_fd=dir_fd, follow_symlinks=False)
306:     if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or not 0 <= st.st_size <= cap:
307:         raise OSError("not a bounded regular file " + name)
308:     fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=dir_fd)
309:     h, parts, got = hashlib.sha256(), [], 0
310:     try:
311:         fs = os.fstat(fd)
312:         if (fs.st_dev, fs.st_ino, fs.st_size) != (st.st_dev, st.st_ino, st.st_size):
313:             raise OSError("file changed at open " + name)
314:         while got < st.st_size:
315:             b = os.read(fd, min(1 << 20, st.st_size - got))
316:             if not b:
317:                 raise OSError("short read " + name)
318:             h.update(b)
319:             got += len(b)
320:             if keep:
321:                 parts.append(b)
322:             budget.check("read " + name)
323:         tail = os.read(fd, 1)
324:         end = os.fstat(fd)
325:         if tail or (end.st_size, end.st_mtime_ns) != (st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns):
326:             raise OSError("file changed during read " + name)
327:     finally:
328:         os.close(fd)
329:     facts = {"sha256": h.hexdigest(), "inode": [st.st_dev, st.st_ino], "bytes": got, "nlink": st.st_nlink}
330:     return facts, (b"".join(parts) if keep else None)
331: 
332: 
333: def make_directory(parent_fd, name, device, budget):
334:     """Exclusive mkdir; its creation identity is fstat of the no-follow descriptor opened right after it."""
335:     os.mkdir(name, 0o755, dir_fd=parent_fd)
336:     fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent_fd)
337:     try:
338:         st = os.fstat(fd)
339:         if not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) or st.st_dev != device or st.st_uid != os.geteuid() or os.listdir(fd):
340:             raise OSError("created directory is not a fresh owned empty same-volume directory " + name)
341:         budget.check("mkdir " + name)
342:     except BaseException:
343:         os.close(fd)
344:         raise
345:     return fd, [st.st_dev, st.st_ino]
346: 
347: 
348: def create_exclusive(dir_fd, name, data, mode, budget):
349:     """Exclusive no-follow creation. Recorded inode/size/nlink are fstat of the CREATED descriptor BEFORE close and
350:     the content is read back on that descriptor; a later no-follow read must still find exactly that file."""
351:     fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, mode, dir_fd=dir_fd)
352:     try:
353:         view = memoryview(data)
354:         while view:
355:             n = os.write(fd, view)
356:             if n <= 0:
357:                 raise OSError("zero-progress write " + name)
358:             view = view[n:]
359:             budget.check("write " + name)
360:         os.fsync(fd)
361:         st = os.fstat(fd)
362:         if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_size != len(data) or st.st_nlink != 1 or \
363:                 os.pread(fd, len(data) + 1, 0) != data:
364:             raise OSError("created descriptor facts differ " + name)
365:         created = {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "inode": [st.st_dev, st.st_ino],
366:                    "bytes": st.st_size, "nlink": st.st_nlink}
367:     finally:
368:         os.close(fd)
369:     if read_bounded(dir_fd, name, len(data), budget)[0] != created:
370:         raise OSError("created file changed after close " + name)
371:     return created
372: 
373: 
374: def main():
375:     if len(sys.argv) != 5:
376:         raise ValueError("usage: SCENARIO ACTUAL_PYTHON_APP OUTSTANDING_BYTES LANE_OUTSTANDING_BYTES")
377:     name, python = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
378:     outstanding, lane_outstanding = decimal(sys.argv[3]), decimal(sys.argv[4])
379:     if name not in SC:
380:         raise ValueError("unknown qualification scenario")
381:     if os.path.abspath(__file__) != SOURCES["writer"]:
382:         raise ValueError("writer must execute from its fixed package path")
383:     if not python.endswith(PYTHON_SUFFIX) or os.path.realpath(python) != python:
384:         raise ValueError("interpreter is not the actual non-launcher Python.app executable")
385:     budget = Budget(IO_BUDGET_S)
386:     with contextlib.ExitStack() as stack:
387:         def held(fd):
388:             stack.callback(os.close, fd)
389:             return fd
390: 
391:         lane = held(open_directory(LANE, budget))
392:         lane_st = os.fstat(lane)
393:         code = held(os.open("code", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=lane))
394:         actual = {}
395:         for key, path in SOURCES.items():
396:             facts = read_bounded(code, os.path.basename(path), SOURCE_CAP, budget)[0]
397:             actual[key] = {"path": path, "sha256": facts["sha256"]}
398:         source_pins = fixed_source_pins(actual)
399:         interpreter = held(open_directory(os.path.dirname(python), budget))
400:         python_sha = read_bounded(interpreter, os.path.basename(python), PIN_CAP, budget)[0]["sha256"]
401:         allocation = pinned_inputs(name, source_pins, python, python_sha, outstanding, lane_outstanding)
402:         marker = marker_for(source_pins)
403:         negative = negative_designation(name)
404:         # Arguments, fixed paths and actual source/interpreter digests are validated; creation starts only here.
405:         created_root = True
406:         try:
407:             os.mkdir(PACKAGE, 0o755, dir_fd=lane)
408:         except FileExistsError:
409:             created_root = False
410:         qfd = held(os.open(PACKAGE, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=lane))
411:         qst = os.fstat(qfd)
412:         if not stat.S_ISDIR(qst.st_mode) or qst.st_dev != lane_st.st_dev or qst.st_uid != os.geteuid():
413:             raise OSError("package root is not an owned same-volume directory")
414:         if created_root:
415:             if os.listdir(qfd):
416:                 raise OSError("newly created package root is not empty")
417:             root = create_exclusive(qfd, "package-root.json", canonical(marker), 0o444, budget)
418:         else:
419:             root, raw = read_bounded(qfd, "package-root.json", SOURCE_CAP, budget, keep=True)
420:             if raw != canonical(marker) or root["nlink"] != 1:
421:                 raise ValueError("existing package root marker is not this package's actual source identity")
422:         try:
423:             rfd, run_inode = make_directory(qfd, name, lane_st.st_dev, budget)
424:         except FileExistsError:
425:             raise ValueError("RUN already exists; preparation is fresh-only and never reuses a run") from None
426:         held(rfd)
427:         ofd, out_inode = make_directory(rfd, "out", lane_st.st_dev, budget)
428:         held(ofd)
429:         tfd, tmp_inode = make_directory(rfd, "tmp", lane_st.st_dev, budget)
430:         held(tfd)
431:         fixture_inode = None
432:         if name == "mutate_pin":
433:             fixture_inode = create_exclusive(rfd, "pinned-scratch.txt", FIXTURE, 0o644, budget)["inode"]
434:         prep = preparation_for(name, run_inode, out_inode, fixture_inode, source_pins, python, python_sha,
435:                                outstanding, lane_outstanding)
436:         prepared = create_exclusive(rfd, "preparation.json", canonical(prep), 0o444, budget)
437:         channel = creation_channel_for(name, prepared["sha256"])
438:         cfg = config_for(name, python, python_sha, outstanding, lane_outstanding, source_pins, prepared["sha256"],
439:                          channel)
440:         configured = create_exclusive(rfd, "config.json", canonical(cfg), 0o444, budget)
441:         os.fsync(rfd)
442:         os.fsync(qfd)
443:         if created_root:
444:             os.fsync(lane)
445:         run = QROOT + "/" + name

85: def integer(x, lower=None):
86:     return isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) and (lower is None or x >= lower)

89: def finite(x):
90:     try:
91:         return isinstance(x, (float, int)) and not isinstance(x, bool) and math.isfinite(x)
92:     except OverflowError:
93:         return False

96: def pairs(kv):
97:     out = {}
98:     for k, v in kv:
99:         if k in out:
100:             raise Invalid("duplicate JSON key")
101:         out[k] = v
102:     return out

105: def floating(s):
106:     n = float(s)
107:     if not math.isfinite(n):
108:         raise Invalid("nonfinite numeric literal")
109:     return n

113:     raise Invalid("nonfinite JSON constant")

116: DECODER = json.JSONDecoder(object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_float=floating, parse_constant=constant)

119: def decode(raw):
120:     return DECODER.decode(raw.decode())

123: def sequence(raw):
124:     text, at, objects = raw.decode(), 0, []
125:     while at < len(text):
126:         while at < len(text) and text[at].isspace():
127:             at += 1
128:         if at == len(text):
129:             break
130:         obj, at = DECODER.raw_decode(text, at)
131:         if not isinstance(obj, dict):
132:             raise Invalid("sample is not an object")
133:         objects.append(obj)
134:     return objects

137: def directory(path):
138:     fd = os.open("/", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
139:     try:
140:         for c in path.lstrip("/").split("/"):
141:             if c in ("", ".", ".."):
142:                 raise Invalid("invalid directory component")
143:             nxt = os.open(c, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
144:             os.close(fd)
145:             fd = nxt
146:         return fd
147:     except BaseException:
148:         os.close(fd)
149:         raise

152: def snapshot(fdroot, name, cap=64 << 20, optional=False):
153:     deadline = time.monotonic() + 30
154:     def signature(s):
155:         return (s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_mode, s.st_size, s.st_nlink, s.st_mtime_ns, s.st_ctime_ns)
156:     try:
157:         st = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fdroot, follow_symlinks=False)
158:     except FileNotFoundError:
159:         if optional:
160:             return None
161:         raise
162:     if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or not 0 <= st.st_size <= cap:
163:         raise Invalid("not a bounded regular file: " + name)
164:     fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=fdroot)
165:     try:
166:         s1 = os.fstat(fd)
167:         if signature(s1) != signature(st):
168:             raise Invalid("file changed at open")
169:         parts, n = [], 0
170:         while n < st.st_size:
171:             b = os.read(fd, min(1 << 20, st.st_size - n))
172:             if not b:
173:                 raise Invalid("short read")
174:             parts.append(b)
175:             n += len(b)
176:             if time.monotonic() > deadline:
177:                 raise Invalid("read deadline")
178:         tail, s2 = os.read(fd, 1), os.fstat(fd)
179:         named_end = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fdroot, follow_symlinks=False)
180:         if tail or signature(s2) != signature(st) or signature(named_end) != signature(st):
181:             raise Invalid("file changed during read")
182:     finally:
183:         os.close(fd)
184:     if time.monotonic() > deadline:
185:         raise Invalid("read completion deadline")
186:     raw = b"".join(parts)
187:     return {"raw": raw, "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(raw),
188:             "inode": [st.st_dev, st.st_ino], "nlink": st.st_nlink, "birth_epoch": st.st_birthtime}
189: 
190: 
191: def absolute_snapshot(path, cap=64 << 20):
192:     fd = directory(os.path.dirname(path))
193:     try:
194:         return snapshot(fd, os.path.basename(path), cap)
195:     finally:
196:         os.close(fd)

328: def date_epoch(text):
329:     if not isinstance(text, str):
330:         raise Invalid("date is not an actual helper string")
331:     parsed = datetime.datetime.strptime(text, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z")
332:     if parsed.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z") != text:
333:         raise Invalid("noncanonical actual date")
334:     return parsed.timestamp()

337: def same(a, b):
338:     return json.dumps(a, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False) == \
339:         json.dumps(b, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)

342: def identity(p):
343:     if not isinstance(p, dict) or not all(integer(p.get(k), 0) for k in
344:         ("pid", "ppid", "pgid", "uniqueid", "euid", "ruid", "status")) or not integer(p.get("idversion")) or \
345:             not -(1 << 31) <= p["idversion"] < 1 << 31 or \
346:             not all(p[k] > 0 for k in ("pid", "uniqueid")) or not isinstance(p.get("traced"), bool) or \
347:             not isinstance(p.get("comm"), str):
348:         raise Invalid("full process incarnation missing")
349:     return tuple(p[k] for k in ("pid", "uniqueid", "idversion", "euid", "ruid", "ppid", "pgid", "traced"))
350: 
351: 
352: def histories(view, baseuids):
353:     required = ("identity_history", "unproved_history", "coverage_history", "census_observations", "observation_attempts")
354:     if any(not isinstance(view.get(k), list) for k in required):
355:         raise Invalid("complete current/retained history schema missing")
356:     ih, uh, ch, cs, ats = (view[k] for k in required)
357:     if len(ih) > 8192 or len(uh) > 8192 or len(ch) > 16384 or len(cs) > 4096 or len(ats) > 4096:
358:         raise Invalid("history entry cap")
359:     if not integer(view.get("history_charge_bytes"), 0) or view["history_charge_bytes"] > 4 << 20 or \
360:             view.get("history_overflow") is not None or view.get("history_halted", False) is not False:
361:         raise Invalid("history cap/overflow or missing charged bytes")
362:     imap = {}
363:     for e in ih:
364:         first, last, variants = e.get("first_identity"), e.get("last_identity"), e.get("variants")
365:         f, l = identity(first), identity(last)
366:         uid = first["uniqueid"]
367:         if uid in imap or first["uniqueid"] != last["uniqueid"] or first["pid"] != last["pid"] or \
368:                 not finite(e.get("mono_first_seen")) or not finite(e.get("mono_last_seen")) or \
369:                 e["mono_first_seen"] > e["mono_last_seen"] or not integer(e.get("observations"), 1) or \
370:                 not isinstance(e.get("source"), str) or not isinstance(variants, list) or not 1 <= len(variants) <= 64:
371:             raise Invalid("identity ledger malformed")
372:         fps = [identity(p) for p in variants]
373:         if len(set(fps)) != len(fps) or f not in fps or l not in fps or any(
374:                 p["uniqueid"] != uid or p["pid"] != first["pid"] for p in variants):
375:             raise Invalid("identity variants changed/disappeared incarnation")
376:         imap[uid] = e
377:     def retained(p, stamp=None):
378:         fp, uid = identity(p), p["uniqueid"]
379:         e = imap.get(uid)
380:         if e is None or fp not in {identity(v) for v in e["variants"]}:
381:             raise Invalid("observed incarnation omitted from retained ledger")
382:         if stamp is not None and not e["mono_first_seen"] <= stamp <= e["mono_last_seen"] + 1e-6:
383:             raise Invalid("retained incarnation time does not enclose observation")
384:     umap = {}
385:     for e in uh:
386:         first, last = e.get("first_identity"), e.get("last_identity")
387:         retained(first)
388:         retained(last)
389:         uid = first["uniqueid"]
390:         if uid in umap or uid in baseuids or uid != last["uniqueid"] or not finite(e.get("mono_first_seen")) or \
391:                 not finite(e.get("mono_last_seen")) or e["mono_first_seen"] > e["mono_last_seen"] or \
392:                 not imap[uid]["mono_first_seen"] <= e["mono_first_seen"] <= e["mono_last_seen"] <= imap[uid]["mono_last_seen"]:
393:             raise Invalid("unproved immutable first/last history invalid")
394:         umap[uid] = e
395:     prior = None
396:     for e in ch:
397:         row = e.get("observation")
398:         if not finite(e.get("mono")) or (prior is not None and e["mono"] < prior) or not isinstance(row, list) or \
399:                 len(row) != 3 or not integer(row[0], 1) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in row[1:]):
400:             raise Invalid("coverage evidence malformed/reordered")
401:         prior = e["mono"]
402:     attempt_by_end = {}
403:     for e in ats:
404:         if e.get("kind") not in ("identity_scan", "resource_scan") or e.get("phase") not in ("ADMISSION", "PRE_GO", "RUNNING", "FINAL") or \
405:                 not isinstance(e.get("inside_helper"), bool) or not isinstance(e.get("aborted"), bool) or \
406:                 not finite(e.get("mono_start")) or not finite(e.get("mono_end")) or e["mono_end"] < e["mono_start"] or \
407:                 (e["aborted"] and not isinstance(e.get("error"), str)) or (not e["aborted"] and e.get("error") is not None):
408:             raise Invalid("completed/aborted attempt missing")
409:         if e["kind"] == "resource_scan":
410:             if e["mono_end"] in attempt_by_end:
411:                 raise Invalid("duplicate resource attempt")
412:             attempt_by_end[e["mono_end"]] = e
413:     prior_start = None
414:     for c in cs:
415:         a = attempt_by_end.pop(c.get("mono_end"), None)
416:         if a is None or not finite(c.get("mono_start")) or a["mono_start"] < c["mono_start"] or \
417:                 (prior_start is not None and c["mono_start"] <= prior_start) or c.get("phase") != a["phase"] or \
418:                 c.get("inside_helper") != a["inside_helper"] or c.get("aborted") != a["aborted"] or \
419:                 not isinstance(c.get("complete"), bool) or not integer(c.get("incomplete_n"), 0) or \
420:                 not isinstance(c.get("incomplete"), list) or len(c["incomplete"]) != min(c["incomplete_n"], 30):
421:             raise Invalid("resource observation/attempt binding invalid")
422:         prior_start = c["mono_start"]
423:         if c["aborted"]:
424:             if c["complete"] is not False or c.get("mono_complete") is not None or not finite(c.get("mono_aborted")) or \
425:                     not c["mono_start"] <= c["mono_aborted"] <= c["mono_end"] or c.get("abort_error") != a["error"]:
426:                 raise Invalid("aborted observation presented as complete")
427:         elif not finite(c.get("mono_complete")) or not c["mono_start"] <= c["mono_complete"] <= c["mono_end"] or \
428:                 c["complete"] != (c["incomplete_n"] == 0):
429:             raise Invalid("completed resource observation missing")
430:         if not all(integer(c.get(k), 0) for k in ("pids", "lane_busy", "lane_rss")) or c["lane_busy"] > 2 or \
431:                 not all(isinstance(c.get(k), list) for k in
432:                     ("owned_live", "unproved_live", "group_live", "owned_zombies", "lane_pids", "lane_busy_pids", "measurements")):
433:             raise Invalid("resource/history hard policy or schema")
434:         for row in c["incomplete"]:
435:             if not any(same(e["observation"], row) and c["mono_start"] <= e["mono"] <= c["mono_end"] for e in ch):
436:                 raise Invalid("missing-read coverage omitted")
437:         for p in c["owned_live"] + c["unproved_live"]:
438:             retained(p)
439:         if any(p["uniqueid"] not in umap for p in c["unproved_live"]):
440:             raise Invalid("current unproved incarnation missing historical first evidence")
441:         measurements, mpids, bpids, total, last = c["measurements"], [], [], 0, None
442:         for m in measurements:
443:             p = m.get("identity")
444:             retained(p)
445:             after = m.get("identity_after")
446:             retained(after)
447:             if identity(after) != identity(p):
448:                 raise Invalid("final resource identity bracket changed")
449:             if not integer(m.get("rss"), 0) or not integer(m.get("cpu_ticks"), 0) or not isinstance(m.get("busy"), bool) or \
450:                     not finite(m.get("mono_observed")) or not c["mono_start"] <= m["mono_observed"] <= c["mono_end"] or \
451:                     (last is not None and m["mono_observed"] <= last) or p["pid"] in mpids:
452:                 raise Invalid("bracketed PID-specific measurement missing")
453:             mpids.append(p["pid"])
454:             total += m["rss"]
455:             if m["busy"]:
456:                 bpids.append(p["pid"])
457:             last = m["mono_observed"]
458:         if c["lane_rss"] != total or sorted(c["lane_pids"]) != sorted(mpids) or \
459:                 sorted(c["lane_busy_pids"]) != sorted(bpids) or c["lane_busy"] != len(bpids) or \
460:                 (last is not None and c.get("mono_last_observation") != last):
461:             raise Invalid("partial resource counters disagree with retained individual measurements")
462:     if attempt_by_end:
463:         raise Invalid("resource attempt omitted from observation history")
464:     return imap, umap, retained

466: 
467: def history_extensions(old, new):
468:     for key in ("coverage_history", "census_observations", "observation_attempts"):
469:         a, b = old[key], new[key]
470:         if len(b) < len(a) or not same(b[:len(a)], a):
471:             raise Invalid("postpublication history prefix changed: " + key)
472:     for key in ("identity_history", "unproved_history"):
473:         before = {e["first_identity"]["uniqueid"]: e for e in old[key]}
474:         after = {e["first_identity"]["uniqueid"]: e for e in new[key]}
475:         if not set(before) <= set(after):
476:             raise Invalid("historical incarnation vanished")
477:         for uid, first in before.items():
478:             last = after[uid]
479:             for k in ("first_identity", "mono_first_seen"):
480:                 if not same(first[k], last[k]):
481:                     raise Invalid("immutable first incarnation/observation changed")
482:             if last["mono_last_seen"] < first["mono_last_seen"]:
483:                 raise Invalid("last observation rewound")
484:             if last["mono_last_seen"] == first["mono_last_seen"] and not same(first["last_identity"], last["last_identity"]):
485:                 raise Invalid("last incarnation changed without a new observation")
486:             if key == "identity_history" and (last["source"] != first["source"] or last["observations"] < first["observations"] or
487:                     not same(last["variants"][:len(first["variants"])], first["variants"])):
488:                 raise Invalid("incarnation variant/count prefix changed")
489:     if new["history_charge_bytes"] < old["history_charge_bytes"]:
490:         raise Invalid("history accounting rewound")
491: 
492: 
493: def census_join(c, observations, subset=False):
494:     if not isinstance(c, dict):
495:         raise Invalid("embedded census missing")
496:     matches = [r for r in observations if (all(k in r and same(v, r[k]) for k, v in c.items()) if subset else same(c, r))]
497:     if len(matches) != 1:
498:         raise Invalid("embedded census does not join a unique retained observation")
499:     return matches[0]

502: def integrated_v15_evidence(receipt, summary, stability, cfg, go, name, samples):
503:     checks = {}
504:     baseline = receipt.get("baseline")
505:     if not isinstance(baseline, dict) or not finite(baseline.get("mono_complete")) or not isinstance(baseline.get("identities"), list):
506:         raise Invalid("complete pre-spawn baseline missing")
507:     baseuids = set()
508:     for p in baseline["identities"]:
509:         identity(p)
510:         if p["uniqueid"] in baseuids:
511:             raise Invalid("duplicate baseline incarnation")
512:         baseuids.add(p["uniqueid"])
513:     imap, umap, retained = histories(receipt, baseuids)
514:     current = summary.get("reconciliation")
515:     current_keys = {"owned_identities", "unproved_history", "identity_history", "coverage_history", "census_observations",
516:                     "observation_attempts", "helpers", "helper_log", "failures", "main_identity", "main_signal", "main_stop",
517:                     "baseline_uniqueids", "stability_expiry_mono", "history_charge_bytes", "history_cap_bytes", "history_halted",
518:                     "history_overflow", "publication_resources", "ack_resources", "publication_payload_bytes",
519:                     "last_accounting_observation", "maxima", "min_free"}
520:     if not isinstance(current, dict) or set(current) != current_keys or current["history_halted"] is not False or \
521:             not integer(current["history_cap_bytes"], 0) or current["history_cap_bytes"] != 4 << 20:
522:         raise Invalid("mandatory complete current refusal/ACK reconciliation schema missing")
523:     current_imap, current_umap, current_retained = histories(current, baseuids)
524:     history_extensions(receipt, current)
525:     if not same(current.get("baseline_uniqueids"), sorted(baseuids)):
526:         raise Invalid("current reconciliation baseline changed")
527:     owned = current.get("owned_identities")
528:     if not isinstance(owned, list) or not same(owned[:len(receipt["owned_identities"])], receipt["owned_identities"]):
529:         raise Invalid("owned registry prefix changed")
530:     for o in owned:
531:         current_retained(o["identity"])
532:     if not same(current.get("helpers"), receipt["helpers"]) or not same(current.get("helper_log"), receipt["helper_log"]) or \
533:             not same(current.get("failures"), receipt["failures"]) or not same(current.get("main_identity"), receipt["child_identity"]) or \
534:             not same(current.get("main_signal"), receipt["signal"]) or not same(current.get("main_stop"), receipt["stop_request"]):
535:         raise Invalid("current reconciliation contradicts frozen outcome/helper/stop evidence")
536:     obs = receipt["census_observations"]
537:     later = current["census_observations"]
538:     embedded = [receipt.get("final_census")]
539:     embedded += receipt.get("admission", {}).get("censuses", [])
540:     embedded += [receipt[k] for k in ("launch_census", "pre_go_census") if k in receipt]
541:     if receipt.get("clear") is not None:
542:         embedded.append(receipt["clear"]["census"])
543:     embedded += [s.get("census") for s in samples]
544:     for c in embedded:
545:         census_join(c, obs)
546:     for h in receipt["helpers"]:
547:         census_join(h["proof"], obs, subset=True)
548:     if summary.get("acknowledged") is True:
549:         for k in ("unproved_history", "coverage_history", "census_observations"):
550:             if not same(summary.get(k), current[k]):
551:                 raise Invalid("duplicated current history contradicts reconciliation")
552:         ack = summary.get("ack_census")
553:         census_join(ack, later)
554:         final = receipt["final_census"]
555:         if ack.get("complete") is not True:
556:             raise Invalid("ACK coverage incomplete")
557:         fids = {p["uniqueid"] for p in final["owned_live"] + final["unproved_live"]}
558:         aids = {p["uniqueid"] for p in ack["owned_live"] + ack["unproved_live"]}
559:         if not aids <= fids or (receipt["settlement"] != "UNSETTLED" and aids) or not \
560:                 summary["post_publish_mono"] <= ack["mono_complete"] <= summary["ack_commit"]["mono"] or \
561:                 summary["ack_commit"]["mono"] - ack["mono_complete"] > max(1, cfg["sample_s"]):
562:             raise Invalid("ACK census not fresh or current blockers changed")
563:         if not same(summary.get("owned_live"), ack["owned_live"]) or not same(summary.get("unproved_live"), ack["unproved_live"]) or \
564:                 not same(summary.get("contract_violations"), receipt["contract_violations"]) or \
565:                 not same(summary.get("failure_codes"), sorted(f["code"] for f in receipt["failures"])) or \
566:                 not same(summary.get("child_returncode"), receipt["child_returncode"]):
567:             raise Invalid("duplicated outcome/current blocker fields contradict receipt")
568:         if not same(later[-1], ack):
569:             raise Invalid("ACK is not the final retained resource observation")
570:     checks["full_history_prefix_tail_and_embedded_joins"] = True
571:     stable_epoch = date_epoch(stability.get("now_date"))
572:     good = []
573:     expiry = None
574:     helper_by_pid = {h["pid"]: h for h in receipt["helpers"]}
575:     purposes = {}
576:     for e in receipt["helper_log"]:
577:         purpose = e.get("purpose")
578:         if purpose not in ("admission", "start", "stop", "end") or purpose in purposes:
579:             raise Invalid("helper purpose missing/duplicated")
580:         purposes[purpose] = e
581:         if e.get("outcome") == "OK":
582:             h = helper_by_pid.get(e.get("pid"))
583:             if h is None or h.get("clock_failed") is not False or h.get("date") is None or \
584:                     h["mono_call_start"] != e["mono_start"] or not h["mono_proof_complete"] <= e["mono_end"]:
585:                 raise Invalid("actual date purpose/PID/proof not joined")
586:             raw = bytes.fromhex(h["capture_hex"])
587:             if raw != (h["date"] + "\n").encode("ascii"):
588:                 raise Invalid("actual /bin/date capture does not match exact canonical date line")
589:             floor = date_epoch(h["date"])
590:             if floor < stable_epoch:
591:                 raise Invalid("actual date floor precedes canonical stability receipt")
592:             candidate = h["mono_call_start"] + cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"] - (floor - stable_epoch + 1.0)
593:             effective = candidate if expiry is None else min(expiry, candidate)
594:             if not same(h.get("stability_expiry_before"), expiry) or not finite(h.get("stability_expiry_candidate")) or \
595:                     abs(h["stability_expiry_candidate"] - candidate) > 1e-6 or not finite(h.get("stability_expiry_effective")) or \
596:                     abs(h["stability_expiry_effective"] - effective) > 1e-6:
597:                 raise Invalid("monotonic stability expiry was renewed/omitted")
598:             expiry = effective
599:             good.append((h, effective))
600:     for purpose, value in (("admission", receipt.get("admission", {}).get("date")),
601:                            ("start", receipt.get("start_date")), ("end", receipt.get("end_date"))):
602:         e = purposes.get(purpose)
603:         if e is None:
604:             if purpose == "admission" and name == "go_unlisted" and value is None:
605:                 continue
606:             if purpose == "start" and receipt["child_pid"] is None and value is None:
607:                 continue
608:             raise Invalid("mandatory actual-date purpose missing: " + purpose)
609:         expected = helper_by_pid[e["pid"]]["date"] if e["outcome"] == "OK" else None
610:         if not same(value, expected):
611:             raise Invalid("date field contradicts purpose/PID/capture")
612:     stop = receipt.get("stop_request")
613:     if stop is not None:
614:         e = purposes.get("stop")
615:         if e is None or not same(stop.get("date"), helper_by_pid[e["pid"]]["date"] if e["outcome"] == "OK" else None):
616:             raise Invalid("stop date provenance missing")
617:     if not same(receipt.get("stability_expiry_mono"), expiry) or not same(current.get("stability_expiry_mono"), expiry):
618:         raise Invalid("frozen/current stability cutoff mismatch")
619:     if good:
620:         h, _ = good[-1]
621:         observation = receipt.get("clock_observation")
622:         if not isinstance(observation, dict) or observation.get("date") != h["date"] or \
623:                 observation.get("mono_before_read") != h["mono_call_start"] or \
624:                 observation.get("epoch_date_floor") != date_epoch(h["date"]) or \
625:                 not finite(observation.get("mono_after_read")) or not h["mono_proof_complete"] <= observation["mono_after_read"]:
626:             raise Invalid("latest actual clock observation omitted/contradictory")
627:     elif receipt.get("clock_observation") is not None or expiry is not None:
628:         raise Invalid("invented clock on deliberate helper/admission failure")
629:     commits = []
630:     for index, h in enumerate(receipt["helpers"]):
631:         commits.append((h.get("launch_commit"), "HELPER", index == 0,
632:                         cfg["helper_budget"]["call_budget_s"] + cfg["helper_budget"]["term_grace_s"]))
633:     launched = receipt["child_pid"] is not None
634:     if launched:
635:         commits += [(receipt.get("admission", {}).get("checked"), "ADMISSION", False, cfg["max_wall_s"] + cfg["settle_s"] + 120),
636:                     (receipt.get("launch_commit"), "LAUNCH", False, cfg["max_wall_s"] + cfg["settle_s"] + 120),
637:                     (receipt.get("go_commit"), "GO", False, None)]
638:     elif any(k in receipt for k in ("launch_commit", "go_commit", "go_delivery_completion")) or \
639:             receipt.get("admission", {}).get("checked") is not None:
640:         raise Invalid("unexpected child/clock commit on admission refusal")
641:     if summary.get("acknowledged") is True:
642:         commits.append((summary.get("ack_commit"), "ACK", False, 0))
643:     for commit, phase, first_clock_exception, required in commits:
644:         if not isinstance(commit, dict) or commit.get("phase") != phase or \
645:                 not all(finite(commit.get(k)) for k in ("mono", "epoch", "required_s")) or commit["required_s"] < 0 or \
646:                 commit["epoch"] > go["not_after_epoch"] or \
647:                 commit["epoch"] + commit["required_s"] > cfg["owned_compute_settled_by_epoch"] - 180 or \
648:                 (required is not None and commit["required_s"] != required):
649:             raise Invalid("mandatory exact-phase commit missing/late/contradictory")
650:         prior = [(h, cut) for h, cut in good if h["mono_proof_complete"] <= commit["mono"]]
651:         if not prior:
652:             if not (phase == "HELPER" and first_clock_exception and commit.get("stability_age_upper_s") is None):
653:                 raise Invalid("only first actual clock helper may lack prior actual clock")
654:         else:
655:             h, cut = prior[-1]
656:             base = date_epoch(h["date"]) - stable_epoch
657:             if base < 0:
658:                 raise Invalid("future stability receipt")
659:             upper = max(base + 1.0 + max(0.0, commit["mono"] - h["mono_call_start"]),
660:                         cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"] + commit["mono"] - cut)
661:             if not finite(commit.get("stability_age_upper_s")) or abs(commit["stability_age_upper_s"] - upper) > 1e-6 or \
662:                     not 0 <= upper <= cfg["admission"]["stability_max_age_s"]:
663:                 raise Invalid("actual date plus immutable monotonic expiry age replay failed")
664:     if launched:
665:         delivery = receipt.get("go_delivery_completion")
666:         if not isinstance(delivery, dict) or not all(finite(delivery.get(k)) for k in
667:                 ("mono", "spawn_age_s", "spawn_age_bound_s", "remaining_main_runtime_s")):
668:             raise Invalid("actual post-write/close GO completion omitted")
669:         age, remaining = delivery["mono"] - receipt["mono_spawn"], receipt["mono_child_deadline"] - delivery["mono"]
670:         bound = min(60.0, cfg["max_wall_s"] / 2.0)
671:         if not (0 <= age <= bound and remaining > 0 and delivery["spawn_age_bound_s"] == bound and
672:                 abs(delivery["spawn_age_s"] - age) <= 1e-6 and abs(delivery["remaining_main_runtime_s"] - remaining) <= 1e-6 and
673:                 receipt["go_commit"]["mono"] <= receipt["go_written_mono"] <= delivery["mono"] and
674:                 receipt["mono_child_deadline"] == receipt["mono_spawn"] + cfg["max_wall_s"] and
675:                 receipt["go_commit"]["required_s"] >= max(0, receipt["mono_child_deadline"] - receipt["go_commit"]["mono"]) + cfg["settle_s"] + 120):
676:             raise Invalid("spawn-to-actual-GO upper age or remaining main runtime violated")
677:     if summary.get("acknowledged") is False:
678:         cause = "GO_DOES_NOT_NAME_THIS_CONFIG" if name == "go_unlisted" else "STABILITY_AGE_UNVERIFIABLE"
679:         if summary.get("error_code") != cause:
680:             raise Invalid("ACK refusal exact machine cause mismatch")
681:     checks["mandatory_clock_commits_provenance_cutoff_and_actual_GO_age"] = True
682:     if not isinstance(receipt.get("runtime_observations"), list):
683:         raise Invalid("runtime-obligation evidence missing")
684:     return checks

687: def common_final_evidence(receipt, summary, cfg, files, late, runfd, require):
688:     """Run for EVERY scenario, including all admission negatives; no early-return exemption."""
689:     final = receipt.get("final_census")
690:     if not isinstance(final, dict) or final.get("complete") is not True:
691:         raise Invalid("complete final census required for every scenario")
692:     if receipt.get("child_pid") is None:
693:         main_fields = {"child_pid", "child_identity", "child_returncode", "child_exit_mono", "child_exit_epoch",
694:                        "wait_status_lost", "stop_request", "signal", "streams", "samples_written", "samples_bytes",
695:                        "artifacts", "evidence_expected_sha256"}
696:         main_paths = [cfg[k] for k in ("stdout", "stderr", "samples", "launch_record")] + [o["path"] for o in cfg["outputs"]]
697:         if not main_fields <= set(receipt) or not same(receipt["artifacts"], {p: None for p in main_paths}) or \
698:                 not same(receipt["evidence_expected_sha256"], {}) or not same(receipt["streams"], {}) or \
699:                 not all(integer(receipt.get(k), 0) and receipt[k] == 0 for k in ("samples_written", "samples_bytes")) or \
700:                 any(receipt[k] is not None for k in ("child_pid", "child_identity", "child_returncode", "child_exit_mono",
701:                                                      "child_exit_epoch", "stop_request", "signal")) or receipt["wait_status_lost"] is not False:
702:             raise Invalid("not-launched MAIN artifact/digest/stream/counter/wait manifest missing or contradictory")
703:         # This says nothing is launched for MAIN. Actual helper/date/ownership histories above remain mandatory and untouched.
704:         require("admission_negative_MAIN_manifest_and_zero_counters", True)
705:     owned, helpers = receipt.get("owned_identities"), receipt.get("helpers")
706:     if not isinstance(owned, list) or not isinstance(helpers, list):
707:         raise Invalid("complete owned/helper registry required")
708:     roots = {}
709:     for h in helpers:
710:         identity(h["identity"])
711:         if not integer(h.get("rc")) or h.get("leader_reaped") is not True or h.get("wait_lost") is not False:
712:             raise Invalid("integer known helper wait status missing")
713:         roots[h["identity"]["uniqueid"]] = (h["identity"], "helper")
714:     if receipt.get("child_identity") is not None:
715:         roots[receipt["child_identity"]["uniqueid"]] = (receipt["child_identity"], "child")
716:     registry = {}
717:     for e in owned:
718:         p = e.get("identity")
719:         identity(p)
720:         uid = p["uniqueid"]
721:         if uid in registry or not integer(e.get("root"), 1) or e["root"] not in roots or \
722:                 not isinstance(e.get("via"), str) or not finite(e.get("first_seen_mono")):
723:             raise Invalid("owned registry malformed/duplicated/unrooted")
724:         registry[uid] = e
725:     for uid, (p, via) in roots.items():
726:         if uid not in registry or identity(registry[uid]["identity"]) != identity(p) or \
727:                 registry[uid]["root"] != uid or registry[uid]["via"] != via:
728:             raise Invalid("mandatory main/helper root absent from registry")
729:     for p in final["owned_live"]:
730:         if p["uniqueid"] not in registry:
731:             raise Invalid("current owned incarnation omitted from registry")
732:     retained = receipt.get("census_observations")
733:     if not isinstance(retained, list) or not retained:
734:         raise Invalid("all resource observations required before every early return")
735:     enforced = [c for c in retained if c["phase"] not in ("ADMISSION", "PRE_GO")]
736:     maxima = receipt.get("maxima")
737:     if not isinstance(maxima, dict) or not enforced or not all(integer(maxima.get(k), 0) for k in
738:             ("lane_busy", "lane_rss", "owned_live", "charged_bytes")):
739:         raise Invalid("complete explicit extrema missing")
740:     require("common_all_partial_retry_resource_maxima", maxima["lane_busy"] == max(c["lane_busy"] for c in enforced) and
741:             maxima["lane_rss"] == max(c["lane_rss"] for c in enforced) and
742:             maxima["owned_live"] == max(len(c["owned_live"]) for c in enforced))
743:     require("common_min_free_explicit", integer(receipt.get("min_free"), 0) and receipt["min_free"] >= cfg["free_trip_bytes"])
744:     require("common_wait_registry_and_final_census", True)
745:     published = {"supervisor-receipt.json", "settlement.json", "settlement.json.staged"}
746:     before = sum(v["bytes"] for n, v in files.items() if v is not None and n not in published and n not in late)
747:     if any(v["nlink"] != 1 for n, v in files.items() if v is not None and n not in published):
748:         raise Invalid("nonpublication artifact has an undeclared hard link")
749:     payload = files["supervisor-receipt.json"]["bytes"] + files["settlement.json"]["bytes"]
750:     before_resources, br, ar = receipt.get("publication_precheck"), summary.get("publication_resources"), summary.get("ack_resources")
751:     current = summary["reconciliation"]
752:     for r in (before_resources, br, ar, current.get("publication_resources"), current.get("ack_resources")):
753:         if not isinstance(r, dict) or set(r) != {"charged_bytes", "lane_bytes", "free"} or \
754:                 not all(integer(r[k], 0) for k in r):
755:             raise Invalid("actual historical publication/ACK accounting missing, including refused paths")
756:     if not same(br, current["publication_resources"]) or not same(ar, current["ack_resources"]) or \
757:             summary.get("publication_payload_bytes") != payload or current.get("publication_payload_bytes") != payload:
758:         raise Invalid("historical b/end/payload contradict current reconciliation")
759:     observation = current.get("last_accounting_observation")
760:     if not isinstance(observation, dict) or set(observation) != {"resources", "mono_observed"} or \
761:             not same(observation["resources"], ar) or not finite(observation.get("mono_observed")) or \
762:             not finite(summary.get("post_publish_mono")) or observation["mono_observed"] < summary["post_publish_mono"]:
763:         raise Invalid("latest actual disk accounting provenance missing")
764:     require("common_exact_payload_and_distinct_inode_charge", before_resources["charged_bytes"] == br["charged_bytes"] == before and
765:             ar["charged_bytes"] == before + payload and payload <= 4 << 20 and
766:             before + payload <= cfg["output_trip_bytes"] and before + payload <= RESERVATION and
767:             maxima["charged_bytes"] == before)
768:     adm = cfg["admission"]
769:     remaining = max(0, RESERVATION - before - payload)
770:     require("common_publication_reservation_free_lane_bounds", all(
771:             r["free"] - added - adm["outstanding_reservations_bytes"] - remaining >= cfg["free_trip_bytes"] and
772:             r["lane_bytes"] + added + adm["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"] + remaining <= cfg["lane_disk_ceiling_bytes"]
773:             for r, added in ((before_resources, payload), (br, payload), (ar, 0))))
774:     current_max = current.get("maxima")
775:     if not isinstance(current_max, dict) or not all(integer(current_max.get(k), 0) for k in maxima) or \
776:             any(current_max[k] < maxima[k] for k in maxima) or current_max["charged_bytes"] != before + payload or \
777:             not integer(current.get("min_free"), 0) or current["min_free"] > receipt["min_free"] or \
778:             current["min_free"] > min(br["free"], ar["free"]) or current["min_free"] < cfg["free_trip_bytes"]:
779:         raise Invalid("current extrema/free minimum absent or contradictory")
780:     later = current["census_observations"]
781:     later_enforced = [c for c in later if c["phase"] not in ("ADMISSION", "PRE_GO")]
782:     require("common_current_extrema_reconciled", current_max["lane_busy"] == max(c["lane_busy"] for c in later_enforced) and
783:             current_max["lane_rss"] == max(c["lane_rss"] for c in later_enforced) and
784:             current_max["owned_live"] == max(len(c["owned_live"]) for c in later_enforced))
785:     tfd = os.open("tmp", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=runfd)
786:     try:
787:         require("common_test_tmp_empty", os.listdir(tfd) == [])
788:     finally:
789:         os.close(tfd)

792: def child_observation(o, deadline, allowed_observers):
793:     if not isinstance(o, dict) or not integer(o.get("observer"), 1) or o["observer"] not in allowed_observers or \
794:             not integer(o.get("pid"), 1) or not isinstance(o.get("role"), str) or not isinstance(o.get("phase"), str) or \
795:             not finite(o.get("mono_start")) or not finite(o.get("observed_mono")) or \
796:             not o["mono_start"] <= o["observed_mono"] < deadline or not isinstance(o.get("bracket"), dict):
797:         raise Invalid("reported actual child observation schema/deadline")
798:     if o.get("error") is not None:
799:         if not isinstance(o["error"], str) or o.get("identity") is not None:
800:             raise Invalid("failed child read invented an identity")
801:         return None
802:     p, b = o.get("identity"), o["bracket"]
803:     identity(p)
804:     if set(b) != {"unique_before", "short_before", "unique_middle", "short_after", "unique_after"} or \
805:             not same(b["unique_before"], [p["uniqueid"], p["idversion"]]) or \
806:             not same(b["unique_before"], b["unique_middle"]) or not same(b["unique_before"], b["unique_after"]):
807:         raise Invalid("reported incarnation lacks the three equal actual unique/version reads")
808:     before, after = dict(b["short_before"]), dict(b["short_after"])
809:     for s in (before, after):
810:         s.update(uniqueid=p["uniqueid"], idversion=p["idversion"])
811:     if identity(before) != identity(after) or not same(after, p) or p["pid"] != o["pid"]:
812:         raise Invalid("reported current parent/group/UID/version/trace bracket changed")
813:     return p
