816: def child_envelope(report, name, main, receipt, cfg, has_output):
817:     """Validate actual reported incarnations/private joins; report all variants for independent parent ended-identity audit."""
818:     if not isinstance(report, dict) or report.get("child") != "guard_qual_child_v11" or report.get("scenario") != name or \
819:             report.get("pid") != main["pid"] or report.get("pgid") != main["pgid"] or \
820:             report.get("negative_control") is not (name not in ("ok", "rc_nonzero")) or \
821:             report.get("evidence_failures") != []:
822:         raise Invalid("child report identity/negative declaration/failure evidence")
823:     entry, life = report.get("mono_module_entry"), report.get("mono_life_deadline")
824:     if not all(finite(report.get(k)) for k in ("mono_module_entry", "epoch_module_entry", "mono_life_deadline", "mono_output")) or \
825:             abs(life - entry - SC[name][0]) > 1e-6 or not receipt["mono_spawn"] <= entry <= report["mono_output"] < life:
826:         raise Invalid("shared module-entry child life not proved")
827:     coop = report.get("cooperative")
828:     reserve = min(0.25, SC[name][0] / 8.0)
829:     expected_coop = {"mono_life_deadline": life, "mono_work_end": life - 2 * reserve,
830:                      "mono_join_deadline": life - reserve, "mono_inner_deadline": life - 3 * reserve,
831:                      "poll_interval_s": 0.01, "deadline_extended": False}
832:     if not same(coop, expected_coop) or any(not isinstance(report.get(k), list) for k in
833:             ("descendants", "joins", "identity_waits", "ownership_edges")) or not isinstance(report.get("transports"), dict):
834:         raise Invalid("cooperative bounds or full child evidence lists missing")
835:     proto = report.get("stdin_protocol")
836:     line = b""
837:     if name in ("mutate_pin", "mutate_evidence"):
838:         manifest = receipt.get("go_creation_manifest")
839:         line = (json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()
840:         if len(line) > 4096 or hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest() != receipt.get("go_creation_manifest_sha256"):
841:             raise Invalid("actual bounded creation line digest missing")
842:     if not isinstance(proto, dict) or proto.get("eof") is not True or proto.get("error") is not None or \
843:             proto.get("bytes") != len(b"GO\n" + line) or proto.get("sha256") != hashlib.sha256(b"GO\n" + line).hexdigest() or \
844:             not all(finite(proto.get(k)) for k in ("mono_start", "mono_eof", "mono_end", "deadline_mono")) or \
845:             not entry <= proto["mono_start"] <= proto["mono_eof"] <= proto["mono_end"] < proto["deadline_mono"] < life or \
846:             proto["mono_eof"] < receipt["go_written_mono"] or \
847:             proto.get("creation_line_bytes") != (len(line) if line else None) or \
848:             proto.get("creation_manifest_sha256") != (hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest() if line else None):
849:         raise Invalid("exact token/optional declared line/EOF/absolute child startup evidence")
850:     allowed = {main["pid"]}
851:     allowed.update(x["pid"] for x in report["descendants"] if isinstance(x, dict) and integer(x.get("pid"), 1))
852:     allowed.update(x for x in (report.get("intermediate"), report.get("grandchild")) if integer(x, 1))
853:     observations = []
854:     p = child_observation(report.get("main_identity_observation"), life, allowed)
855:     if p is None or identity(p) != identity(main) or not same(p, report.get("main_identity")) or \
856:             report.get("main_identity_observed_mono") != report["main_identity_observation"]["observed_mono"]:
857:         raise Invalid("main reported full incarnation does not join actual launch")
858:     observations.append(report["main_identity_observation"])
859:     if has_output:
860:         child_observation(report.get("main_identity_at_output"), life, allowed)
861:         observations.append(report["main_identity_at_output"])
862:     for o in report["descendants"]:
863:         child_observation(o, life, allowed)
864:         observations.append(o)
865:     for edge in report["ownership_edges"]:
866:         if not isinstance(edge, dict):
867:             raise Invalid("child current-parent edge missing")
868:         ps = [child_observation(edge.get(k), life, allowed) for k in ("parent_before", "child", "parent_after")]
869:         observations += [edge[k] for k in ("parent_before", "child", "parent_after")]
870:         if edge.get("error") is None:
871:             a, b, c = ps
872:             if any(p is None for p in ps) or identity(a) != identity(c) or b["ppid"] != a["pid"] or \
873:                     edge.get("current_parent_verified") is not True or edge.get("same_group_verified") is not (b["pgid"] == a["pgid"]):
874:                 raise Invalid("child asserted ownership edge without actual full parent/child brackets")
875:         elif not isinstance(edge.get("error"), str):
876:             raise Invalid("edge uncertainty missing")
877:     for j in report["joins"]:
878:         if not isinstance(j, dict) or not integer(j.get("pid"), 1) or j["pid"] not in allowed or \
879:                 not integer(j.get("observer"), 1) or j["observer"] not in allowed or j.get("wait_options") != ["WNOHANG"] or \
880:                 j.get("mono_life_deadline") != life or not all(finite(j.get(k)) for k in
881:                     ("mono_start", "mono_end", "deadline_mono", "mono_reaped")) or \
882:                 not entry <= j["mono_start"] <= j["mono_reaped"] <= j["mono_end"] < j["deadline_mono"] < life or \
883:                 j.get("resolved") is not True or j.get("error") is not None or j.get("unresolved_observation") is not None or \
884:                 not integer(j.get("polls"), 1) or not integer(j.get("interruptions"), 0) or \
885:                 not integer(j.get("wait_status"), 0) or j.get("exit_code") != os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(j["wait_status"]) or j["exit_code"] != 0:
886:             raise Invalid("private child join unresolved/late/nonzero/not-WNOHANG")
887:     for w in report["identity_waits"]:
888:         if not isinstance(w, dict) or not integer(w.get("pid"), 1) or w["pid"] not in allowed or \
889:                 w.get("matched") is not True or not integer(w.get("attempts"), 1) or \
890:                 not isinstance(w.get("errors"), list) or len(w["errors"]) >= 8 or not all(finite(w.get(k)) for k in
891:                     ("mono_start", "mono_end", "deadline_mono")) or not entry <= w["mono_start"] <= w["mono_end"] < w["deadline_mono"] < life:
892:             raise Invalid("identity polling escaped shared absolute lifetime")
893:         for k in ("first_observation", "last_observation"):
894:             child_observation(w.get(k), life, allowed)
895:             observations.append(w[k])
896:         for o in w["errors"]:
897:             child_observation(o, life, allowed)
898:             observations.append(o)
899:     if name in ("ownership_double", "zombie_hold"):
900:         mid = report.get("intermediate")
901:         t = report["transports"].get("intermediate_report")
902:         if not integer(mid, 1) or report.get("intermediate_status") != 0 or not any(
903:                 j["pid"] == mid and j["observer"] == main["pid"] for j in report["joins"]) or \
904:                 not isinstance(t, dict) or t.get("eof") is not True or t.get("error") is not None or \
905:                 not integer(t.get("bytes"), 1) or t["bytes"] > 32768:
906:             raise Invalid("complete private intermediate transport and actual join required")
907:     unique = {}
908:     for o in observations:
909:         if o.get("identity") is not None:
910:             p = o["identity"]
911:             unique[identity(p)] = p
912:     return observations, list(unique.values())

915: def intended_case_evidence(name, childout, stdout, receipt, cfg, prep, preparation, files, main, late, require):
916:     report = childout
917:     if name in ("busy2", "rss"):
918:         lines = stdout.splitlines()
919:         if len(lines) != 1 or not lines[0].startswith(b"IDS "):
920:             raise Invalid("one complete actual IDS report required")
921:         report = decode(lines[0][4:])
922:     if report is None:
923:         if name in ("ok", "rc_nonzero", "mutate_pin", "mutate_evidence", "stray_file", "zombie_hold") or name.startswith("ownership_"):
924:             raise Invalid("mandatory intended-child report missing")
925:         return []
926:     observations, reported = child_envelope(report, name, main, receipt, cfg, childout is not None)
927:     imap = {e["first_identity"]["uniqueid"]: e for e in receipt["identity_history"]}
928:     registry = {e["identity"]["uniqueid"]: e for e in receipt["owned_identities"]}
929:     roots = {h["identity"]["uniqueid"] for h in receipt["helpers"]} | {main["uniqueid"]}
930:     extra = {uid: e for uid, e in registry.items() if uid not in roots}
931:     reported_core = {identity(p)[:5] for p in reported}
932:     reported_uids = {p["uniqueid"] for p in reported}
933:     if any(identity(e["identity"])[:5] not in reported_core for e in extra.values()) or any(
934:             identity(p)[:5] not in reported_core for p in receipt["contract_violations"]):
935:         raise Invalid("unreported owned incarnation or deleted reported registry entry")
936:     for c in receipt["census_observations"]:
937:         for p in c["owned_live"]:
938:             if p["uniqueid"] in reported_uids and p["uniqueid"] not in registry:
939:                 raise Invalid("observed reported owned identity disappeared from the registry")
940:     def in_ledger(p, full=False):
941:         e = imap.get(p["uniqueid"])
942:         return e is not None and any((identity(v) == identity(p) if full else identity(v)[:5] == identity(p)[:5]) for v in e["variants"])
943:     def selected(role, phase=None):
944:         matches = {identity(o["identity"]): o["identity"] for o in observations if o.get("role") == role and
945:                    (phase is None or o.get("phase") == phase) and o.get("identity") is not None}
946:         return list(matches.values())
947:     if name.startswith("ownership_"):
948:         role, phase = ("exec_descendant", "post_exec_post_reparent") if name == "ownership_double" else ("descendant", "post_setup")
949:         targets = selected(role, phase)
950:         if len(targets) != 1 or targets[0]["pid"] != report.get("grandchild"):
951:             raise Invalid("full intended descendant incarnation missing")
952:         target = targets[0]
953:         final = receipt["final_census"]
954:         blockers = final["owned_live"] + final["unproved_live"]
955:         if len(blockers) != 1 or identity(blockers[0])[:5] != identity(target)[:5] or \
956:                 blockers[0]["pgid"] != target["pgid"] or blockers[0]["traced"] != target["traced"] or \
957:                 blockers[0]["status"] == 5 or not in_ledger(blockers[0], True):
958:             raise Invalid("intended full incarnation is not the sole retained live blocker")
959:         uid, classification = blockers[0]["uniqueid"], report.get("required_ownership_class")
960:         if name in ("ownership_group", "ownership_slow"):
961:             owned = registry.get(uid)
962:             if owned is None or owned["root"] != main["uniqueid"] or not in_ledger(target, True) or \
963:                     final["unproved_live"] != [] or len(final["owned_live"]) != 1:
964:                 raise Invalid("group/slow requires known ownership, never an unproved alternative")
965:             if name == "ownership_group" and (classification != "known_group" or owned["via"] != "owned_group" or
966:                     owned["identity"]["pgid"] != main["pgid"]):
967:                 raise Invalid("known same-group root proof missing")
968:             if name == "ownership_slow" and (classification != "known_current_parent" or
969:                     owned["via"] != "current_parent:%d" % main["uniqueid"] or owned["identity"]["ppid"] != main["pid"]):
970:                 raise Invalid("slow control lacks observed current-parent ownership")
971:         elif classification != "known_or_unproved" or not in_ledger(target) or \
972:                 (uid not in registry and uid not in {e["first_identity"]["uniqueid"] for e in receipt["unproved_history"]}):
973:             raise Invalid("fast/double permitted branch not retained as known or unproved")
974:         require("intended_full_descendant_and_exact_ownership_root", True)
975:     if name == "zombie_hold":
976:         targets = selected("zombie", "zombie_hold_start")
977:         if len(targets) != 1:
978:             raise Invalid("actual held-zombie incarnation missing")
979:         z = targets[0]
980:         if z["status"] != 5 or z["pid"] != report.get("grandchild") or z["uniqueid"] not in extra or \
981:                 extra[z["uniqueid"]]["root"] != main["uniqueid"] or not in_ledger(z, True) or not any(
982:                     z["pid"] in c["owned_zombies"] and any(identity(p) == identity(z) and p["status"] == 5 for p in c["owned_live"])
983:                     for c in receipt["census_observations"]) or not any(j["pid"] == z["pid"] for j in report["joins"]):
984:             raise Invalid("reported full zombie/parent-root/wait evidence not observed and retained")
985:         require("intended_full_zombie_retained_and_reaped", True)
986:     if name == "busy2":
987:         targets = selected("busy_spinner", "pre_spin")
988:         if len(targets) != 1 or not in_ledger(targets[0], True):
989:             raise Invalid("intended pre-spin full descendant not retained")
990:         kid = targets[0]
991:         if kid["uniqueid"] not in extra or extra[kid["uniqueid"]]["root"] != main["uniqueid"] or \
992:                 extra[kid["uniqueid"]]["via"] != "owned_group" or kid["pgid"] != main["pgid"] or \
993:                 report.get("busy_pids") != [main["pid"], kid["pid"]] or \
994:                 report.get("busy_gate") != "not_released_before_identity_and_IDS" or cfg["cpu_slots"] != 2:
995:             raise Invalid("two-slot intended busy ownership/gate proof missing")
996:         trip = receipt["trips"][0]
997:         d = trip.get("detail")
998:         if not isinstance(d, dict) or d.get("count") != 2 or set(d.get("pids", [])) != {main["pid"], kid["pid"]}:
999:             raise Invalid("BUSY cause not the exact two intended PIDs")
1000:         pair = {identity(main)[:5], identity(kid)[:5]}
1001:         witnessed = False
1002:         for c in receipt["census_observations"]:
1003:             busy = [m for m in c["measurements"] if m["busy"]]
1004:             if {identity(m["identity"])[:5] for m in busy} == pair and len(busy) == 2 and \
1005:                     max(m["mono_observed"] for m in busy) <= trip["mono"] <= c["mono_end"]:
1006:                 witnessed = True
1007:         require("two_full_intended_busy_measurements_cause_first_excess", witnessed)
1008:     if name == "rss":
1009:         if report.get("rss_pid") != main["pid"] or report.get("allocated") != 320 * 1024 * 1024 or \
1010:                 report.get("touched_bytes") != report["allocated"] or report.get("touch_stride") != 4096 or \
1011:                 report.get("touch_writes") != 81920 or report.get("touch_write_bytes") != 81920 or \
1012:                 not integer(report.get("page_size"), 1) or report.get("term_masked_for_evidence") is not True:
1013:             raise Invalid("actual bounded intended RSS allocation/touch evidence missing")
1014:         for key in ("rss_before", "rss_after"):
1015:             r = report.get(key)
1016:             if not isinstance(r, dict) or r.get("pid") != main["pid"] or r.get("error") is not None or \
1017:                     not integer(r.get("rss_bytes"), 0) or not finite(r.get("mono_start")) or not finite(r.get("mono_end")):
1018:                 raise Invalid("PID-specific child RSS bracket missing")
1019:             a = child_observation(r.get("identity_before"), report["mono_life_deadline"], {main["pid"]})
1020:             b = child_observation(r.get("identity_after"), report["mono_life_deadline"], {main["pid"]})
1021:             if a is None or b is None or identity(a) != identity(b) or identity(a)[:5] != identity(main)[:5]:
1022:                 raise Invalid("RSS task read not bracketed by the intended incarnation")
1023:         if report.get("rss_before_bytes") != report["rss_before"]["rss_bytes"] or \
1024:                 report.get("rss_after_bytes") != report["rss_after"]["rss_bytes"] or \
1025:                 report.get("rss_delta_bytes") != report["rss_after_bytes"] - report["rss_before_bytes"]:
1026:             raise Invalid("RSS reported counters contradict actual bracketed task records")
1027:         trip, witnessed = receipt["trips"][0], False
1028:         for c in receipt["census_observations"]:
1029:             partial = 0
1030:             for index, m in enumerate(c["measurements"]):
1031:                 partial += m["rss"]
1032:                 following = c["measurements"][index + 1]["mono_observed"] if index + 1 < len(c["measurements"]) else c["mono_end"]
1033:                 if identity(m["identity"])[:5] == identity(main)[:5] and m["rss"] > cfg["rss_trip_bytes"] and \
1034:                         partial == trip.get("detail") and m["mono_observed"] <= trip["mono"] <= following:
1035:                     witnessed = True
1036:         require("intended_PID_alone_exceeds_RSS_and_is_in_exact_first_trip_prefix", witnessed)
1037:     if name in ("mutate_pin", "mutate_evidence"):
1038:         creation = report.get("creation")
1039:         manifest = receipt["go_creation_manifest"]
1040:         path = cfg["creation_channel"]["target_path"]
1041:         actual = files["launch.json"] if name == "mutate_evidence" else absolute_snapshot(path, 1 << 20)
1042:         raw_original = actual["raw"][:-1]
1043:         original = {"path": path, "inode": actual["inode"], "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw_original).hexdigest(),
1044:                     "bytes": len(raw_original), "nlink": 1}
1045:         after = {"path": path, **hash_entry(actual)}
1046:         expected_manifest = {"kind": "qualification-creation-manifest-v1", "scenario": name,
1047:                              "preparation_sha256": preparation["sha256"], "run": prep["run"],
1048:                              "output_root": prep["output_root"], "target": original}
1049:         manifest_line = (json.dumps(expected_manifest, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()
1050:         if not same(manifest, expected_manifest) or len(manifest_line) > 4096:
1051:             raise Invalid("creation manifest semantic schema/original publication binding")
1052:         if not isinstance(creation, dict) or not same(creation.get("manifest"), manifest) or \
1053:                 creation.get("manifest_sha256") != receipt["go_creation_manifest_sha256"] or \
1054:                 creation.get("manifest_line_bytes") != len(manifest_line) or \
1055:                 creation.get("preparation_sha256") != preparation["sha256"] or \
1056:                 not same(creation.get("run"), prep["run"]) or not same(creation.get("output_root"), prep["output_root"]) or \
1057:                 not same(creation.get("original"), original) or not same(creation.get("after"), after) or \
1058:                 not same(manifest.get("target"), original) or not same(manifest.get("run"), prep["run"]) or \
1059:                 not same(manifest.get("output_root"), prep["output_root"]) or \
1060:                 manifest.get("preparation_sha256") != preparation["sha256"] or \
1061:                 not same(creation.get("preparation_facts"), {"path": QROOT + "/" + name + "/preparation.json", **hash_entry(preparation)}) or \
1062:                 creation.get("name_inode_before") != original["inode"] or creation.get("name_inode_after") != original["inode"] or \
1063:                 creation.get("append_bytes") != 1 or actual["raw"] != raw_original + b"x" or \
1064:                 not all(finite(creation.get(k)) for k in ("mono_verified", "mono_append", "mono_fsync_completed", "mono_after_verified")) or \
1065:                 not creation["mono_verified"] <= creation["mono_append"] <= creation["mono_fsync_completed"] <= creation["mono_after_verified"] < report["mono_life_deadline"]:
1066:             raise Invalid("original approved creation identity not continuously verified BEFORE mutation")
1067:         if name == "mutate_pin" and (not same(original, prep["fixture"]) or raw_original != FIXTURE):
1068:             raise Invalid("mutated inode not original CREATED approved fresh fixture")
1069:         if name == "mutate_evidence" and original["sha256"] != receipt["launch_record_sha256"]:
1070:             raise Invalid("launch mutation not bound to original publication digest/inode")
1071:         require("approved_original_creation_inode_and_before_append_checks", True)
1072:     return reported
1073: 
1074: 
1075: def verify(name, approval_digest, actual_exit, runfd, outfd):
1076:     exp, C = expectations(name), {}
1077:     result = {"checker": VERSION, "scenario": name, "criteria": C,
1078:               "parent_release": "not granted; separate ended-owned and late-artifact disposition mandatory"}
1079:     def require(key, ok):
1080:         C[key] = bool(ok)
1081:     ap = snapshot(runfd, "parent-authorization.json", 1 << 20)
1082:     if ap["sha256"] != approval_digest:
1083:         raise Invalid("approval digest differs from the parent-supplied trusted digest")
1084:     approval = decode(ap["raw"])
1085:     if not isinstance(approval, dict) or approval.get("kind") != "v15-qualification-invocation-v1" or approval.get("scenario") != name:
1086:         raise Invalid("approval kind/scenario")
1087:     cp, gp, sp, pp = (snapshot(runfd, f, 1 << 20) for f in ("config.json", "go.json", "stability.json", "preparation.json"))
1088:     require("approval_artifact_bindings", all(approval.get(k) == v["sha256"] for k, v in (
1089:         ("config_sha256", cp), ("go_sha256", gp), ("stability_receipt_sha256", sp), ("preparation_sha256", pp))))
1090:     package = approval.get("sources")
1091:     if not isinstance(package, dict) or set(package) != {"supervisor", "child", "writer", "checker"}:
1092:         raise Invalid("approval must pin the entire four-source package")
1093:     expected_sources = {"supervisor": (SUPERVISOR, SUPERVISOR_SHA), "child": (CHILD, None),
1094:                         "writer": (WRITER, None), "checker": (CHECKER, None)}
1095:     source_ok = True
1096:     for key, (path, h) in expected_sources.items():
1097:         src = absolute_snapshot(path, 1 << 20)
1098:         pinned = package[key]
1099:         source_ok = source_ok and isinstance(pinned, dict) and set(pinned) == {"path", "sha256"} and \
1100:             pinned["path"] == path and pinned["sha256"] == src["sha256"] and (h is None or h == src["sha256"])
1101:     require("approved_sources_match_actual_bytes", source_ok)
1102:     py = approval.get("python")
1103:     if not isinstance(py, dict) or not isinstance(py.get("path"), str) or not py["path"].endswith("/Resources/Python.app/Contents/MacOS/Python") or os.path.realpath(py["path"]) != py["path"]:
1104:         raise Invalid("approval interpreter must be the actual Python.app executable, not its launcher")
1105:     require("actual_interpreter_digest", absolute_snapshot(py["path"])["sha256"] == py.get("sha256"))

1113:                               adm["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"], package, pp["sha256"], channel)

1116:                    "outstanding_reservations_bytes": adm["outstanding_reservations_bytes"],
1117:                    "lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes": adm["lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes"]}

1119:             all(integer(adm[k], 0) for k in ("outstanding_reservations_bytes", "lane_outstanding_reservations_bytes")))
1120:     expected_prep = {"kind": "qualification-creation-preparation-v1", "scenario": name,
1121:                      "run": {"path": QROOT + "/" + name, "inode": [os.fstat(runfd).st_dev, os.fstat(runfd).st_ino]},
1122:                      "output_root": {"path": QROOT + "/" + name + "/out", "inode": [os.fstat(outfd).st_dev, os.fstat(outfd).st_ino]},
1123:                      "fixture": None, "source_pins": package, "python": py, "allocation": reservation,
1124:                      "negative_control": name not in ("ok", "rc_nonzero")}

1305:     if name not in ("ignore", "identity", "ownership_group", "ownership_slow", "ownership_fast"):

1307:     require("supervisor_expected_digests_exact", set(ev) == expected_ev and all(
1308:         ev[p] == (hashlib.sha256(files[amap[p]]["raw"][:-1]).hexdigest() if name == "mutate_evidence" and p == cfg["launch_record"] else files[amap[p]]["sha256"])
1309:         for p in expected_ev))
1310:     require("stderr_empty", files["stderr.txt"]["raw"] == b"")
1311:     samplefile = files["samples.jsonl"]
1312:     require("sample_counts_and_bytes", bool(samples) and integer(receipt.get("samples_written"), 1) and
1313:             len(samples) == receipt["samples_written"] and samplefile["bytes"] == receipt.get("samples_bytes"))
1314:     previous, observed = None, []
1315:     sample_ok = True
1316:     for s in samples:
1317:         c, r = s.get("census"), s.get("resources")
1318:         sample_ok = (sample_ok and isinstance(c, dict) and c.get("complete") is True and integer(c.get("lane_busy"), 0) and
1319:                      0 <= c["lane_busy"] <= 2 and integer(c.get("lane_rss"), 0) and isinstance(c.get("lane_busy_pids"), list) and
1320:                      c["lane_busy"] == len(c["lane_busy_pids"]) and finite(s.get("mono")) and (previous is None or s["mono"] > previous) and
1321:                      isinstance(r, dict) and integer(r.get("charged_bytes"), 0) and r["charged_bytes"] <= cfg["output_trip_bytes"] and
1322:                      integer(r.get("free"), 0) and r["free"] >= cfg["free_trip_bytes"] and set(s.get("failure_codes", [])) <= exp["failures"] and
1323:                      s.get("trips") in ([], exp["trips"]))
1324:         if not sample_ok:
1325:             break
1326:         previous = s["mono"]
1327:         observed.append(c)
1328:     require("complete_uncontaminated_samples", sample_ok)
1329:     final_c = receipt.get("final_census")
1330:     if not isinstance(final_c, dict) or final_c.get("complete") is not True:
1331:         raise Invalid("complete final census required")
1332:     observed.append(final_c)
1333:     clear = receipt.get("clear")
1334:     if exp["loop"] == "EXITED":
1335:         require("timely_all_predicate_clearance", isinstance(clear, dict) and finite(clear.get("mono_all_predicates_verified")) and
1336:                 isinstance(clear.get("census"), dict) and clear["census"].get("complete") is True and clear["census"].get("owned_live") == [] and
1337:                 clear["census"].get("unproved_live") == [] and clear["census"].get("group_live") == [] and
1338:                 clear["mono_all_predicates_verified"] >= clear["census"].get("mono_complete", math.inf))
1339:         if isinstance(clear, dict) and isinstance(clear.get("census"), dict):
1340:             observed.append(clear["census"])
1341:         if isinstance(stop, dict):
1342:             require("clear_before_stop_grace", clear["mono_all_predicates_verified"] <= stop["mono"] + cfg["settle_s"])
1343:         require("final_owned_clear", final_c.get("owned_live") == [] and final_c.get("unproved_live") == [] and final_c.get("group_live") == [])
1344:     else:
1345:         blockers = final_c.get("owned_live", []) + final_c.get("unproved_live", [])
1346:         if name.startswith("ownership_"):
1347:             require("ownership_blockers_retained", bool(blockers) and clear is None)
1348:         else:
1349:             require("owned_child_still_blocking", isinstance(final_c.get("owned_live"), list) and len(final_c["owned_live"]) == 1 and
1350:                     final_c["owned_live"][0].get("uniqueid") == ident["uniqueid"] and final_c.get("unproved_live") == [] and clear is None)
1351:         result["owned_or_unproved_not_ended"] = blockers
1352:     stdout = files["stdout.txt"]["raw"]
1353:     childout = decode(files["child-out.json"]["raw"]) if files["child-out.json"] is not None else None
1354:     if childout is not None:
1355:         require("child_output_identity", childout.get("scenario") == name and childout.get("pid") == ident["pid"] and
1356:                 childout.get("pgid") == ident["pgid"] and
1357:                 ("child-out.json" in late or childout.get("ppid") == receipt.get("supervisor_pid")))
1358:     result["child_reported_identities"] = intended_case_evidence(name, childout, stdout, receipt, cfg, prep, pp, files, ident, late, require)
1359:     if name == "ok":
1360:         require("stdout_exact", stdout == b"line 1\nline 2\nline 3\n")
1361:     if name in ("rc_nonzero", "mutate_pin", "mutate_evidence", "stray_file", "ignore", "identity", "deadline"):
1362:         require("stdout_empty", stdout == b"")
1363:     if name == "missing_output":
1364:         require("missing_output_intended", files["child-out.json"] is None and stdout == b"no output written\n")
1365:     if name == "mutate_pin":
1366:         fixture = snapshot(runfd, "pinned-scratch.txt", 1 << 20)
1367:         require("fixture_one_byte_append", fixture["raw"] == FIXTURE + b"x" and fixture["nlink"] == 1 and
1368:                 childout.get("target_inode") == fixture["inode"] and childout.get("size_before") == len(FIXTURE))
1369:     if name == "mutate_evidence":
1370:         require("launch_mutation_same_inode", childout.get("target_inode") == files["launch.json"]["inode"] and
1371:                 childout.get("size_before") + 1 == files["launch.json"]["bytes"])
1372:     if name == "stray_file":
1373:         require("stray_actual_bytes", files["stray.txt"]["raw"] == b"undeclared file\n" and files["stray.txt"]["nlink"] == 1)
1374:     if exp["trips"]:
1375:         t = trips[0]
1376:         require("trip_stop_signal_order", finite(stop.get("mono")) and t["mono"] <= stop["mono"] and
1377:                 (exp["signal"] != "sent" or stop["mono"] <= sig["mono_sent"]))
1378:     if name in ("deadline", "ignore", "identity"):
1379:         require("deadline_after_wall_limit", trips[0]["mono"] >= receipt["mono_child_deadline"] == receipt["mono_spawn"] + cfg["max_wall_s"])
1380:     if name in ("deadline", "flood", "busy2", "rss"):
1381:         require("signal_to_persistence_le_7s", 0 <= summary["post_publish_mono"] - sig["mono_sent"] <= 7 and
1382:                 0 <= summary["post_publish_mono"] - trips[0]["mono"] <= 7)
1383:     if name == "flood":
1384:         st = receipt.get("streams", {}).get("stdout") or {}
1385:         require("flood_trigger_proved", st.get("overflow") is True and st.get("bytes_written") == len(stdout) == cfg["stdout_cap_bytes"] and
1386:                 integer(st.get("bytes_seen"), 0) and st["bytes_seen"] > st["bytes_written"] and
1387:                 any(s.get("streams", {}).get("stdout", {}).get("overflow") is True for s in samples))
1388:     streams = receipt.get("streams")
1389:     require("stream_schema", isinstance(streams, dict) and set(streams) == {"stdout", "stderr"} and all(
1390:         isinstance(v.get("closed"), bool) and v.get("error") is None and integer(v.get("bytes_written"), 0) and
1391:         v["bytes_written"] == files[k + ".txt"]["bytes"] for k, v in streams.items()))
1392:     if exp["loop"] == "EXITED":
1393:         require("streams_closed", all(v["closed"] is True for v in streams.values()))
1394:         require("clearance_after_stream_and_reap", finite(receipt.get("child_exit_mono")) and
1395:                 receipt["child_exit_mono"] <= clear["mono_all_predicates_verified"] and all(
1396:                     finite(v.get("mono_close_completed")) and v["mono_close_completed"] <= clear["mono_all_predicates_verified"]
1397:                     for v in streams.values()))
1398:     if name != "go_unlisted":
1399:         commits = [receipt.get("launch_commit"), receipt.get("go_commit"), summary.get("ack_commit")]
1400:         require("commit_order_and_expiry", all(isinstance(c, dict) and finite(c.get("mono")) and finite(c.get("epoch")) and c["epoch"] <= go["not_after_epoch"] for c in commits) and
1401:                 commits[0]["mono"] <= receipt["mono_spawn"] <= commits[1]["mono"] <= summary["post_publish_mono"] <= commits[2]["mono"])
1402:     result["verdict"] = "PASS" if all(C.values()) else "FAIL"
1403:     return result
1404: 
1405: 
1406: def main():
1407:     if os.path.abspath(__file__) != CHECKER or os.path.realpath(__file__) != CHECKER:
1408:         raise Invalid("checker must execute at the exact approved fixed source path")
1409:     if len(sys.argv) != 4 or sys.argv[1] not in SC:
1410:         raise Invalid("usage: SCENARIO PARENT_APPROVAL_SHA256 ACTUAL_EXIT")
1411:     name, approval_digest, exit_text = sys.argv[1:4]
1412:     if len(approval_digest) != 64 or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in approval_digest):
1413:         raise Invalid("parent approval digest format")
1414:     actual_exit = int(exit_text)
1415:     runfd = directory(QROOT + "/" + name)
1416:     outfd = os.open("out", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=runfd)
1417:     try:
1418:         try:
1419:             result = verify(name, approval_digest, actual_exit, runfd, outfd)
1420:         except (Invalid, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError, IndexError, UnicodeDecodeError, OverflowError, RecursionError) as e:
1421:             result = {"checker": VERSION, "scenario": name, "verdict": "FAIL", "criteria": {}, "error": repr(e)}
1422:         data = (json.dumps(result, sort_keys=True, indent=1, allow_nan=False) + "\n").encode()
1423:         fd = os.open("check.json", os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o644, dir_fd=runfd)
1424:         try:
1425:             view = memoryview(data)
1426:             while view:
1427:                 n = os.write(fd, view)
1428:                 if n <= 0:
1429:                     raise Invalid("zero-progress checker write")
1430:                 view = view[n:]
1431:             os.fsync(fd)
1432:         finally:
1433:             os.close(fd)
1434:         os.fsync(runfd)
1435:         print(json.dumps({"scenario": name, "verdict": result["verdict"], "failed": [k for k, v in result["criteria"].items() if not v]}))
1436:         return 0 if result["verdict"] == "PASS" else 1
1437:     finally:
1438:         os.close(outfd)
1439:         os.close(runfd)
1440: 
1441: 
1442: if __name__ == "__main__":
1443:     try:
1444:         sys.exit(main())
1445:     except Exception as e:
1446:         sys.stderr.write("CHECKER_ERROR %r\n" % e)
1447:         sys.exit(2)
